Summary

With the new L Corp space station needing to be tested Lena decides to leave her company in Sam’s capable hands so she can spend a year doing it. And more importantly enjoy a year not worrying about people trying to kill her. Two birds, one stone really.

There’s just one small problem with her plan; it means she’s spending the whole year alone, on a space station, with her every move recorded and shared live to the world by a poorly programmed droid.

Watch Lena struggle to maintain her cold Luthor reputation while she yells profanities at a goat, lavishes affection on a cat, goes through what she insists on calling a “grunge” phase, keeps accidentally letting slip how sex starved she is, and desperately tries not to let on how madly in love with her best friend and only visitor she is.

TW:
Threats of violence towards a goat. Nothing bad happens to the goat despite Lena’s many murderous urges. She just REALLY hates it.

Notes

Lena's Space Log

by MyCatIsMyEditor

https://archiveofourown.org/works/48604384

    July 16- DAY 1

    Chapter Notes

    Lena positioned everything checking it was set correctly. 

    “Ready R2?” she asked. 

    The drone made no response but Lena still smiled at it before she hit the power button. R2 lit up and the drone rose slowly to her head height. Lena glanced at her tablet to make sure it was working before smiling at the camera on the drone and began her first log.

    “Hello everyone. So, as I'm sure you're all aware L Corp has been building it’s own space station using a combination of alien tech and human ingenuity. Now, one of the things about space stations and being able to let people use them, is that someone needs to be the first to test them. Well someone and some animals because we need to be sure.”

    She stepped back a little. R2 followed.

    “In order to fully test our new station we needed to send someone into space and… It turns out there’s only six people familiar enough with everything involved in this station to actually be able to fix any bugs that come up, potentially without any outside assistance, and only one of them was willing to risk their life to test it so…”

    She stepped out the door to the viewing platform showing the earth below.

    “Here I am!”

    She smiled at R2. 

    “Yes, I’m in space. Actually in space. As in I got secretly flown in a rocket to this station. But I wasn’t alone. Would you like to meet the other passengers?”

    She paused then glanced at her tablet.

    “As I can’t actually hear you and it would be kind of pointless if you don’t… I’m just going to assume yes.”

    She walked through the next set of doors into the first biome and smiled. The first section was specifically designed to mimic an open field and she knew it would have an impact when people saw it. The station itself was set up with several different biomes and levels that provided for the ones next to them and it was going remarkably well. Probably because she’d programmed the robots who tended the plants that set it up. She glanced down to see the comment feed had finally loaded and was filled with people losing their minds over the plants. She walked through into the next space which was the animal enclosures. 

    “So here’s the chickens. They were named, not by me, Honey, Lemon, and Satay. Very original of you Pete.”

    She motioned to the chickens but R2 kept the camera focused on her. She sighed and took a step closer then turned R2 to show the chickens awkwardly walking around their new enclosure.

    “I’m gonna have to do some bug fixes on you R2. By the way the camera is really a drone, or maybe a droid depending how we define it, called R2, and he follows me everywhere as a camera. I can of course turn him on and off because there are some things I do not want on camera. There are boundaries of what I’m willing to share, even for science.”

    She turned and walked through to the next enclosure. 

    “So these are Nigerian Dwarf goats. They are Bridget, Jude, and Shazzer. Again I did not name them. And the lone masculine influence here, Darcy. I’m assuming their names are a reference but I can’t be sure. I’m sure someone will tell me if they are.”

    She moved R2 to show the goats and pointed to Darcy who was the only all brown one. He apparently had not enjoyed the flight or disembarking and was screaming repeatedly at her. Lena ignored this. She was sure he’d settle down.

    “Right, next up is the labs.”

    She went through a set of doors to the next section which was the main living area, including dining and sleeping. She kept going pointing out each room as she passed and then unlocked the door to the main laboratory where she pointed to the large tank with a dozen rats all moving about in it.

    “So these are the rats. Technically they were all individually named but well…” She sighed. “Look they’re all plain white lab rats so they’re collectively all going to be called Linguine because I cannot tell them apart Pete, there’s ten of them.”

    She turned and walked back out and then went through to the next biosphere which was the main water filter and treatment zone with a large two to six foot deep pond full of fish, water grasses and covered in the algae that provided a lot of her fresh oxygen. Sitting on the grass by the water was a ginger cat. Lena smiled and sat next to the cat.

    “So there’s fish in this pond, which is one of three water filtration setups on this station. This is the biggest and a major part of the connected ecosystem here that is making this whole place habitable for me and is also the provider of most of my fresh water. There’s also a special mushroom here but I’m not supposed to open that for a bit because spores, so you’ll have to wait to see it.”

    Lena turned to the cat and reached out gently touching a paw.

    “But this is my newest best friend, and first mate here I guess, everyone meet Ripley. Say hello to Earth Ripley.”

    Lena turned R2 to show the cat. Ripley looked up briefly then went back to watching the fish. Lena smiled then sighed.

    “So in case you haven’t worked it out I’ll be here for a whole year, alone. I’ll be doing a daily log because apparently that’s a thing I need to do for public interest so I guess…”

    She sighed and looked around. 

    “Look if anything is going to make me go crazy it’ll be this. So everyone you got your wish, you have a front row seat to what will probably be a Luthor slowly going crazy. See y’all same time tomorrow.”

    Lena gave the camera a final smile.

    “End Livestream.”

    She turned back to Ripley and gently tapped her paw again. Ripley turned to her and then flopped onto the grass and stretched, presenting her tummy.

    “No way, I know that’s a trap,” Lena said with a smile as she put in her earpiece. 

    Ripley gave her a look then shifted so her head was over the edge of the pond to continue watching the fish below. Lena smiled and turned on her tablet again switching over to call Sam.

    “Okay Sam, was I sufficiently good at acting normal to get public approval, or am I going to be playing whack a mole with the various home made missiles people shoot at me?”

    She lay down next to Ripley.

    “I don’t think there will be missiles…” Sam said. “Maybe a few nasty comments.”

    “Well I figure as long as I manage to put pants on for the daily log, all the animals survive, and I don’t drown in a depression induced suicidal spiral over this somehow being the best solution to the number of people trying to kill me…” Lena drew a deep breath. She tried to add a little levity to her voice. “Then we should be good.”

    Sam sighed.

    “It sounds very dark when you say it like that.”

    “Well it’s not like I can realistically rely on Supergirl to keep saving me.”

    “I still can’t believe Kara and Supergirl get coffee in the middle of the night.”

    “I’m not complaining. You have no idea how nice it is to be carried by-”

    “Lena!”

    “What?”

    “The livestream is still going.”

    “Oh shit,” she muttered. She looked at R2. “Sorry everyone apparently we’ve found our first major bug. Livestream off.” 

    She waited.

    “Nope still going,” Sam said with a sigh.

    “Livestream end. End livestream. Camera off.”

    “Still going,” Sam said with a sigh.

    Lena sighed.

    “R2 Down.”

    R2 switched off and fell to the grass. Ripley turned to look at the drone and then got up to go paw at it.

    “You’re offline,” Sam said.

    “Right so I have to turn it off off to end the livestream. That’s helpful. Well at least I have something else to do today then.”

    Sam sighed as Lena got up grabbing the drone and heading back to the labs, followed by the now intrigued Ripley.

    She put R2 on the desk and started running a diagnostic. 

    “Okay I’m going to try turning R2 back on and we can see if it’s just a turn it off and on again thing,” Sam said.

    Lena pushed the button and R2 started up again. 

    “You’re back online.”

    Lena sighed and rubbed her head.

    “R2 down.”

    The drone shut off. 

    It took three hours but Lena finally found the problem and it was stupid.

    “Okay, so some idiot programmed R2 to only stream to the live feed on the website,” Lena said.

    Sam sighed.

    “How did they-”

    “Because he was the guy we fired halfway through. Dan was supposed to go through all the code but he was only looking for something potentially dangerous so... It looks like R2 can only broadcast my life signs to you while also streaming video.”

    Ripley decided to jump up on the workstation and meowed sadly at Lena. Lena absently patted her head.

    “Okay what do you want to do?” Sam asked.

    “There’s not much we can do from here. I’d need to take R2 apart and then completely reprogram him and that would take months and honestly I’m not sure if it would work completely. I’d have to create the programming again effectively from scratch.”

    “So you’ll have to be live constantly?” Sam asked.

    “In a way. He’s one of the key security measures here and my alert system. We put half the alarms through him because he’s supposed to always follow me. I can do some superficial tweaks but we made him pretty much tamper proof for a reason. I could probably make him patrol but whenever I’m doing something potentially dangerous I need him. He’s the up to the second record for all life sign data. So I’m kinda gonna have to put up with it. At least until you can send me the screwdrivers so I can crack him open and change his base programming.”

    Sam sighed.

    “Well I guess you’re going to be providing a lot of extra footage for the team to humanise you.”

    Lena scoffed.

    “There’s going to be endless hours of me sitting in a chair typing and reading things.”

    Sam chuckled.

    “Just to separate out I’ll change the daily log to this computer, to make it clearer what is R2 footage and what’s the official log,” Lena said.

    Sam hummed her agreement. 

    “Is anyone even watching the feed?” Lena asked with a sigh.

    Sam tapped a few keys as Lena picked up her cup of tea and took a sip. 

    “About three thousand people,” Sam said.

    Lena scoffed.

    “Well that’s not gonna last long.”

     

    It took a little digging through some of the supply crates but Lena created a temporary stay option for R2 so she could go to the bathroom without a camera following her. That being a rope tied to R2 and then secured to the lab desk so he couldn’t follow. 

    “And they said I’d only ever have one use for shibari,” Lena muttered looking at the drone now bound by a chest harness as it flew back and forth tugging against the rope in an attempt to follow her.

    She returned from the bathroom to find R2 on the floor with Ripley chewing on the camera.

    “Shit!”

    She quickly removed Ripley and R2 floated back up to the correct height. A diagnostic check and a few tests told her the microphone was now broken.

    Lena looked at Ripley who was napping on the desk beside her.

    “Well, I suppose I should thank you for that. I didn’t really want everyone being able to hear me walking around talking to myself.”

    Ripley didn’t respond but Lena didn’t mind. She sat back in her chair and then looked at her to do list. Then sighing she got up. She had a lot of supplies to unpack before she could sleep. She sighed again as she stepped out of the labs and heard Darcy was still screaming.

    That night, or rather what would be nine thirty if she was still in National City, she secured R2 to the kitchen table and gave the drone a fond pat. To prevent further issues she put Ripley in her bedroom before going to have a shower, grateful to be washing off the sweat from a day of moving around crates and equipment. She checked the stream numbers and saw that approximately four thousand people had watched her unpack boxes and pat a cat for six hours. She shook her head in disbelief and crawled into bed. 

    Ripley jumped onto the bed and curled up purring beside her. Lena smiled and scratched Ripley behind the ears before rolling over and hugging her pillow to get the first night of proper sleep she’d had in a long time.

    Chapter End Notes

    July 18- DAY 3

    Chapter Notes

    “Sam I found your care package and… seriously?”

    Lena gave the camera a look.

    She’d found a small box among the medical equipment marked Care package for Lena and been curious. Then she’d opened it and quickly realised what it was. She barely managed to hide it before R2 captured footage. One quick bit of drone bondage later she collected the box and took it to her room to go through the contents in private.

    She found a large quantity of lube, a vibrator (the updated model of her previous one because of course Sam remembered her drunken rambling about how good it was), a bottle of twenty year old scotch and five kilos of good quality Swiss chocolate. Sam had left a note too.

    In case you get lonely.

    “Sam the chocolate and whiskey yes but the other…” Lena gave the camera a long look hoping to convey to Sam the rest of that sentence. “Seriously? How did you even get that onto the manifesto? Please tell me that isn’t listed-”

    Her computer chimed with a message.

    Sam: It’s listed as “anxiety treatments and a muscle relaxant”

    Lena rolled her eyes.

    “Wow. I appreciate the thought but it’s completely unnecessary. You could have sent more scotch instead of the other thing, it works better at that…” Lena awkwardly waved her hand. “Aim.”

    Chapter End Notes

    Wasn't that nice of Sam, a little something for Lena in case she gets lonely.

    July 21- DAY 6

    Chapter Notes

    “First space omelet from eggs grown in space,” Lena said holding up her plate for the camera. “Honey, Lemon, and Satay are already earning their keep.”

    She added a little sauce in a neat patten then cut off a mouthful. Lena looked at the camera her fork poised in front of her mouth.

    “Now to find out if space eggs taste like regular eggs…” She put the fork in her mouth and chewed thoughtfully. “Yes. That’s not really a surprise though.”

    She kept eating. Her computer chimed with an alert with a message from Sam.

    Sam: You’re supposed to be updating everyone about what you’ve been doing not eating.

    Lena sighed.

    “Sam everyone already knows what I’ve been doing, they’ve been able to watch me every moment I’m not in the bathroom or my bedroom. I fed the animals, shovelled goat shit and tied up R2 while I showered. After that I made this. Yesterday I spent all morning fixing minor issues with the agricultural droids and then the afternoon working on the new code for R2. The PR team put together the closest thing to highlights they could for an extra video and it’s all Ripley.”

    Lena kept eating. She was on a very regimented diet because of supplies so any extra, such as the fresh eggs, was a bonus. Diets weren’t a new thing for her but she did miss the treats Kara used to bring her when she had a bad day. The closest thing she had to donuts was the chocolate Sam had sent, which she was saving for when she was having a really bad day. She’d had to turn to dried apricots as a treat and it just wasn’t the same. Maybe because they didn't come with Kara.

    She sighed heavily. She missed Kara.  Especially her hugs, and her smile, and the way she always knew just the right thing to say to make her feel better. What she would give right now to be wrapped in Kara's arms and held so tight against that beautiful muscular-

    Her computer chimed with a message. Lena pulled herself back from her thoughts of Kara and looked at her messages.

    Sam: either there’s a glitch with the feed or you’ve been starring off into space doing nothing for a whole minute. 

    Lena cleared her throat. 

    “Anyway I’ll see you all tomorrow. Maybe Darcy will have stopped screaming by then.”

    Chapter End Notes

    So Lena misses Kara, I wonder if Kara misses Lena just as much.

    July 30- DAY 15

    Chapter Notes

    “Sam…” Lena gave the camera a meaningful look. “I take back what I said about the care package, thank you.”

    Sam: You’re welcome.

    Lena smiled then looked back at the livestream.

    “So apparently I’ve repaired, maintained, measured, put away, and done pretty much everything there is to do on this station. So apart from the animal care and my own cleanup I’ve only really got R2’s programming, which I’m already halfway through and don’t need for another three months. So I guess…” 

    Lena looked over at Ripley who was asleep on desk where she had been watching the rats.

    “I may as well follow Ripley’s lead and just take a nap this afternoon then read for a few hours.”

    She shrugged at the camera.

    “Space is only interesting for the first week then you’re basically trapped alone on a small but very fancy hobby farm.”

    Chapter End Notes

    August 7- DAY 23

    Chapter Notes

    “Sam, Darcy has to die.”

    Lena held up a knife.

    “Don’t kill the goat!” Sam yelled.

    “Oh come on!” Lena pleaded. “I hate him. He won’t stop screaming. He’s worse than Veronica was, and you know how much I hated her.”

    “Veronica?” Sam asked, confused.

    “My ex, Ronnie.” 

    Sam was silent for a moment her head tilted clearly trying to remember. She shook her head.

    “Yeah, sorry Lena I’m gonna need more information to work out which of your I didn’t go through a rebellious phase exes you’re referring to.”

    Lena sighed.

    “I went to school with her… We met up last year and I dated her for a week. We broke up because she was a needy alien hating bigot. Is any of this ringing a bell?”

    Sam made a vague noise.

    “Sorry Lena, there’s still a few possibilities for that so I’m gonna need something more.”

    “Uh… I found out later that she ran an alien fighting ring? Changed her name to Roulette? She got arrested by Supergirl…”

    “Sweetie I’m gonna need something that you would have told me about her to figure out which one she is,” Sam said. 

    “Uh…” Lena cast her mind about for something that would twig Sam’s memory. “The needy pillow princess.”

    “Oh! Right, her. Yeah you did hate her a lot.”

    “Knew we’d get there,” Lena said with a sigh. “Anyway, I would trade Darcy for her in a heartbeat.”

    Sam started laughing.

    “This isn’t funny Sam. I wanna kill the goat and then make like… curry or stew or something from him. I want fresh meat damnit! And I want it with a side of vengeance against that fucking-”

    “You can’t kill the goat. We need him for science,” Sam said, her voice full of patient understanding and barely concealed amusement.

    Lena growled and contemplated the knife in her hand.

    “What about if I just cut his vocal cords. He don’t need his vocal cords,” she said, her voice dropping to something almost predatory.

    “With you having to be the one to do the surgery that would probably be animal cruelty, so again no,” Sam said smiling at her.

    Lena sighed in exasperation. 

    “Well me having to listen to him all day is also cruelty. What about my feelings Sam? Don’t you care about them?”

    Sam laughed again.

    “Of course I care. You know I do.”

    Lena grumbled sitting back and twirling the knife in her fingers.

    “Can I at least eat one of the fish then?”

    “Not for another two weeks, and only if their population increases as predicted. And stop playing with the knife it makes you look villainous.”

    Lena huffed and angrily dropped the knife on the desk.

    “I miss fresh meat,” she whined.

    Lena felt a few tears gathering. Sam made a sympathetic noise. 

    “I also miss donuts,” Lena mumbled sinking in her chair and crossing her arms feeling a pout forming.

    Ripley jumped up with a small meow and Lena gave her a smile and started gently stroking her head. 

    “Who’s my favourite kitty?” Lena asked as Ripley begun to purr. “You are. Yes, yes you are my precious baby. I love you so much. Yes I do. You’re the bestest kitty in the whole wide galaxy.”

    The purring increased as Lena started using both hands to scratch Ripley under the chin.

    “Do you wanna watch something with mama? Do you wanna curl up with me for your afternoon nap and watch something with pretty women falling in love and kissing? Yes-”

    “Lena.”

    “Mmm?”

    Lena looked up and sighed as she remembered the livestream was still going. Something she really should have remembered five minutes earlier but she was sure that would be forgotten by the end of the year.

    “Leave a comment with a recommendation for what I should watch. It needs a happy ending and lesbians. Both of whom live. And no porn recommendations. I don’t need any more of that. Bye.”

    She ended the livestream.

    “Well I suppose horny cat lady is better than murderous Luthor in terms of public perception,” Sam said.

    Lena sighed.

    “I plead temporary insanity.”

    “It’s been three weeks.”

    “I have a genetic predisposition,” Lena said smirking at Sam as she picked up Ripley.

    Chapter End Notes

    August 17- DAY 33

    Chapter Notes

    I highly encourage y'all to go check out the incredible art Rustingcat has been doing for this story.
    You can find it here

    “So great news for all those of you with a penis. It will work normally on this space station, unlike other space stations. Yeah, I bet none of you knew that was a thing. No erections in space. Well until now. And I know this because Darcy and Bridget got uh… better acquainted last night.”

    Lena sighed hearing Darcy scream loudly from the chicken enclosure.

    “A problem I was not supposed to have on this trip as the does were all supposed to be desexed but apparently there was a mix up of some kind on the day so Bridget is going to go into heat every 18-23 days for the next couple of months. Which of course means I now need to add check goat for signs of heat to my daily tasks list. A task I am not looking forwards to. Especially as it means I need to separate Darcy every time she shows signs so I don’t end up needing to deliver some kids.”

    Lena took a deep calming breath remembering how hard it had been to capture the remarkably slippery goat that morning. She was sure the footage of her falling over repeatedly in the half hour it took her to capture him was already being shared all over the internet. It hadn’t helped that she’d needed to hold him so she could tie him up and separate him with the chickens.

    “So now I need to find some way to give him his own enclosure because apparently the chickens hate him too. So yay me, I get to do heavy lifting today while a goat screams at me.”

    Lena sighed again rubbing her head.

    “Today is gonna suck.”

     

    It did indeed suck. First Lena had to find a storage space on the station big enough to fulfill the SPCA requirements and then she had to empty it storing all the crates around the rest of the station in as many out of the way places as she could. Then she had to spend an extra hour finding all the crates again so she could locate the one with the part she needed to add the climate controls to the storage space so Darcy wouldn’t get too cold. Even if a part of her really wanted to let him suffer for a few days with the low temperature.

    By the time she’d finally gotten that done she then had to recapture him as he’d apparently chewed through the rope holding him and chased the chickens into their coop. 

    Lena didn’t bother tying him up once she got hold of him and instead just carried him through the station swearing in every language she knew as he tried to bite her. He finally succeeded in turning his head far enough to bite her shoulder hard enough to cause her to scream and drop him.

    Thankfully she dropped him in his new enclosure. 

    She barely resisted the urge to kick him as she left locking the door behind her. She tore back her shirt and saw blood. Swearing furiously Lena returned to the lab pulling out the first aid kit and turning on the livestream as she ripped open her shirt to reveal the bite.

    “Just in case anyone was wondering if I still want to kill that fucking goat, the answer is yes!”

    She poured antiseptic on a cloth and hissed as she applied it to the bite.

    “I hate that goat. I hate that goat. I hate that goat,” she muttered cleaning the bite.

    She grabbed a sterile dressing tearing it open and slapping it on the bite. She hissed at the pressure and then forced herself to calm down. She looked back at the livestream.

    “Now, I’m sure someone will tell me I am a horrible person for my treatment of him but frankly I don’t care! He has the legally required amount of space, access to food and clean drinking water, and he can go back once his return will not result in me needing to bring a baby goat into the world four months from now. Which according to my goat expert is going to be two to three days to be sure Bridget is no longer in heat. So fuck off he’s fine. I on the other hand am not!”

    She took several deep breaths and then heard the chime of a message appearing. 

    Sam: Perhaps today is a good day to open the scotch.

    Lena nodded.

    “Yes Sam, today is a very good day to open the scotch.”

    Lena sighed and then saw the comments. Most of which were about her tits.

    “Perverts the lot of you,” she muttered ending the livestream.

    She took another breath then got up heading for the shower. She was going to have a nice hot shower then cuddle up with Ripley to watch something with a lot of gore and drink a couple of fingers of scotch. And maybe text Kara to see if she had any more recipe ideas that involved goat.

     

    Lena stepped out of the bathroom and found herself suddenly facing something at head height. She reacted on instinct her fist coming up to punch R2 sending the drone crashing to the floor.

    “Fuck R2, don’t do that!” she yelled.

    Lena took a few deep breaths as R2 rose back to his usual height. 

    “Pervy goddamn robot.”

    She went over to her tablet to send him on patrol again, muttering angrily as she went. Ripley came racing over with a concerned little meow.

    “It’s okay Ripley. I just got surprised by R2,” she said gentling her tone.

    Ripley weaved around her legs looking up at her and tapping her knee with a paw, still seeming concerned. Lena sighed and bent down to give her a pat. 

    “Don’t worry I’ll send him back on patrol and we can watch something with an excessive amount of violence before sleepy time.”

    September 9- DAY 56

    Chapter Notes

    To say Lena was surprised to look out her bedroom window to see Supergirl waving madly at her in a convertible would be an understatement. Once she recovered from her shock she raced for the airlock controls. She unlocked the external doors and watched the cameras in amazement as Kara stepped out of the flying car carrying with her a large metal crate.

    Lena closed the external lock and opened the secondary one to let Kara into the station proper. The moment the doors closed behind her again Lena was running through the station towards Kara and wrapped the grinning super in the tightest hug she could.

    “Kara!” Lena said holding her close. “It’s so good to see you!” 

    “You know?” Kara asked shocked. “How could you know?”

    “You’re not exactly subtle Ms I flew here on a bus.”

    Kara laughed and Lena felt a very warm and comforting arm hold her closer for a moment.

    “I’ve missed you too.”

    “Hi R2,” she said. “And I guess everyone else.”

    Kara frowned and Lena had a feeling she was using her powers.

    “Lena… that is an intense locking mechanism. How many…” Kara squinted and Lena couldn’t help her laugh. “Can a human even open R2?” she finished.

    Lena nodded.

    “I may have spent a month practicing how, but yes I can.”

    Lena grinned at Kara.

    “Want me to prove it?”

    “Maybe later,” Kara said. “But first,” She held up the large metal box. “I have gifts.”

    Lena sent R2 on patrol and took Kara’s hand leading her through the station to the labs where they unpacked all the box. When she saw the pink box of donuts Lena melted. Kara grinned clearly pleased with herself. Lena couldn’t help but hug her again. Ripley, in Kara’s arms, protested but Lena for once ignored her. 

    Half an hour later they were in the kitchen sharing a cup of tea and chatting like they used to when Kara stopped talking and sighed.

    “You have to go?” Lena guessed.

    Kara nodded sadly.

    “Snapper is wondering where I am.”

    Lena laughed. 

    “Did you come to see me on your lunch break?”

    Kara nodded giving her a guilty smile. Lena felt something warm inside her grow as she looked at Kara. Even in the suit with her hair down she still did the same little fidget with her hands when she got nervous. The cup of tea in her hands moving one way then another as Kara hesitated. Lena looked up meeting Kara’s eyes. Kara gave her a shy smile before her eyes dropped back to her tea.

     “Can I… Can I come visit you again another time?”

    Lena felt her face break into an instant smile.

    “Yes! Yes of course you can.”

    She reached over the counter to still Kara’s nervous fingers.

    “You know you’re always welcome to see me, whenever you want,” she said.

    Kara seemed to be holding her breath as she looked at Lena. Lena felt her heart rate increase a little then remembering Kara could probably hear it she forced it to slow. Kara let out her breath and Lena shivered at the sudden cold, which made them both giggle. 

    Then suddenly Kara made a face.

    “Uh oh… he’s checking the time.”

    Lena rose.

    “Come on, let’s get you back before you get in trouble.”

    Kara sighed and picked up the metal box. She walked with Lena to the airlock and gave Ripley a kiss an hugged Lena one last time. Kara handed Ripley over before she stepped into the airlock. 

    Lena unlocked the external doors and watched as Kara stepped into the car and gave a final wave before it flew off in a blur. Lena sighed softly and looked down at Ripley in her arms. The station felt quieter all of a sudden, lonelier.

    Ripley meowed softly and Lena smiled as space instantly felt a little less lonely. She gave Ripley a kiss and set her down. Giving the airlock one last wistful look at she closed the external doors letting the shields reconnect. 

    Making her way through the station she returned to the kitchen and saw the two cups sitting on the bench. A tiny proof Kara had been there. She slowly washed their cups letting herself relax with the familiar routine. That done she opened the fridge and took out the goat meat Kara had brought her. A scream from Darcy made her sigh. She turned and looked at Ripley.

    “Shall we have goat stew for dinner?” she asked.

    Ripley gave a soft little meow in response. 

     

    Lena entered the lab and stopped. There sitting on the bench was the pink box of donuts. Smiling she sat in her chair and looked around the lab at the other small signs of Kara’s visit. The metal box open to reveal the screwdrivers for R2, the padded case with the spare temperature control panel, and -most importantly to Lena- the set of specially modified DEO earplugs. Lena fitted them into her ears and felt the world get a little quieter. She sighed happily as for the first time since arriving, she couldn't hear Darcy.

    Checking the time she hit the button to do her daily log.

    “So I uh… had a visitor today. Supergirl came to see me,” She found herself smiling as the words left her mouth. “She brought me the parts I needed, including the screwdrivers for R2 modifications and more importantly-” she reached into the pink box and removed one. “Donuts. I’ve missed them so much.”

    Lena looked around her and frowned.

    “Ripley!” she called.

    There was no answering meow. Lena shook her head and turned around to keep working. Then she stopped. She hadn't seen Ripley for at least a half hour.

    “Ripley baby, where are you?” she called a little louder.

    There was no answer so she got up and walked out of the labs, a sinking feeling growing in her stomach. 

    “Ripley?”

    Forcing down the urge to panic she went to her room. No Ripley. The fish. No Ripley. She pulled out her tablet and opened the life signs data on R2. She found Ripley and felt her body seize up in sudden fear. Device on, no life signs.

    Silently praying it was just a technical fault she pulled up the tracking and followed it to one of the storage areas where she found Ripley’s collar on the ground in the middle of the corridor. Lena was starting to worry. After five minutes of searching the storage area she was starting to panic. It was time to call for help. She called Kara.

    “Hey Lena, what’s up?” 

    “I can’t find Ripley.”

    Her voice came out in an anxious rush and she immediately bit her lip at the obvious distress in her voice.

    “Can you-”

    She felt the prickle of tears as thoughts of her brother somehow sending someone up to mess with her. Dozens of horrible possibilities filtered through her mind.

    “She’s alive,” Kara said. “Supergirl can hear her on the ship.”

    Lena let out a breath as relief flooded her.

    “Where is she?”

    Kara was silent for a minute. Lena felt her anxiety return.

    “What did you line the station with?” Kara muttered. “It’s making it harder to see her.”

    Lena groaned and put her head in her hands.

    “A lot of things,” she muttered. 

    “Okay she’s in the orchard. Supergirl said she’ll come to help you.”

    Lena thanked her and raced for the orchard. How Ripley had gotten there she had no idea. She wasn’t even supposed to be able to access the storage space let alone two biomes beyond. 

    She sprinted through the vegetable garden with it’s layers of hydroponics, past the second water tank, and burst into the orchard. She heard Ripley then. Her pitiful meows making it easy for Lena to locate her, ten feet up in one of the pear trees.

    “Ripley!”

    Ripley saw her and let out another pitiful meow that both mended and broke Lena’s heart. Ripley was clearly safe even if Lena had no idea how she’d gotten up that high.

    “Baby what are you doing up there?” she asked. 

    Ripley didn’t reply for a moment then there was another sad meow. 

    Lena sighed and looked around. She could probably climb up there and get her down. An alert chime from R2 told her Kara had arrived. She unlocked the doors for her as fast as safety would allow.

    Ten seconds later Kara was beside her looking up at Ripley. The immediate meow from Ripley at the sight of Kara made Lena smile. Kara floated up and gently removed her from the tree bringing her back down to Lena. 

    Lena felt immediate relief as she held Ripley safely in her arms once more. She covered the meowing Ripley with kisses and held her close. 

    “How did you even get in here?” Lena demanded.

    Ripley didn’t reply too busy purring up a storm as Kara stroked her head. Lena felt the prickle of tears again as she looked at Ripley.

    “I’m guessing she followed you on your rounds this morning,” Kara said. 

    Lena sighed and gave Ripley another kiss before they made their way back to the labs. Kara played with Ripley for a few minutes while Lena fixed her collar. Lena double and triple checked it was working properly before she let Ripley leave the labs again.

    She sighed watching on the tablet as Ripley made her way to her usual place at the fish pond. Satisfied Ripley was safe she turned to Kara. Kara held out her arms and Lena hugged her tightly feeling a fresh wave of tears. 

    “Thank you,” she choked out.

    Kara held her tighter. Lena forced herself to get control of her emotions again.

    “Sorry,” she said. “I just-”

    She quickly wiped her eyes and found herself looking at a handkerchief with the crest of El on it. A laugh escaped her as she took it and wiped her eyes.

    “It’s okay,” Kara said her hands coming up to hold Lena’s arms.

    Lena nodded.

    “I know-” she drew in a deep breath. “It was just the moment I couldn’t find her, my mind went to the possibility that Lex, or even Mum, had sent someone up to kill her to get back at me.”

    Kara gave her a worried look.

    “Your family would really do that?” 

    Lena sighed and thought about it.

    “Okay, Mum probably wouldn’t order a hit on a cat. Just because it would be too ridiculous even for her.”

    Kara laughed and Lena sank into another hug.

    “Thank you, again.”

    She felt Kara hold her tighter again.

    “Anytime,” Kara whispered into her hair.

    Chapter End Notes

    Supergirl, rescuing cats from trees even in space.

Do you think people captured the soundbite of Lena calling Ripley "baby" so they could put it in a thousand and one tiktoks? How annoyed do you think Lillian is that's she's going to have to make a public statement that she would never order a hit on a cat?

    October 8- DAY 85

    Chapter Notes

    Lena leapt up as she saw Kara arrive with the space backpack, as she insisted on calling it, in her hand.

    “I thought I’d help you move Darcy,” Kara said, once Lena had let her go from her now customary 30 second welcome hug.

    Lena grinned at her. 

    “Thank you,” she said, hugging Kara again. 

    Kara hugged her back as Ripley meowed loudly and rubbed herself against Kara’s boots. 

    “I also brought you some fan mail,” Kara said digging in the crate.

    “Fan mail?” Lena asked. “Please tell me you haven’t been reading the comments on my livestream again.”

    “No I haven’t,” Kara said with a sigh. “And yes actual fan mail. You do have fans. Which Sam said you would know if you read your comments.”

    Lena sighed. She avoided her comments for some very good reasons.

    “Let’s move Darcy then we can look at this alleged fan mail.”

     

    It took Kara less than a second to catch Darcy with superspeed. A feat that actually silenced him for a moment as he seemed stunned that someone had so easily caught him.

    “Wow,” Lena said. “That seemed a lot easier for-”

    Darcy turned his head and immediately bit Kara. She smiled as he stared at her confused his mouth around her bicep. He tried again.

    “I’m Supergirl,” she said.

    Darcy screamed in response and Lena saw Kara’s wince.

    “To the asshole enclosure,” Lena said turning and leading Kara down the hallway and through the next two sections. One was the fruit trees . They were too young to produce fruit yet but next year the crew would have plenty of fresh produce so long as the plants continued to grow as planned, and Ripley stayed out of them. 

    Then they were in the large item storage where a half dozen boxes were stacked with the other non climate sensitive items. It was significantly colder in here for those items. They reached what was Darcy’s isolation pen. Kara paused at the door looking at the handmade sign taped to the door. The words asshole enclosure were done in neat calligraphy. 

    “I didn’t know you knew calligraphy,” Kara said nodding at the perfectly inked letters.

    Lena shrugged.

    “Private school, we had fancy art classes.”

    Kara snorted and Lena opened the door so Kara could drop Darcy over the makeshift crate fence. He screamed at her and Kara sighed stepping back.

    The door closed and two minutes later Lena sighed in relief. She couldn’t hear Darcy. She turned to Kara with a smile.

    “So the fan mail?” she asked.

    “Sam said you could make it your log for today.”

    Lena smiled and they headed for the lab.

    Lena got settled in front of the camera setting up to start the stream. Kara handed over the small stack of letters and their accompanying note from Sam.

    Try to keep it appropriate for children.

    Lena rolled her eyes as Kara hit the button to start the livestream.

    “I have a visitor again today, and she moved Darcy for me.”

    Lena reached up and hugged Kara who was standing behind her. Kara laughed and leaned down hugging her back.

    “Well I couldn’t let him bite you again, otherwise I’d have to watch you cry through giving yourself a vaccination again and I’d just feel awful knowing I could prevent it.”

    Lena laughed and gave Kara a smile adjusting the camera so it could capture them both as Kara grabbed the unused chair from another lab station. Lena picked up the stack of letters.

    “And now to answer some letters, because apparently I do have children who watch this so… I hope your parents watch first because this is not a family friendly log.”

    Sam: Yes it is!

    Lena scoffed.

    “No Sam, it’s really not. I swear a lot and there’s been so many sexual references at this point I’d have earned an R rating if this was a movie.”

    Lena opened the first letter.

    “Okay this one is from David in Starr City, they want to know… can you poop in space?”

    Lena sighed and gave Sam a glare through the camera.

    “Yes David, you can. As is shown by my need to shovel it from the goat enclosure every day.”

    Kara handed her the second one.

    “This is from… Ava, and she wants to know, what happens to the flowers I grow here, am I going to keep them or sell them like her mummy. Well Ava, the flowers here will either be fed to the goats or composted. There’s a few bees here on this station that pollinate them but other than that they’re purely aesthetic or serve some important role in maintaining the ecosystem here.”

    Lena accepted the third letter.

    “Okay so Wilson from North Dakota, what’s going to happen to the animals after you leave?”

    Lena looked at the camera. 

    “Well, the animals are all going to stay on the station until they die, then they’ll likely be devoured, respectfully, by whatever crew members are here. Except Darcy. Darcy will not survive the year because I’m going to kill and butcher him myself before I leave.”

    Sam sent several messages in quick succession as Lena stared at the camera. Kara sighed and opened another letter.

    “Sam also said to tell you, again, you can’t kill Darcy.”

    Lena grumbled unhappily and looked at Kara who was looking from the letter to Lena and back again. Lena raised a questioning eyebrow.

    “Okay so this is from Pamela, she’s 36, from Gotham, and wants to know what brand your care package is so she can get one for a friend.”

    Lena laughed.

    Chapter End Notes

    How long before you think there's also a bunch of letters asking about their relationship? Let me know in the comments below what you would ask Lena in your fanmail.

    As always I highly encourage you to check out it the amazing art that's been done in connection to this story down below and until next time, stay safe.

    October 24- DAY 101

    Chapter Notes

    Lena heard the alert as she left her room heading for the kitchen. She smiled and changed course heading directly for the airlock with Ripley chasing after her. Lena smiled and ran a little faster hearing Ripley’s annoyed meow follow her and the whir of R2 as it followed.

    She opened the external doors and Kara stepped through waiting as Lena closed the airlock then opened the interior door.

    Kara stepped out and Lena was immediately wrapped in a tight hug as Ripley meowed trying to get Kara’s attention.

    “Hi,” Lena said pulling back a little with a smile.

    Kara grinned back at her then scooped up Ripley who immediately began nuzzling her face against her. Lena felt her heart swell a little at the sight. 

    “R2 go on patrol,” she said. 

    The drone turned and begun it’s regular circuit of the space station. Lena hugged Kara again then quickly pulled away feeling a little embarrassed at her eagerness. Kara seemed to understand as she pulled her back in for another hug.

    “I miss you too,” she said. “Video calls aren’t enough.”

    Lena hid her smile in Kara’s shoulder as her heart sped up. Kara held her a little tighter and she had a moment as she realised Kara could probably hear it.

    “So what brings Earth’s strongest hero to see me?” Lena asked, quickly pulling back.

    “I am here to help you celebrate 100 days. So I have cake.”

    “It’s day 101 but thank you.”

    “I tried but there was a small emergency and by the time I’d dealt with it you were asleep so…”

    Kara gave her an apologetic look. Lena smiled and touched Kara’s arm to let her know she’d just been teasing. Kara returned the gesture and they made their way to the lab Kara insisting the supplies she had would all be fine while they did her daily log. In the lab Lena opened up the log and adjusted the camera slightly before hitting the broadcast button.

    “Okay people, Supergirl is here again to help me celebrate 101 days in space. So what have you-”

    She turned to see Kara holding a cake with the words Happy Birthday Lena. Two white candles were carefully placed around the words and Kara was smiling at her. 

    Lena felt tears suddenly well in her eyes as she realised Kara had planned all this to celebrate her birthday with her. Then Kara started to sing. Lena felt her heart swell with a depth of emotion she truly hadn’t ever expected to feel hearing someone singing Happy Birthday. 

    Lena quickly wiped away her tears. She hadn’t expected she’d have anyone celebrate her birthday after she broke up with Jack. Last year she’d let it slide past with only a glass of whiskey and a new pair of shoes she bought herself. This year she had almost completely forgotten about it. She hadn’t expected anyone to care about it. At most she expected a call from Sam and a few messages from Kara if Sam made it public, but instead here she was, in space with her best friend smiling at her holding a cake and making sure everyone knew that today was her birthday. A few more tears escaped.

    Kara smiled at her and gave her hand a gentle squeeze as she moved the cake a little closer.

    “Make a wish,” she said. 

    Lena closed her eyes and wished with all her heart for Kara to be there for her next birthday. She opened her eyes and blew out the candles. Kara seeming her somewhat overcome emotional state smiled as she set the cake aside and wrapped her arms around Lena, holding her close. Lena buried her face in Kara's neck wiping her eyes on the cape before pulling back a little with a smile. 

    For a moment she was still overcome with her emotions as she looked at Kara. She forced herself to take a breath pulling herself back together.

    “Thank you,” she whispered.

    Kara’s smile grew impossibly wider.

    “You are an incredible woman Lena. You are a wonderful, kind hearted… beautiful soul, and you are someone to celebrate,” Kara said.

    Lena felt a fresh wave of tears threaten as her smile grew. She opened her mouth to thank Kara again but the words wouldn’t come. She cleared her throat.

    “How-”

    Kara’s smile grew anticipating the question.

    “How did I know your birthday when you absolutely refused to tell me? I have a friend in the FBI, they may have found out for me. Then Sam informed me that technically your birthday is publicly available information on some L Corp paperwork and I could have just looked it up.”

    Lena laughed. 

    “But we’re going to ignore that. The cake is my present, and I brought a few from others who caught me before I left.”

    Lena felt her heart melting all over again at the realisation others had remembered. Then she was handed the first carefully wrapped present.

    “From Jess,” Kara said.

    Lena smiled and unwrapped the box to see a bottle of Glenlivet single malt Scotch and a note.

    Before you say anything I bought it with your personal card -Jess.

    She laughed setting it aside and was handed her second gift.

    “Just a suggestion, maybe don’t open this one on camera, it’s from Sam.”

    Lena laughed and checked the card.

    Just in case the other one breaks. 

    Lena started laughing.

    “I have a feeling that’s another care package.”

    Kara nodded quickly and handed her a third one. She knew from the potsticker themed paper who this one was from. 

    “From Kara?” she asked.

    Kara nodded. Lena smiled as she opened it. Inside was a gameboy and a few cartridges.

    “She has a friend who apparently has experience altering Doom and so she had him make a special version for you.”

    Lena found her smile growing as she looked at the custom art on one cartridge. It had Darcy on it with copious amounts of blood.

    Goat Murder Simulator 

    “She thought it might help with your boredom and murderous rage if you could kill Darcy in a game.”

    Lena looked up at Kara and felt the urge to kiss her growing as her eyes met Kara’s and held.

    “Thank you,” she whispered. “Can you tell her thank you from me, and… this-” She sighed. “It’s really thoughtful and-”

    Her computer chimed several times and they both turned to look at the messages appearing from Sam.

    Sam: You’re still live.

    Sam: That means everyone can see you!

    Sam: And more specifically how you’re looking at her.

    Another message appeared.

    Sam: and you’re already trending.

    Sam: Also happy birthday!

    “Whoops,” Kara said.

    Lena quickly checked and sighed seeing the new hashtag. 

    #LuthorLovesASuper

    “Did people really not know we love each other I mean-”

    Lena looked at Kara she watched the sudden blush climbing her cheeks as her eyes flicked over the screen.

    “They don’t mean platonic love Supergirl,” Lena said smiling.

    Kara nodded looking away.

    “Yeah I uh… worked that out once I read the comments.”

    Lena laughed again and squeezed Kara’s hand.

    “And that’s why I don’t read them. Come on. Let’s turn the cameras off and eat some cake.”

    Kara gave her a bright smile at the mention of cake. 

    Chapter End Notes

    Kara couldn't let Lena be alone on her birthday, and now everyone knows it.

    How crazy do you think the Supercorp shippers went seeing them smiling at each other? Do you think goat murder simulator will keep Darcy safe? Let me know your thoughts and feelings down below, and until next time stay safe y'all.

    For those of you so inclined I am on Tumblr. I mostly post deleted scenes, head canon, and other ideas.

    October 31 DAY 108

    Chapter Notes

    “Happy Halloween everyone,” Lena said. “Since I am a certified genius I made my plans for Halloween in advance, so…”

    She picked up one of the pumpkins she’d harvested that morning.

    “I’m gonna carve a few pumpkins. Then I’m going to find a way to destroy them, because my psychologist said I could use an outlet for my murderous urges. And before anyone gets on my back for wasting the pumpkins, I’m going to feed the remains to the animals. So lots of fun for everyone today.”

    Her phone chimed and she smiled at seeing Kara’s name.

    Someone wants to help with the pumpkin destruction if that’s okay. Also the carving, if you save a pumpkin for them they will show you their mad carving skills.

    Lena laughed and looked at the camera.

    “I’ll set one aside,” she promised.

    Kara sent her a string of hearts that made Lena’s heart grow three sizes. As a result it took her a bit too long to remember she was live.

    “Sorry, apparently there is an idea for how I can destroy them in a creative way. But you’ll have to wait for that. Now, since I will be destroying these babies, I’m going to go with a theme, that being goats.”

     

    Lena found herself having a surprising amount of fun carefully extracting the insides of the pumpkins and drawing her various goats on them for carving. Ripley seemed very interested in helping and more than once Lena had to stop her from climbing into one of the pumpkins. Her inexperience and perfectionism with pumpkin carving had the added benefit of the carving taking far longer than she had anticipated. She was still working on them when she got the alert of Kara approaching. 

    Lena got up with a smile and quickly washed her hands before she remotely unlocked the airlock and then the internal doors letting Kara inside. She set R2 to wait and ran to greet Kara. Ripley close behind her.

    Kara had the space backpack in one hand and a smile on her face as Lena threw her arms around her. Kara hugged her back, lifting Lena off her feet which of course made Lena laugh. When Kara set her down again it was Ripley’s turn.

    “I saved you a pumpkin,” Lena said, as she watched Kara nuzzle her head against Ripley's.

    Kara hugged her again and then in a rush of air they were in the kitchen. Ripley looked a little confused at the change of location but seeing an unguarded pumpkin made a dive for it. Kara caught her effortlessly.

    “Okay Supergirl,” Lena said, gesturing at the final pumpkin and then R2. “Show us how good you are.”

    Kara’s face lit up as she took the pumpkin and after a final smile at Lena used her heat vision in quick bursts. Lena sighed as she watched. Of course her powers were involved. Kara grinned and spun her pumpkin around. Lena looked from it to the smug Kara. Kara had done a near photographic carving of Ripley.

    “You win,” she said.

    Kara laughed.

    “It’s not a competition, you don’t have heat vision.”

    “So this is your challenge to Superman?” Lena teased.

    Kara, to Lena’s surprise, immediately shook her head.

    “Nope, last time we had a carving competition it got really competitive.”

    “How competitive?”

    “Lois had to be brought in to stop us arguing.”

    “How did she-”

    “She said there would be no dessert if we didn’t stop and then we were banned from competing against each other in any artistic competition.”

    Lena laughed.

    “I didn’t realise Superman got so competitive.”

    “Superman no, my cousin, absolutely.”

    “What did you carve?”

    “He did a map of Metropolis, and I did the Argo skyline. The argument may have gotten a little off topic.”

    Kara looked away as some memory filled her eyes. Lena subtly touched her hand bringing her back to the moment. She had a feeling she knew what topic their fight had ended on.

    “Well you’ve done an amazing carving of Ripley.”

    Kara grinned at her and showed it to R2 so anyone watching could get a good look at her creation.

    “Thank you. Now, about destroying them. I thought you could use it to show off one of your many talents.”

    Lena raised an eyebrow, curious what Kara had in mind. She watched Kara dig around in the space backpack and then smiled as Kara straightened up holding a familiar sabre. 

    “I have your sword, and I specially sharpened it so it would be able to cut through the pumpkins, so be careful when you take it out.”

    Kara handed over the sword. Lena smiled wider as she slid the blade out of its protective case and saw the white shimmer on the edge. Kara had likely used something alien to sharpen it.

    “And how exactly am I going to be showing off my fencing skills?” she asked.

    Kara hefted one of the pumpkins and flashed her a cocky smile.

    “Moving targets.”

    Lena turned to properly face Kara.

    “So it’s Luthor vs Super?” she asked, her voice shifting to her best Luthor tone.

    Kara shook her head.

    “No, it’s Luthor vs, the Pumpkin Lord!”

    Lena tried not to laugh. She did not succeed.

    “You’re such a dork,” she said.

    Kara did her best impression of Lena's eyebrow raise.

    “As if you’re not.”

    Lena conceded with a nod.

    “Well then, let’s go destroy some pumpkins.”

     

    Lena insisted on leaving the one of Ripley behind so she could enjoy it for a few days. Ripley went over to her usual place by the water to watch the fish as Kara quickly assembled her little pile of pumpkins and Lena did a few warm ups. R2 was carefully positioned to capture it all. Lena smiled as she went through a few basic moves with her sabre. She had missed playing with her swords more than she thought. 

    “Ready?” Kara called.

    Lena turned with a smile and slipped into a relaxed starting stance. 

    “How are we gonna do this?” she asked. 

    “Just fight your way towards the pumpkin lord,” Kara said, motioning towards one of the smaller pumpkins she’d carefully balanced on top of R2. “I’ll give you the targets.”

    Lena grinned back. Kara counted down and then Lena was running towards her. The first pumpkin came flying towards her and Lena easily sliced through it, taking a moment to marvel at how sharp her sabre was, but then came the next one and Lena found herself smiling wider as she added a little flourish to her strike. She stabbed it mid air and threw it behind herself in one motion.

    Kara cheered and threw the next as Lena kept coming. She was slightly breathless by the time she reached Kara and R2. but before she could stab the pumpkin lord there was a rush of air and he was gone. Kara grinned at her from the other side of the room.

    “Hey!” Lena jokingly protested.

    “It’s a boss fight Lena. you know you gotta pass multiple levels, and they get harder each time.”

    Lena laughed.

    “And how is this one going to get harder?” she asked. 

    “The pieces get smaller, and I throw them faster.” 

    Lena straightened up and lifted her sabre. 

    “Bring it on,” she said.

    Kara narrowed her eyes at the challenge and Lena charged. 

     

    Lena collapsed to her knees the form of the pumpkin lord sliding from her sabre as Kara feigned horror. The final “level” of the fight had involved Lena trying to stab the Pumpkin Lord as Kara, moving at strictly human speed tried to move him out of the way. But in the end Lena had succeeded with a feint and the Pumpkin Lord was skewered on the tip of her sabre. 

    Lena turned to grin at Kara who had collapsed to the ground dramatically covering her face with one hand. Their eyes met and Lena tossed her hair back.

    “And so ends the reign of the Pumpkin Lord,” she said with false solemnity.

    Kara nodded with equal solemnity. Lena looked down at herself then Kara. They were both covered in pumpkin. Kara seemed to be thinking the same thing as she brushed bits of pumpkin off her house sigil.

    “Well that was fun,” Kara said, jumping up.

    Lena started laughing and Kara quickly joined her. Lena collapsed against her as they looked around at the pumpkin carnage spread around the now somewhat trampled artificial grassland.

    Lena sighed.

    “I’m going to be finding pumpkin for weeks,” she said. 

    Kara scoffed and Lena watched amazed as Kara carefully sped all over filling the animal feed buckets with the remains of the pumpkins. Kara came to a stop in front of her and Lena gave an approving nod.

    “Having superspeed must be pretty good.”

    Kara shrugged.

    “It’s definitely got benefits.”

    Lena picked up one of the buckets and they headed for the animal pens. The chickens were excited by the extra food and the goats did their best to stick their heads in the buckets as Kara and Lena made their way over to the food trough. Darcy screamed at them and in perfect unison they both yelled at him to shut up. 

    Back in the kitchen Kara carefully blunted the sabre again using her thumb. Lena did her best not to let on how cute she found Kara’s deep look of concentration as she did it. She wasn’t sure she succeeded but hopefully no one was looking at her with Kara standing there a sword in her hands carefully running her thumb over the edge.

    Then Kara was showing her the Halloween themed donuts and a collection of sweets she'd brought along for her, along with the half dozen Halloween themed toys for Ripley to play with. But once the box was empty they were stuck looking at each other, still covered in pumpkin and both hesitating. Ripley had taken up residence in one of Lena’s favourite places, Kara’s arms.

    “You have to go don’t you?” Lena finally said.

    Kara nodded.

    “Halloween night. The people of National City like to celebrate a bit too hard.”

    Lena nodded her understanding and took Ripley. Kara gave her one last hug and then pulled back making a face as she removed a piece of pumpkin from her face. 

    “See you next time,” Lena said, keeping a firm hold on Ripley as she squirmed trying to escape and follow Kara.

    Kara gave her a very soft look.

    “See you next time,” she said, her voice unusually quiet.

    Lena stayed in the kitchen until Kara was gone. Ripley started to loudly protest being held. 

    “If you didn’t keep following people where you’re not allowed I wouldn’t have to do this,” she said, putting Ripley down.

    Ripley gave her a grumpy look before trotting away. Lena sighed and then looked at her sabre. She picked it up with a smile and gave it a few practice stabs. She did miss her swords. She put it back in it’s sheath and hung it on a small hook in her room. She would play with it again tomorrow, but first she needed to get all the pumpkin out of her hair. 

    She set R2 to “Ripley cam” the newest setting she’d created. Considering Ripley’s popularity with people it was her new default for when she wanted R2 to leave her alone. It also had the added benefit of making sure if Ripley did get out of her collar again R2 would be able to tell her where she was. 

     

    Lena ended her day curled up in a blanket looking down at the earth in between watching the coverage of Supergirl in National City on her laptop. She smiled watching the clips of Kara posing with dozens of kids dressed as Supergirl and carefully rescuing many a drunk from precarious situations. Eventually Ripley came to protest that she wasn’t in bed as per usual and she was forced to go to her room. 

    She had just started Alien, Ripley now purring at her side on the bed when she got a message from Kara. 

    She opened it and was confused for a moment as she stared at the picture. A grinning Supergirl was posing with a half dozen women all dressed in sweatpants bearing the L corp logo and holding stuffed ginger cats. They were all glaring at someone dressed as a goat. It took her a moment but then she saw the drone balloon hovering behind them and she started laughing. 

    Happy Halloween.

    Chapter End Notes

    Lena's popularity is only growing, and now she has a sword to play with.

    November 6 DAY 114

    Chapter Notes

    Lena walked into the kitchen and stopped. She stared in amazement at the mess that Ripley had managed to make in the ten minutes it took her to feed the other animals.

    “Seriously?” Lena muttered.

    Her eyes moved slowly around the kitchen taking in the bag of flour that had split in half on the floor covering half the kitchen in a fine white mist. Mixed in with the flour was what looked to be half her sauces and quite a few of her spices that had all been knocked out of the cupboard with the flour. A large bottle of soy sauce was still leaking slowly into the flour. 

    In the middle of it all, covered in a mix of the cupboard’s contents was Ripley, meowing piteously as she emerged from the remains of the pumpkin. Apparently putting the Halloween pumpkin in the cupboard hadn’t been enough to prevent Ripley getting to it. 

    She looked at the mess in her cupboards and then back at Ripley.

    “How did you even-”

    Lena sighed and went over carefully removing Ripley from the mess she’d made.

    Ripley started squirming the moment she was picked up and Lena was forced to hold her close so she could wipe the flour, pumpkin, and- she sniffed- fish sauce off her face. Ripley blinked up at her and meowed again. Looking at the mess Lena sighed trying to decide her best course of action.

    Another meow from Ripley decided it, she would clean Ripley then the kitchen.

    “I really hope you handle what is about to come well,” Lena said, standing up the leaking soy sauce bottle before making her way towards the bathroom.

     

    Ripley did not handle it well. Lena initially tried to wash her in the bathroom sink but Ripley, very much against being in water, had escaped and Lena had to catch her again. Not wanting to distress Ripley more than necessary she had put her in the shower, which resulted in Ripley meowing with increasing volume until a still clothed Lena was in there with her, at which point she seemed to decide Lena was a climbing post and all attempts to remove the muck from her fur were some kind of game. 

    Lena hissed in pain as Ripley’s claws dug into her leg. Ripley, noticing she’d hurt Lena, stopped and rubbed her head against her, smearing the mess of flour and pumpkin still in her fur onto Lena’s shirt.  

    Lena sat on the floor of the shower, her clothes sticking uncomfortably to her and carefully combed everything out of Ripley’s fur as Ripley meowed in her lap. Each time she had to reposition Ripley to reach the next section for combing Ripley would try to escape leading to Lena having to catch her again. Thankfully Ripley wasn’t able to escape the shower, despite her best efforts.

    Nearly an hour after they entered the bathroom Lena began drying Ripley, a task she had thought would be easier than washing her, but she had been wrong. There was no hair dryer on the station and so she had to use three towels as Ripley’s fur was apparently capable of incredible water absorption. But eventually Ripley was at least mostly dry. She escaped Lena and dived into the laundry pile having moved on from meowing to angry yowls and attempts to scratch and bite. Lena decided that was probably good enough for now. She was honestly impressed Ripley hadn't tried scratching her earlier.

    Stripping down she turned on the shower once more and stepped under the spray. She had just begun lathering shampoo into her hair when she heard a meow above her.

    Blinking away water she looked up and saw Ripley staring down at her from the top of the shower door. Lena had one second of warning and then Ripley leapt into her arms and clawed her way to Lena’s shoulder. Lena was utterly bewildered for a moment, then Ripley began attacking the showerhead. Lena winced as Ripley’s claws dug into her shoulder. She decided to just give up before Ripley’s claws did any further damage and quickly rinsed the shampoo from her hair.

     

    The moment Lena stepped out of the bathroom an alert came through. She let Kara in before heading to her room to change. She emerged two minutes later to see her kitchen spotless, and a very fluffy, and completely dry, Ripley purring in Kara’s arms. 

    “Tea?” Kara asked.

    “Please,” Lena said sitting down. “And thank you.”

    Kara shrugged.

    “After having to wash and dry Ripley it seemed unfair that you’d have to clean the kitchen too.”

    Lena took a few minutes to enjoy the view as Kara reached up to remove the tea from the high shelf. 

    “Have I told you you’re my favourite?” she asked, her voice a coming out with more of a seductive purr than she had intended.

    Kara looked over her shoulder to smile at Lena.

    “Once or twice.”

    Chapter End Notes

But there’s just me here and even at bare minimum maintenance levels, and despite ditching every diet plan from the last twenty years of my life, there is only so much I can eat and I am running out of storage places for the excess. Which means I need to get rid of some food. Option one, I just throw the extra produce out the airlock, but that seems kind of wasteful. Option two I stop the production of most of the plants, which while it would solve the immediate storage problem could cause some other problems later when the proper crew turns up. So I’ve decided to go with option three, which is… Supergirl.”

    November 25 - DAY 133

    Chapter Notes

    Lena was getting ready to sleep when she got the alert. Confused about what could have Kara coming by so late she threw on a sweatshirt and went to let her in.

    “Hey you,” she said, greeting Kara with a small smile.

    Kara stepped into the station and wordlessly wrapped her arms around Lena in a very careful hug.

    “Hey,” Lena said softly. “Are you okay?”

    “Just one of those days,” Kara whispered.

    Lena held her tighter. She wasn’t sure how long they stood there holding each other but at one point they separated and headed for the observation room. Lena shivered and in a rush of air Kara returned with the blanket from Lena’s bed. Lena let Kara wrap it around her.

    For several long minutes they simply sat side by side looking through the window at the planet below.

    “What happened?” Lena finally asked. “Were you too late to save someone?”

    Kara sighed heavily.

    “No, I-” Kara sighed again and it was like the weight of the world had slowly settled on her shoulders. “I am having trouble remembering my love of humanity tonight, so I came here where it’s… quieter.” Kara’s lips twitched in a small smile. “Except for Darcy.”

    Lena felt her own smile as she wrapped an arm around Kara, letting herself enjoy the warmth that always seemed to grow between them whenever she did.

    “So just a general ennui?” she murmured.

    Kara sighed and gave her an almost guilty look.

    “Promise not to laugh?” she asked.

    Lena smiled and nodded.

    “I tried online dating again and everyone was so boring,” Kara said.

    Lena pressed her lips together to prevent her laugh.

    “I got eight dick pics in an hour. And don’t get me started on the couples looking to spice it up with a third.”

    Lena hid her face in Kara’s shoulder so she couldn’t see her smile.

    “And when I decided to switch to only women and the first three all had simp for Supergirl in their bio. Then I matched with one who seemed like she might be nice and she told me I looked like Supergirl and asked if I would rail her in a Supergirl costume.”

    Lena couldn’t help the small snicker that escaped her at that.

    “Did you tell her you have your own?” Lena teased.

    Kara huffed and gave her a half hearted glare. Lena patted her arm.

    “I can understand the frustration, my own dating life has been… difficult.”

    Kara wrapped her arm around Lena pulling her a little closer.

    “Why is love so hard to find?” she muttered.

    Lena let herself look at Kara for a long moment. Some part of her whispered it hadn't been as hard to find as she thought. She carefully took that thought and put it in a little box.

    “What made you try online dating again?” she asked, looking away.

    “Alex was giving me a hard time about being unwilling to get back out there after Mon-el. She said that while it is important for me to have close relationships with family and friends that I- Well she implied I was going to start blurring some lines if i wasn’t careful.”

    Lena hummed softly.

    “She saw the people shipping us together didn’t she?”

    Kara groaned and put her head in her hands. Lena chuckled and snuggled a little closer to Kara.

    “Well, at least we have each other,” she said, half teasing.

    Kara lifted her head and gave her a very soft smile. 

    “Always,” she said.

    Lena felt her heart race a little faster and promptly tried to stop it. Kara didn’t seem to notice as she was frowning at the wall.

    “Where’s R2?” she asked. 

    “Ripley watch. Ripley likes to go stare at the fish until I go to bed. She’ll probably come looking for me soon.”

    Kara hummed her agreement as Lena rested her head on Kara’s shoulder. Kara smiled and rested her head on Lena’s. Lena felt a small wave of tiredness seep into her. How many times had she thought of how nice it would be to drift off in Kara’s arms., and well… it wasn’t quite the same as her dreams but this was pleasant all the same.

    “I do love watching Ripley with the fish,” Kara said absently. “She gets so excited when they get close. Hey, do you ever worry Ripley will try to catch the fish?”

    “Nah,” Lena said, her eyes drifting closed. “She’d have to get wet to catch one and she hated her shower. So I think they’re safe.”

    Kara laughed and put her other arm around Lena pulling her almost into her lap.

    “Thank you,” Kara whispered.

    “For what?” Lena murmured sleepily.

    “For always treating me as Kara and not Supergirl.”

    Lena smiled.

    “Suit or no suit, you’re always my hero.”

    Kara’s soft chuckle seemed to roll over her as she sank further into Kara’s arms.

    “Come on, let’s get you to bed,” Kara whispered. 

    Lena wanted to protest but then Kara was lifting her very gently in her arms and any protest of this seemed like it might lead Kara to put her down and she really didn’t want that. 

    Lena yawned again and heard a faint meow as she was carried through the station. She barely managed to keep her eyes open long enough to let Kara out of the station.

    Chapter End Notes

She desperately wanted to rub her ass but considering she’d already screamed she didn’t really want to see what the internet would do with footage of her rubbing her ass. Considering what they’d done with the lingerie footage she really didn’t want to find out what samples they’d use to insinuate she’d been railed by Supergirl. Then again…

    Lena sighed again and got up. She knew she really shouldn’t encourage the internet if she could avoid it, not after her last slip up. She took off her other shoe just to make it even and looked around her. She’d been doing maintenance checks on the pipes. Which was totally necessary and not something she was doing to distract herself from thinking more about the completed designs for Kara’s vibrator. No, this was an important maintenance function and not at all a way to look busy enough that no one would question why she was going to bed a little earlier than normal tonight. If she was just spending time crawling into vents and pretending to be carefully examining the pipes for minutes at a time while actually reading articles about Supergirl that was her business and no one else needed to know. She did after all check the pipes each time so it wasn’t a complete lie.

    She closed the hatch and then her toolbox. Considering her new injuries she decided to just leave it for another time and headed back to the living areas. 

    Ripley ran over with her usual little meow of greeting and Lena paused to give her a few full body pats as compensation for being away for so long. Wincing a little as she put pressure on her sore toes, by doing her now habitual skip over Ripley’s bed, she made her way to the labs. She pulled up the inventory lists and did a quick search, then frowned. She checked again then sighed. Well at least she had something to talk about in her post for the day. 

    She turned on the log feed and waited a moment to make sure the switch happened with the feed.

    “I have a potentially shocking thing to tell everyone," she began. "I own one hundred and six pairs of shoes.”

    She paused for dramatic effect.

    “Guess how many I brought to space?” Another pause. “Two!”

    She held up her sneaker with a sigh and showed off the damage as best she could.

    “You know, I really should have seen this coming. Now I have to either be barefoot -which would violate OHS,- wear the boots I have for the animal pens, or…”

    Lena considered the shoe. It was just the sole that had torn away. She thought back to her toolbox and the various ways she could attempt repairs. She decided to stick with the basics.

    “Electrical or Duct tape? Which will be more effective?” she asked.

    She looked over at the chat. Most people seemed to be saying duct tape but a few people were saying to just glue it back together instead. Her phone chimed with another message. 

    Kara: Duct tape. Trust me it’ll last longer.

    “Duct tape it is,” Lena declared.

    She leaned over to open one of the draws and remove a roll of duct tape and dropped it on the table. She gave the camera a smile. 

    “Which tells you I really am an engineer. I always have duct tape and WD40 on hand.”

    She reached into the draw and took out the bottle of WD40 holding it up to the camera and giving it a little shake before returning it to the draw. Ripley prevented her closing the draw by sticking her head in it.

    “Ripley I’m trying to work here,” Lena said with a sigh. 

    Ripley meowed and tried to put her head deeper into the draw.

    “Baby no,” Lena said gently pushing her out of the way and closing the draw.

    Ripley did not seem pleased as she pawed at the draw. Lena let her. She turned her attention to her shoe and carefully lifted the edge of the tape before circling it twice around her shoes. Her phone beeped again.

    Kara: minimum six times around and have the final end on the top or you’ll regret it.

    Lena smiled and nodded to the camera adding another five wraps to be sure.

    She put her shoes back on then did a few more wraps around the toes. Satisfied she put them on again and tested the feeling. She could deal with it.

    “Hey Jess, you said to think of something practical for my Christmas present. I’ve changed my mind, I don’t want a new sander, I want new shoes.”

    Chapter End Notes

    Poor Lena, all those heels and she didn't bring a single pair to space. Good thing she had duct tape. Until next time, stay safe y'all.
