    December 25- DAY 165

    Chapter Notes

    “Merry Christmas to me!” Lena said throwing her hands wide.

    “And for some of you. Yes I am going by Australian time because orbit. Now what is my plan today you wonder? I’ve fed everyone and now me, so my intent is to watch Die Hard and eat ice cream then drink only two glasses of whiskey because I can’t get drunk in case anything breaks and I need to fix it before I die, which is sad.”

    Lena smiled as Ripley turned to look at her from her place in front of the rats. The proximity alert came through and Lena smiled.

    “I think Santa might be here,” she said, tapping the buttons to let Kara in. 

    Barely twenty seconds later Kara sped in and came to a stop wearing a Santa hat and a bright smile. Lena couldn’t help her answering smile. 

    “Merry Christmas!” Kara called.

    “Merry Christmas Supergirl,” Lena said, a warmth was growing inside her as she looked at Kara.

    Kara came over as Lena rose and then she was swept into a hug by her favourite Kryptonian. 

    “Santa asked me to do the space run for him,” Kara said.

    Lena laughed and forced herself to let go.

    “Uh huh, well then I guess I’m doing another unboxing video,” she said.

    Kara grinned and they took their places in front of the camera. Kara held out the first package. It was wrapped in paper covered in cartoon dogs. Lena smiled.

    “Kara?” she guessed.

    Kara nodded.

    Lena opened it and laughed as she found a half dozen t-shirts with the crest of the house of El on them. She grinned and ducked out of view of the camera whipping off her t-shirt and putting on one. She climbed back into her chair with a smile.

    “Is this blasphemy?” she asked. “A Luthor wearing the crest of the house of El.”

    Kara laughed then her face softened as she reached out brushing something off Lena’s shoulder.

    “You are my friend, and so an honorary member of my family,” she said, her voice soft.

    Lena noticed the oddly dreamy look in Kara’s eyes as she looked at the crest of El emblazoned over her chest

    “You good there Supergirl?” she asked gently.

    Kara shook her head to clear it and gave Lena a quick smile.

    “Yeah, totally. Your next gift is from Jess. She said they’re a replacement for your other shoes.”

    Kara handed over the box and Lena smiled easily tearing through the black wrapping paper. She stared at the label and sighed.

    “Jess OHS says I’m not allowed to wear heels in the lab. You insisted after the last time.”

    Lena opened the box and laughed as she saw nestled inside the Louboutin box was a pair of sneakers identical to her duct taped ones. There was a note pinned to the shoes. Had you for a minute there didn’t I? - Jess

    Lena sighed.

    “Yes Jess, you did.”

    She put on the replacement sneakers with a sigh of relief. 

    The next gift was from Sam. It was a 6000 piece puzzle, and she’d painted over the picture on the box, to make it harder. 

    “I really hope Ripley doesn’t try to eat this,” Lena said.

    “Just do it by the fish pond, there’s no wind in the station and she’ll be too busy watching the fish,” Kara suggested. 

    Lena nodded, that would work. Next was a small package from Alex. She’d given her a green fidget spinner and a set of about two dozen hand made cue cards all with dos and don’ts for talking about Supergirl. The note attached told her to memorise them and use the fidget spinner out of sight when she got asked difficult questions. Lena smiled as she flicked through them. Alex apparently had a secret talent for drawing angry emojis.

    There were red lines through some parts of what seemed to be the original printed black text with corrections underneath in red.

    Don’t jump off buildings when telling people who you are that you know Supergirl.

    She looked at Kara.

    “Did she originally make these for you?” Lena asked holding up the card.

    Kara made a face then nodded.

    “She thought I should have handy reminders since apparently my incredible Kryptonian memory did not include her advice.”

    Lena smiled a little wider picturing Kara being given the cards by a frustrated Alex.

    “You jumped off a building?” she asked.

    Kara sighed.

    “I was just going to fly upwards but he thought I was coming out to him and I was having a rough day so I just fell backwards off the building before flying up. You know, for dramatic effect.”

    Lena barely controlled her snicker at Kara’s pained expression. Then she saw the next card.

    Remember you are not defined by what HE did. 

    In red below was an added note.

    That goes doubly for you Lena.

    A sudden prickle of tears filled her eyes. She looked up at Kara to see her smiling very softly at her. 

    “She might be grumpy and mean on the outside but she cares very deeply. Even when threatening to take away your share of Christmas dessert.”

    Lena looked at the cards again and suddenly they were no longer an attempt by Alex to get Lena to stop making her job harder and became something… more.

    “I didn’t know she cared,” she admitted. “We haven’t talked very much.”

    Kara covered Lena’s hand with her own.

    “Like I said, you’re a part of my family. That comes with family beyond just me.”

    Lena managed not to sniffle but it was close as she carefully set the cards on the t-shirts from Kara.

    Lena expected that to be all but when she looked at Kara she was smiling at her with her hands behind her back.

    “Close your eyes and hold out your hands,” she said.

    Lena obeyed with a small smile. She shivered as Kara’s hands brushed hers, slipping what felt like a two fingered glove onto her middle and pointer fingers before depositing something warm and metallic feeling in her hands. Lena’s hands instinctively curled around the edges of the oddly bumpy object.

    “Okay,” Kara said.

    Lena opened her eyes and stared in amazement at the crystal shard in her hands. There was several odd metal pieces that seemed to be a part of it but otherwise the blue crystal looked oddly familiar.

    “Is this-”

    “It’s a Kryptonian data crystal, with some adaptation,” Kara said.

    Lena looked up at Kara in shock.

    “It’s a uh… well it’s a toy on Krypton. We didn’t really play games like humans so our toys were all educational. This one I had to modify so a human could use it but, it’s a map.”

    Lena was struggling to understand even as she held the crystal in her hands. 

    “I had to make some other changes just because the information on it was in Kryptonian not English. So I had to add a bit of myself to get it to work but I-”

    “Thank you,” Lena whispered. 

    Kara met her eyes and she gave Lena a nervous smile. 

    “You said once that you wished you knew more about Krypton, this can tell you. It’s a map of- of my planet.”

    Lena could feel something squeezing her heart, something big and powerful and it was desperately trying to escape.

    “Here,” Kara carefully took Lena’s left hand and gently manipulated it so Lena’s gloved fingers slid along one of the metal pieces. A hologram emerged from the crystal showing a planet. 

    “You can use the finger gloves to interact with it. The ones on your left hand are for the crystal, and the ones on your right for the hologram.”

    Lena cautiously raised her right hand to touch part of the hologram and it changed, zooming in.

    A dozen or so red dots appeared on the map like pins. She tapped one and the hologram shifted again showing her a view of what looked like a city. She heard Kara’s voice come from the crystal.

    “The city of Kandor.”

    She looked at Kara who gave her a smile and a shrug.

    “I had to put a little of my DNA into it to get it to work, so it’s in my voice. It’ll be like me telling you about it, it is in a way actually. It also uh… you’ll find them if you play around with it but the blue dots, they’re- they’re my stories. About my family my… my life on Krypton and who I used to be… before.”

    Lena was having trouble with the feelings building in her chest. She felt her fingers slide along the metal and the hologram disappeared then she was throwing herself at Kara holding her as tight as she could as her eyes filled with tears. She clung to Kara and was relieved when Kara hugged her back tight enough to hurt.

    “Thank you Kara,” she whispered. 

    Kara buried her face in Lena’s neck to whisper back.

    “I always wanted to share everything with you.”

    Lena didn’t know what to say, everything inside her was threatening to burst out of her chest and she needed to do something to show Kara how much it meant. Her head turned and she kissed Kara’s cheek.

    Kara sucked in a breath and Lena worried for a second that she’d gone too far moving her face back but then Kara’s head turned and she felt the press of Kara’s lips on her cheek in return. She felt her heart beating faster as the emotions in her seemed to be bursting from every point of contact between her and Kara as she looked at Kara’s blushing face. Lena stared deep into Kara’s eyes and saw the same emotional mess swirling inside them as was brewing in her.

    There was a meow and then Lena felt something very like a shove in her back followed by a dozen needle like pricks as Ripley’s claws dug into her. Lena swore and Kara quickly removed Ripley before she could damage Lena further. Lena turned to glare at Ripley who was now climbing Kara to sit on her shoulders.

    Lena didn’t know if she was grateful for Ripley breaking the moment or not. On one hand it did mean she hadn’t kissed her best friend for the whole world to see, on the other… well now the moment was gone. 

    Lena took a deep breath trying to steady herself as she looked back down at the crystal. Kara had made this for her. It had a piece of Kara’s DNA inside it. She glanced up at Kara and gave her a quick smile before looking back down at the crystal again.

    “Thank you,” she said again. “Your gift it’s- it’s far too generous. You’re putting a lot of trust in me.”

    Kara let out a small laugh.

    “Says the woman who bought her secretary a new car for Christmas.”

    “The is entirely practical,” Lena said looking up quickly, feeling a little relieved to be on more familiar ground.

    “How?” Kara asked, giving her usual smile.

    Lena could see the emotions that had filled her eyes retreating, returning to their usual state. She swallowed and quickly tried to remember her argument from a moment ago.

    “If she has a new car she can give her old one to her cousin. Kim just finished their masters and could really use a reliable car to get to and from work,” Lena said.

    Kara sighed and looked at Lena who quickly smiled back, the crystal still held almost reverently in her hands.

    “Okay you’re right, that was practical.”

    “Unlike my gift to my mother.”

    “What did you get her this year? Another ugly Superman sweater?”

    Lena smirked at the memory. Her mother had been furious, mostly at the card she’d sent with it.

    “Nope, I am banned from giving her anything super themed. So I got her a mug.”

    Kara looked at her for a long moment as Lena’s smile grew.

    “A mug?”

    Lena nodded, still smiling. Kara narrowed her eyes a smile growing on her face.

    “What does it say?”

    Lena laughed.

    “Love is love, with a little rainbow and an alien.”

    Kara’s jaw dropped, then she started laughing.

    “It’s fifty fifty about whether she just threw it against the wall or she simply decided to trade it to another prisoner.”

    Kara laughed louder. 

    Lena’s heart was still pounding in her throat but she managed to pull her emotions down into little boxes so she could end the livestream.

    Then before she could say anything else, before she could even try to express her feelings about Kara’s gift Kara was getting an emergency alert and had to go. 

    Lena watched the coverage as Kara raced to save people from a collapsing bridge. She looked at the crystal and making sure R2 was by the fish pond with Ripley she ran her fingers over the metal and then carefully searched the map for a blue dot. She found one and touched it. A holographic projection of a red waterfall showed. Kara’s voice came out of the crystal and Lena felt fresh tears in her eyes.

    “When I was only five my Ukr, father, brought me here to see the falls with him. He was collecting samples and I wanted to help him so he gave me a bottle to go collect some water falling directly from the falls. He told me to be very careful when I did. I promised I would, so of course I fell in and he had to drop all his samples and dive into the water to save me. We got washed downstream and he lost two of his data crystals but we were otherwise fine. He carried me the whole way back up the river because he was so scared of me falling in again if I stood too close to the river. 

    I of course thought it was a wonderful day because I got to help Ukr do science then I went swimming in the falls, a very cool thing to do, and I got carried the whole way back so I didn’t even get tired. Jeju, Mum, swears that day gave Ukr the start of his white streak. Oh that’s the equivalent for us of getting grey hairs we get this white streak in one place that grows as we age. Ukr didn’t have one but Jeju always said it would appear one day as a result of all the trouble I got into.”

    Lena felt a smile grow on her face as the story ended. She looked at the crystal again. 

    “I love you,” she whispered.

    Chapter End Notes

    Well now, I have a feeling things are heating up for the in universe Supercorp fandom over those little cheek kisses. Kara gave Lena t-shirts with her family crest and a Kryptonian data crystal with a little piece of her inside it. I wonder what that might imply on Krypton.

    January 1 -DAY 172

    Chapter Notes

    “Happy New Year Ripley,” Lena said, her eye on the line of light slowly moving over the world. “Maybe next year we can be down there too.”

    Ripley meowed softly and patted at her face. Lena smiled and kissed her head before sitting back. On her laptop the news played showing Supergirl rescuing a boat that had been trapped out to sea. She gave Ripley another kiss and was rewarded with a soft purr.

    Her phone buzzed and she saw a half dozen messages coming through. Curious she picked up her phone and unlocked it looking at the notifications. All were wishing her a happy new year. She smiled as she scrolled down. Sam, Ruby, Kara, Jess, Alex? She checked and saw that Alex had indeed sent her a Happy New Year message.

    She sent her replies and put her phone in her pocket. Her mind drifted back to Alex's message. Then after another minute's consideration decided to check the livestream chat, something she rarely did for a number of reasons, and saw hundreds of messages wishing her and Ripley a happy new year.

    She went back to the news report of Supergirl and looked at Ripley in her arms. She thought about Alex's message again.

    "Next year," she promised, gently stroking the soft fur of Ripley's head. "Next year we can be there too. Who knows, we might even get a kiss."

    January 17- DAY 188

    Chapter Notes

    Kara decided to surprise Lena with a picnic for girls night. The two of them sent R2 to patrol the station while they sat on a picnic blanket in the middle of the artificial grassland watching Ripley chase one of the agricultural droids. Kara had even brought a wicker basket filled with their favourite snacks.

    “So you know I’ve noticed something in all those little extras about your childhood,” Lena said, smiling over at Kara.

    “Oh yeah?” Kara asked, licking leftover cake crumbs off her fingers.

    “You were a menace,” Lena said her smile growing.

    Kara feigned shock.

    “I was the perfect child,” she protested.

    “Oh please,” Lena said rolling her eyes. “You were constantly getting in trouble. You and Thala raced speeders over the city when you were ten. They had to call your aunt in to catch you.”

    Kara laughed.

    “Well, she was the one who taught us to fly them,” she said smiling.

    Her smile slowly faded and Lena looked at her concerned. 

    “I still miss her,” Kara confessed. 

    “You’re allowed to miss her,” Lena said gently.

    “I miss her more than I do my mother sometimes.”

    Kara was slowly sinking into herself, curling into a ball. Lena shifted closer and covered Kara’s hand with her own.

    “You did have the chance to see her more recently,” she reminded her. “Your Jeju was gone a long time ago but you had the chance to speak with your aunt barely more than a year ago. She might have tried to take over the world but she also cared enough to help Alex to save you from the Black Mercy.”

    Kara nodded her eyes far away.

    “It feels… She had her faults but I- She-”

    Kara blew out a breath.

    “She was sorry.”

    Lena looked at her and slowly took Kara’s hands in her own.

    “I don’t miss my mother,” she confessed.

    Kara looked up frowning.

    “Don’t get me wrong; I wish with everything I have that she was still here, but I can’t miss her. I didn’t know her really. What little I do know is less actual memories and more vague imprints of memories. Missing her is hard because it was so long ago. But you know who I do miss, Andrea.”

    Kara carefully reversed their hands so she was holding Lena’s. 

    “She betrayed me but she was also the person who was my first non family friend. She taught me how to do my make up, teased me about boys I liked, and gave me some very… hands on lessons about sex. It’s not the same but I can- I want Andrea back more than I want my mother.”

    Kara looked up to meet her eyes and gave her a small smile.

    “Then why don’t you reach out?”

    Lena scoffed.

    “Oh sure. I’ll just send her a message saying, hey I still don’t trust you but I miss clubbing with you and I need someone to talk about girls with besides Sam because she won’t stop teasing me for my crush.”

    “Your crush?” Kara asked.

    Lena felt her throat go suddenly dry. She felt her hands jerk back, pulling away from Kara. Kara didn’t stop her as Lena looked away. Her cheeks were burning with embarrassment and she could feel sudden nausea threatening.

    “Is it a crush if it’s reciprocated?” Kara asked.

    Lena slowly raised her eyes. Kara was looking at her with an intensity that while not unfamiliar was certainly bolder than it had been before. Kara subtly licked her lips and Lena imitated her. 

    “Kara,” she whispered.

    Kara’s face moved a tiny bit closer and then so did Lena’s. Both barely breathing as they slowly tilted their faces closer and closer, each watching to see if the other would back out. But they didn’t. Lena’s eyes closed and she covered the distance between them, her lips cautiously pressing against Kara’s before she retreated.

    She opened her eyes to see Kara still watching her. She expected her heart to be racing at this moment but it was oddly steady. This was Kara. Kara’s face transformed into a smile filled with so much nervous excitement Lena couldn’t help but echo it on her own. 

    Kara’s hand came up to gently cradle Lena’s face as she breathed out and then their lips were pressed together again, harder this time.

    Lena felt something big, and bright, and incredibly happy bubbling up inside her as she kissed Kara back. This feeling was threatening to overwhelm her so she reached out grabbing hold of the strongest, safest, thing in her entire universe, Kara.

    She could barely contain her smile to kiss Kara again and again. She wanted to kiss her deeply, to feel their tongues slip against each other and press close in a heated embrace but her smile and the feeling filling her chest made it hard. She felt so much and all she could do was wrap her arms tighter around Kara and press her lips to Kara’s in incrementally longer kisses.

    As they broke apart again Kara’s relieved laugh set Lena’s heart racing and that big feeling escaped her in a laugh. She felt like she could fly, her heart was beating so quickly and her whole body seemed to be tingling as she looked at Kara and saw her laughing too. 

    “I’m so glad you kissed me first,” Kara said, sounding breathless.

    “Do you have any idea how hard that was?” Lena said, still laughing as she held Kara closer, tears forming in her eyes. 

    Kara laughed again and held her tight, the two of them somehow falling back and lifting into the air at the same time. Lena looked at Kara and let herself do what she had been aching to do from the moment she had seen Kara smile at her. She left her fingers trace over her smiling cheeks and then held her as she leaned down to kiss her.

    Kara’s lips parted easily and they kissed again. It was perfect and beautiful and everything she-

    Something hard collided with the side of her face followed by something fluffy. Lena yelled and then she heard Kara’s exasperated sigh.

    She opened her eyes to see Kara holding Ripley who had caught the agricultural droid and was determinedly chewing one of it’s arms. Lena glared at her cat and carefully extracted the droid turning it off. She’d repair it later.

    Kara sighed again and handed her Ripley.

    “Duty calls,” she said. 

    Lena nodded her understanding. They got up and Kara hesitated then gave Lena another kiss.

    “I’ll text you later,” she said her smile returning.

    Lena nodded.

    “Yeah.”

    She felt like a teenager all of a sudden and it seemed she wasn’t alone as Kara hesitated for another moment then she gave a nervous wave and disappeared. Lena opened the air lock and then sealed it the moment Kara was through. 

    She gave Ripley a kiss on her head.

    “You couldn’t let us have our moment could you?” she said. 

    Ripley meowed back and wriggled to be let down. Lena sighed and set her down. 

    “Hey Baby,” she whispered. Ripley looked up at her. “I kissed her.”

    Ripley didn’t seem to care as she went back to attacking the droid. Lena didn’t mind. She looked back at the Earth with a happy smile. She’d kissed Kara. They kissed now. She lay back on the blanket with a happy sigh replaying the moment Kara had kissed her back. 

    There was a small crunch sound and she looked over to see Ripley tearing off the arm of the droid. Swearing she quickly rescued the droid before Ripley could damage it beyond her capacity to repair. 

    Chapter End Notes

    Look at that, it took them six months but they finally kissed. Y'all happy? How long do you think before they kiss on camera? How excited do you think Alex and Sam are going to be when they hear about this?

    January 23 DAY 194

    Chapter Notes

    Lena had no idea how Kara had convinced her to have a dance party, there was probably kissing involved, and whiskey. Also Ripley in a little scarf, because apparently Ripley got to dress up too if they were having a party. Then something about how since R2 was powered down fully while his batteries charged no one would see her dancing. Yes, it had been something like that, or a combination of these factors. The key part had been Kara coming to the station as a safety measure while R2 was recharging and now they were having a party.

    Lena pulled Kara in for another kiss completely cutting her off in the middle of singing Wrecking Ball. Lena couldn’t quite stop herself from kissing Kara whenever she remembered that was a thing she could do now. Kara grinned and kissed her again. Lena found her whole body melting then Kara was gone, sliding down the corridor while belting out the chorus.

    Kara crossed her arms. Lena sighed and gave in. Kara was near impossible to dissuade from a course of action once she was set on it. She kissed her instead. Then again, slowly deepening each kiss until she felt Kara’s body melting in a way she knew meant she was doing something very right.

    Kara pulled back and Lena let out a small whine. 

    “You might have a concussion,” Kara protested.

    Lena grinned and kissed her again.

    “And…”

    “No sex until I’m sure you don’t,” Kara said.

    Lena tried Kara’s pout, it didn’t work. Well she got another kiss but that was all. 

    Kara came and went a few times over the day either for Supergirl reasons or to take care of the animals. Also apparently to update everyone on the livestream. 

    Lena stayed in her room patting Ripley and reading Little Women. Kara thought it might be too intellectual but Lena pointed out she’d read it multiple times before. Lena was eventually allowed to walk out of her room to watch Kara make her dinner. She was not however allowed a glass of wine. Kara had also apparently found the time to go see Sam to get her a replacement phone. 

    By the time she had set it up she'd finished dinner and Kara was insisting she needed to go to sleep. Lena didn’t argue.

    She let herself drift off with Ripley purring against her on one side and Kara protectively wrapped around her on the other.

    Chapter End Notes

    Poor Kara, she got so scared for Lena she didn't even change to Supergirl before flying to her side. At least it wasn't an assassination attempt, and now Lena has something to do all day, repairs.
    But we all know the real victim of this was poor Ripley who went swimming against her will.

    March 15 DAY 246

    Chapter Notes

    “I have xray vision,” Kara protested.

    “And I have some lead components down there so you’re going to have to let me do a proper check,” Lena said. “You might be a super smart alien but I’m the certified engineer. Don’t make me bring up what happened with Alex’s bike.”

    Kara gasped.

    Lena smirked and grabbed the torch. 

    “Come on, you can be my ladder,” she said, tapping Kara’s shoulders. 

    Kara picked her up and moments later Lena was sitting on a floating Kara her head in the hatch carefully checking over the displaced lines. She found a few out of place but none were broken. 

    “There’s a small tear on the green one,” Kara said.

    Lena smiled and slid her fingers along to find the tear then wrap some tape around it.

    “Okay. Down we go.”

    Kara floated down until Lena could close and lock the hatch. She turned to R2 floating nearby.

    “O-32 clear. One small tear repaired.”

     Lena looked back at Kara.

    “Onto the next hatch,” she said.

    Kara grinned and with a tap of her foot they flew easily through to the next station coming to a stop exactly under the next hatch. 

    “Show off,” Lena muttered.

    She reached up and opened the hatch. Kara’s fist appeared in front of her. Lena frowned in confusion. Kara rotated her hand and opened her fist to reveal a bolt. 

    “You should be more careful Ms Luthor,” Kara teased.

    Lena scoffed.

    “I am, I’ve got a Kryptonian with me.”

    Lena motioned for her toolbox and Kara held it out to her. Lena smiled and shifted her position to easily reach into the box as Kara floated her up into the hatch. She gathered the fallen lines and fitted them back before replacing the bolt to it’s place double checking it was secure. 

    She tapped Kara’s shoulder and Kara floated them down again.

    “Okay, O-33 all clear. One bolt replaced.”

    Kara smiled and they floated along to the next one. 

    “Wait,” Kara said.

    Lena paused with her hand on the hatch. She looked down at Kara. Kara gave her an apologetic look.

    “I thought you should be prepared, one of the water lines is leaking.”

    Lena sighed and leaned back as she opened the hatch. Water still dripped off the hatch down her arms. Kara offered her a cloth.

    Lean sighed and wiped her arms first then put it in place to soak up the small puddle beneath the lines. She found the severed line and set to work on the repairs. Kara’s fingers began tracing small symbols onto her leg. Lena looked down and Kara gave her a smile. Lena bit her lip and shook her head going back to her task.

    “You’re playing with fire,” she whispered.

    Kara’s body shook with a silenced laugh. Lena wiped up the remaining water and tapped Kara to tell her to lower them again. 

    “O-34 had a leaking water line. Repair in place, it needs a check in a week to make sure it’s holding.”

    “How many of these are there?” Kara asked looking down the corridor.

    Lena grinned at her.

    “in the station, three hundred and nine. But only thirty six of them are possibly damaged and need to be checked today. You going to be alright helping me with that?”

    Kara grinned up at her.

    “I seem to have nothing but time today.”

    Lena grinned back and gently squeezed her thighs around Kara’s head.

    “Well then, onto the next one.”

    Kara floated them over and Lena opened the hatch.

    Another five hatches later Kara let out a gasp as Lena reached for the next hatch. Lena gave her a worried look.

    “What? Is there a cut line?”

    Kara gave her an apologetic look.

    “Sorry no, I just-Um... I was just keeping an ear on things and heard the National City Nighthawks just lost their three point lead to the Gotham Giants.”

    “Basketball?” Lena asked.

    Kara nodded.

    “Our women’s team,” she clarified.

    Lena gave her an alarmed look.

    “How are we losing to Gotham?” she demanded. “Their entire team got gassed by the Joker two weeks ago?”

    Kara shrugged and Lena sighed focusing back on her task.

    “And yet, they now have a ten point lead.”

    Lena swore.

    “Should I stop updating you on the score?” Kara asked giving her a concerned look as Lena looked over the cables.

    “No, I should keep up to date with the game really. I do own-” Lena looked down at Kara. “Wait, do I own the National city Nighthawks?”

    Kara shook her head.

    “Who does?”

    Kara frowned.

    “Uh, no one really. They have a few small sponsors but no one really big enough to be an owner. They were made by the league itself or something.”

    Lena sighed and closed the hatch.

    “Well I’m fixing that, it sounds like they need the help if they’re losing to Gotham. O-40 is all clear.”

    “Well... the Gotham Giants are sponsored by Bruce Wayne.”

    “Of course they are,” Lena muttered. “Remind me to arrange some kind of sponsorship deal so they can compete properly. What’s the score now?”

    “31 to 44, Gotham still winning.”

    Lena sighed again and they moved along to the next hatch. This involved going into the next section. Ripley was very excited to see them the goats less so. 

    Lena glared at Darcy as Kara floated her up to the hatch.

    “33 to 44 at halftime,” Kara said.

    Lena sighed and looked at Darcy who was screaming very loudly at them floating just out of his reach.

    Lena did her best to do the checks properly despite her strong desire to rush as Darcy continued to scream. They got four in the animal pens done before halftime finished. 

    Lena was tightening a bolt when Kara started laughing.

    “What?” she asked.

    “Apparently the team found out over the break that I’m updating you on the score and that you are going to sponsor them. So they're now playing better because they don’t want to disappoint us.”

    Lena felt herself start smiling and then struggle to stop.

    “Please tell them they are not going to disappoint us, we will still support them even if they lose. All they need to do is their best, whatever that is.”

    Kara relayed her words to R2 and Lena closed the hatch. She looked down to tell Kara they could move onto the next one when she saw the gently concerned look Kara was giving her.

    “How often did-”

    “I was called a disappointment in some way four times a week,” Lena said.

    Kara rubbed her leg soothingly and Lena smiled at her fighting back the surge of sudden emotion.

    “Your mum sucked,” Kara said.

    Lena shrugged.

    “She’s also in prison for attempted alien genocide and I haven’t spoken to her since. Unless you count her Christmas gift.”

    Lena patted Kara’s head before she could say anything else. Especially anything that might make Lena forget herself and kiss her.

    “Come on, we have more hatches to examine and you need to update me on how well my future basketball team is going.”

    “39 to 44. They’re really giving it their all according to the commentators.”

    Lena smiled and opened the next hatch.

    Kara had to disappear to go be Supergirl, but she had time to put Lena on a ladder before she did.

    When she returned she had a Nighthawks shirt for Lena and the final score. 68 to 52, Nighthawks won.

    “Apparently the joker gas affected their stamina in the second half, or so the Gotham coach is claiming.”

    Lena smiled and put on the shirt. She looked at it for a moment and then checked her phone. Sam had sent her a message informing her she knew the perfect spot for the L Corp logo on the Nighthawk singlets. Lena put her phone away and suggested they watch the match as a reward once they finished the checks. Kara grinned at her and Lena found herself once more on Kara's shoulders floating up to a hatch.

    Chapter End Notes

    March 18 DAY 249

    Chapter Notes

    Before you start reading you should probably sit down.. and maybe get some fluff ready for after... and possibly tissues.

    “Alright, stay still R2,” Lena said.

    R2 did as it was programmed to and stayed in place as she attached the lines to it. 

    “What are you doing with R2?” Kara asked behind her.

    Lena felt her heart rate spike against her will. She quickly turned to smile at Kara.

    “You’ve gotten sneakier since you discovered how to let yourself in,” she teased.

    Kara gave her an apologetic look.

    “Sorry, I’ll call out to let you know I’m here in the future. Now, what are you doing to poor R2? He’s covered in stuff.”

    Lena sighed and turned back to the droid.

    “The scans show there’s possible damage to the exterior of the hull so I’m preparing to go on a spacewalk and make the repairs. R2 is coming with me to record the repairs, carry my tools, and if things go horribly wrong act as my oxygen line back into the station.”

    She finished connecting R2 to the lines and made sure they were secure. 

    “Or, here’s another idea,” Kara said. “I go do it.”

    “No,” Lena said forcing her best smile. “You can stay in here.”

    Kara laughed. 

    “Sure, the superpowered alien is the one who should stay in the nice safe spaceship, while the squishy delicate human without superpowers goes out into the vacuum of space, alone, with only a robot as protection. I don’t think so. How about you stay here and I go weld the metal in place. I can even do it with my eyes.”

    Lena had thought about it. It did make sense in a way, and she probably wouldn’t even have to explain it if she was right. Kara stood there smiling at her with that gentle smile, the one she always had when she was trying to get Lena to do something they both knew she should. Kara probably wouldn’t notice what it was, that was even if any of it had been broken but- but the very thought of letting her go out there hurt Lena to her core.

    There was a risk, however statistically minuscule, and she couldn’t take it. Lena tried to brush the suggestion off.

    “No, thank you. The squishy human is quite capable of doing the repairs while the superpowered alien stays inside and keeps the cat busy.”

    Kara frowned. Lena fought the urge to swallow. She knew Kara could hear how fast her heart was pounding. She knew she sounded like an idiot from Kara’s perspective. It made no sense, unless you knew. And Lena knew.

    Kara sighed, her hands going to her hip.

    “Lena, if you really want to go on this space walk I won’t stop you. I’ll just come along with R2 as protection-”

    “I will be fine with just R2. Really I will.”

    “Lena-”

    “Supergirl.”

    Kara sighed again, trying not to show she was getting frustrated. 

    “Lena, I can see it’s just a few tears in the metal and some of the insulation, that’s not a complicated fix. I can do it in-”

    “No, you can’t!” 

    She hadn’t meant to yell.

    Kara stared at her, clearly shocked.

    Lena could feel what was coming. She’d always known there was a risk but now it felt so much more than a calculated consideration of the available materials. Her palms felt clammy and her heart was somehow beating faster. She felt cold and almost nauseous as she looked at Kara. 

    Kara took a few steps, closing the space between them, her hands coming up to give Lena a comforting squeeze on the shoulder. Lena could feel it happening. It felt like standing on train tracks as the train came rushing towards her.

    “Lena, it can’t be that complicated I-”

    She just decided to get it over with. Kara would find out either way.

    “There’s synthetic kryptonite under the metal as a protection against high velocity debris.”

    Kara’s hands dropped from her in an instant as she looked at her, horrified.

    “That’s the net,” Lena went on. “It’s threaded through the entire outer shell of the station.”

    There was three heartbreaking seconds where Lena felt her stomach twist and the ground seemed to fall away under her, leaving her suspended, waiting to fall.

    “Why?” Kara whispered.

    Lena forced her mouth to form words.

    “The radiation is changed but-” she swallowed, desperate to moisten her dry mouth and lips. “It’s one of the hardest substances available and so it’s the best protection possible against anything puncturing the hull. As evidenced when a satellite crashed into it at speed, and instead of dying horribly as the entire station was torn apart we all just got bumps and bruises.”

    It sounded hollow to her ears, but it was the truth. 

    “How much kryptonite is there?” Kara asked.

    Lena was quiet, her eyes on the floor of the station as she tried to find the words. She glanced up and saw the anger on Kara’s face.

    “Lena, how much kryptonite is there?” Kara asked again, her voice all Supergirl.

    Lena looked at her feet again. She felt her mind slowly slipping out of her body.

    “1.117 tonnes total in this station,” she heard herself say.

    Kara stared at her for a long time then she just looked away. Lena took it as a dismissal.

    She went to the spacesuit and put it on, completing every single check needed. Once she had finished getting dressed she stepped into the airlock with R2, double and triple checked her line was attached, and opened the door.

    Space felt loud despite the lack of sound. She felt herself hyper aware of the steady beat of her heart as she made her way carefully along the outside of the station to examine the impacted area. 

    It was a slow process, made slower by Lena’s reluctance to finish and return for what would no doubt be a fight. She began slowly and methodically putting her feelings into little boxes one at a time in preparation for the confrontation.

    She came to the nearest and largest tear in the outer hull. Making sure R2 was still floating behind her she carefully examined the insulation layers covering the net. It had been severed in several places and she could see the glittering lines of kryptonite still in place. None appeared broken but there might be microscopic particles floating around the area. 

    Particles that could have hurt Kara. 

    Lena carefully placed a small sheet of metal over the hole and began welding it into place.

    Once that was done she continued the process further along; checking each section of the hull, finding a few more tears and one section where the net had actually ripped under the pressure. She pulled out her tools and painstakingly wove the crystal pieces back together to, if not repair, at least provide some protection. It wouldn’t be as effective but she didn’t have any of the tools she’d need to seal them once more. An oversight that, just like everything else. 

    As she worked her mind went back to Kara's final expression, replaying the anger and then the moment when Kara had looked away. She’d always known this would happen eventually. A Luthor and a Super couldn’t be together. There was always some past crime that would make sure she could never have her happy ending. No matter how hard she tried to make it safe. 

    Lena covered the thin blue lines with the insulted padding once more. She cut away the torn metal and covered the tear with a new panel, sealing the kryptonite away under the safety of a solid metal exterior.

    Her repairs complete she forced her shaking hands to start the long journey back to the door. It felt like it took hours, and at the same time, she wished it could last forever. That she could postpone that moment before it all came crashing down around her. 

    Once she was back inside she stepped out of the airlock and walked over to remove the space suit. That done she took a few moments to set aside her tools and unplug R2, stowing all the connective lines back in their places. She wanted desperately to put off looking at Kara. But finally there was no escaping it. Lena took a deep breath and turned to meet Kara’s furious glare. 

    “Who else knew?” Kara asked.

    “Sam. No one else. I made it myself, the entire net was made by me. I-”

    Her voice gave out. She couldn’t meet Kara’s gaze.

    “It was before I met you,” she whispered.

    Kara sucked in a breath and let it out slowly. Lena shivered. She didn’t know if Kara had changed the temperature or it was her own imagination.

    “Why didn’t you tell me?” Kara asked.

    “I didn’t believe it was a threat to you,” Lena admitted. 

    Kara said nothing. 

    Lena dared look up at her and saw the pain of betrayal so evident in Kara’s eyes. She couldn’t hold her gaze, she couldn’t look and know she’d been the one to do that to Kara. She desperately wanted to make it better.

    “I never expected you to ever come near this station and once you had I-”

    Lena didn’t know how to explain. How could she even begin to.

    Kara hadn’t been a factor when she’d made it. Kara hadn’t been on her mind all those nights when she stayed late in the lab running and rerunning her numbers. As she checked and rechecked Lex’s notes and her own. She had been so sure. She knew she was right. She knew with absolute certainty that it was safe. Right up until the moment that every bit of theoretical and testing certainty, every observation she had made from the moment she had seen Kara casually walking around the station without a care… 

    She knew it was safe.

    It all said it was safe. 

    Until Kara was about to be the one out there in space.

    Until Kara was about to touch it. 

    Until doubt crept in. 

    What if she was wrong? What if there was something she’d overlooked? What if Kara got hurt because of her?

    She couldn't let that happen.

    Kara turned and walked away. Lena didn’t try to stop her. The hand she'd unconsciously reached out towards Kara falling back to her side as she watched her leave.

    She’d always known it would end this way. Everything she touched was doomed from the start, tainted by her. Even with her best intentions. No matter how hard she tried she couldn’t do anything truly good. 

    She was a Luthor after all.  

    Chapter End Notes

    So... I imagine some of you will have feelings about this chapter and I encourage you to express them in the comments section below. Just let it all out, feel your feelings... it's okay. This was all planned from day one, it's always been coming. But remember, there will be a happy ending... eventually. In however many days from now, in a story that still has four months to go.

    “How are you holding up?” Sam asked her tone far too soft and caring for Lena’s fragile emotional state.

    Lena didn’t want to talk about herself, but she also didn’t want to hang up. So she sat there trying to find something to say that wasn't about Kara. Sam winced and rub her head.

    “Nothing, I've just been getting these headaches. Must be all the stress from that merger.”

    “Did you need help with the paperwork?” Lena asked, desperate for anything to distract herself from thinking about Kara.

    Sam shook her head again then looked more carefully at Lena.

    “But I could have some of the failed R&D stuff sent over for you to look at. Maybe you can figure out a way to make it work.”

    March 30 DAY 261

    Chapter Notes

    Lena was drunk. Which was very dangerous but she didn’t care. She had the bottle from Jess in one hand and the empty glass in the other, as she looked at Ripley. Ripley was looking at her with those sweet green cat eyes and a concerned look as a soft paw touched her face. For a moment she was concerned how Ripley could touch her face without sitting in her lap, then Lena remembered she was lying down. 

    “Did I ever tell you how we transported the chickens?” she asked Ripley.

    Ripley didn’t reply but Lena didn’t need one. Ripley understood her pain. Ripley had loved Kara too. So she told her. She explained how they’d spent twelve weeks slowly getting the birds used to the sounds and sensations, introducing them one at a time, making sure they would be alright after being drugged for the time required. They’d even made their hutch a direct copy of what their capsule in the rocket would be.

    It had taken six months to make sure they would be safe on the journey and suffer no ill affects from the travel there. Especially given how easy it was for chickens to die of shock.

    Then how she’d made sure that as the most delicate of the animals to be transported they were the first to be removed and put in their waiting pen before they’d even fully woken up.

    “And we manged that because I’m a responsible person who cares, just like with all the protection I put around the Kryptonite but nooo.” 

    Lena flung her arms wide and there was a splash sound.

    “Supergirl has to be angry because I made kryptonite. Other people have it and use it for evil while I just used it for one itty bitty space station’s protection. It’s not like I made a weapon, no, I made something to improve humanity. I did it for science! But just because I’m a Luthor it means I’m evil.” 

    She took another swig from the bottle. Her glass had apparently vanished. Maybe the splash sound was connected… Someone would probably tell her she was a bad person for throwing her glass in the fish pond, some fish related group no doubt. Everyone seemed ready to think the worst of her at all times. She drank again.

    “Fucking bitch,” she muttered. “I thought she was different. But I guess everyone betrays me eventually. Life advice Ripley, don’t have an evil sibling, they ruin your life, and then your not girlfriend breaks up with you and breaks your stupid fucking heart. How do I still have one after all the shit I’ve gone through.”

    She started sniffling and then she was crying again. 

    There was a quiet whoosh of a door opening somewhere nearby and Lena felt a hand on her shoulder. She stared confused at the figure kneeling over her. She squinted trying to recognise them through her drunk and teary state.

    “You look familiar,” she mumbled.

    There was a quiet masculine laugh in response.

    Chapter End Notes

    March 31 DAY 262

    Chapter Notes

    I did not plan these as Easter posts but I guess they kinda count as an Easter... gift? So Happy Easter to all who celebrate.

    Lena groaned as she crawled into the chair and hit the button.

    Sam was waiting for her.

    “How are you?” Sam asked, her voice gentle.

    “Worst hangover of my life,” Lena mumbled.

    “I’m surprised you don’t have alcohol poisoning at this point,” Sam said.

    Lena groaned and covered her head with her hood. Despite the darkness of the lab it still felt too bright with the windows.

    “I do have one question,” she said from the depths of her hoodie. “How did I get to my bed?”

    Sam gave her a smile. 

    “Apparently Lois Lane is a fan of the stream and she called in Superman to help you out. He tied up R2 and then carried you to bed.”

    Lena nodded. She’d need to send Lois a thank you note.

    “Did I…” 

    Lena’s voice trailed off as she tried to think how to ask.

    “We covered the stream with obnoxious pop up ads once you started crying right up until Superman turned up and put you to bed,” Sam said. “Lois said he would have sent him sooner but there were a bunch of earthquakes again.”

    Lena nodded then groaned again at the movement. Sam made a sympathetic noise.

    “How are your headaches?” Lena mumbled.

    Sam sucked in a breath.

    “I’ll uh… I’ll tell you when you’re not hungover, key part is I’m fine but I've taken a week off. There’s been a lot going on with-”

    Darcy screamed again and Lena covered her ears squeezing her eyes and mouth shut against the urge to vomit, again. She’d done so once that morning and she would not be doing so again.

    “You should probably go back to bed,” Sam said, her voice as gentle as the speakers would allow. “I’ll fill you in on things another time.”

    Lena made a small pitiful noise.

    “And maybe use those ear plugs Sup- uh that you have.”

    “I can’t find my ear plugs for some ungodly reason,” Lena mumbled tears welling up in her eyes. Darcy screamed again and she winced. “I’m going to hide in the pond because at least if I die there Ripley won’t be the one to eat me.”

    Sam gave her a very concerned look that Lena ignored as she closed down the computer and went to the pond. Ripley was there watching the fish. Lena gave her a pat as she passed and then carefully attached the diving mask and oxygen line meant for use during deep tank maintenance. She checked her secondary heart monitor was in place and waded into the water. Ripley watched concerned as she slowly sank down until at last the noise dimmed and became manageable. 

    Lena didn’t plan on coming up for a long time. At the very least until her head stopped hurting, or she got hungry.

    Chapter End Notes

    Congratulations to all of you who guessed Superman.
    It won't be long until we get Kara again, and she might turn up with friends. Until next time, stay safe y'all.

    For those of you so inclined I am on Tumblr. I mostly post deleted scenes, head canon, and other ideas.

    April 5 DAY 267

    Chapter Notes

    Sit down and strap in y'all this is a longer one, and Kara is in peril.

    The call came from Sam.

    “The air is full of kryptonite and Supergirl is dying.”

    Lena felt her heart stop for a second at those words. No, Kara couldn’t be dying. She ran. 

    “Tell them to bring her here. The station has filters for-”

    “They’re already on their way. And Lena… It’s not good.”

    Lena felt like something was squeezing her chest very tightly as she ran for the airlock hearing the proximity alert. She opened it to reveal the same car Kara had driven for her first visit.

    J’onn in his martian form lifted Kara out of the car, an oxygen mask strapped to her face, her skin glowing green as she lay limply in his arms. J’onn was followed by a figure in a DEO spacesuit who leapt from the car into the airlock.

    Lena closed the external doors and opened the airlock to let them in. 

    “This way,” Lena said, leading them at a run.

    In the med bay J’onn rushed to lay Kara on a bed as Alex revealed herself to be the one in the suit.

    “How good are your filters?” Alex asked, throwing away her helmet.

    “They’re designed to remove kryptonite particles from the air as part of regular air filtration,” Lena said rushing for a cupboard.

    “We need to get he kryptonite out of her now,” Alex snapped. “And I need a heart monitor on-”

    Lena rushed back from the cupboard with a watch she wrapped tightly around Kara’s wrist then dived for a screen, pulling her stats up in seconds. Alex threw off the last of the space suit and moved to Kara’s side.

    “How much are they able to-”

    “Not fast enough,” Lena said, she frantically looked around them, then up at the ceiling. “J’onn there’s an air purifier right there, pull it down.”

    “Her heart is slowing,” Alex said. 

    “Can you get a tube in her,” Lena asked.

    Alex nodded and apparently understanding what Lena was planning she moved for the oxygen tanks. 

    Lena did her best not to look at Kara’s unmoving body as she tore apart the air purifier. She didn’t have time, she needed to get this to work. She grabbed a second oxygen tank and mask, her fumbling fingers searching for the lines she needed.

    There was a loud crash and Lena looked up to see Kara’s whole body jerk violently as she began to seize.

    “J’onn!” Alex yelled.

    He grabbed hold of Kara and tried to hold her in place as Alex desperately tried to get the tube in as Kara’s heartbeat kept dropping. Then stopped as her body went still.

    Kara was dying.

    Lena felt her heart stop in sudden panic as the world started to fall out from under her. 

    “Damnit Kara!” Alex yelled.

    She pulled out an electric baton and slammed it into Kara’s chest, once, twice. Kara’s body spasmed each time, then on a third her pulse returned, sluggish but there. 
    Lena refocused on her task. She didn’t have time to worry when Kara’s life was on the line.

    Lena heard the hiss as the oxygen tank was turned up, then the increasing beep as Kara’s heart sped up with it. She dared not look away as she carefully connected the lines. She couldn’t mess this up or Kara would die.

    She heard a choking noise coming from Kara and fought the urge to watch. Taking a deep breath she kept her eyes and hands focused on the task before her. She finished the connection and began the next one. 

    “Kara! Kara, we need you stop struggling and just breathe,” Alex said.

    There was a loud thud and J’onn grunted. 

    “Lena how long?” Alex demanded.

    “Thirty seconds!”

    “Kara it’s going to be okay, I’m right here,” Alex said. “Stop fighting, you’re safe.”

    Kara made another choking noise.

    “Lena?” Alex asked, her voice increasingly panicked.

    Lena finished the second connection and grabbed a mask from another bed, hurriedly pulling it free. She allowed herself one glance at Kara as she did. Alex was looking worriedly at Kara gently stroking her face as J’onn held her twitching body in place, the green tinge getting brighter.

    “Just hang in there Kara. We’re getting it out but you need to breathe,” Alex said. 

    Kara stopped straining against J’onn as Lena came over with her modified machine.

    “Okay, this should work, but it won’t-”

    Alex was already ripping the tube out of Kara’s throat. Lena moved in the moment it was gone, covering Kara’s face with the modified oxygen mask. 

    Not stopping to check further she went over to a nearby computer, her fingers moving rapidly over the keyboard to pull up the purifiers. She needed to increase their power and speed. 

    As her changes went through they heard the whistle of the air system picking up. Lena looked at Kara again. Just in time to see her eyes open and hear her scream. 

    Lena didn’t know Kara could make that noise. 

    A noise of agony so deep it seemed to tear Lena’s heart from her chest.

    Then suddenly it stopped. 

    Lena saw Alex slowly finish emptying the syringe into Kara’s neck as Kara’s whole body went limp. 

    Lena wasn’t sure if it was her imagination but Kara’s veins seemed to be glowing brighter.

    Alex carefully refitted the mask over Kara’s mouth as J’onn righted everything that had fallen over. Alex sniffed and Lena saw tears rolling down her face as she carefully brushed her fingers over Kara’s face.

    She was still in her supersuit, her hair somehow still that perfect golden wave as Alex continued to wipe what Lena realised were tears off her sister’s face.

    “It’s alright Kara,” Alex said. “You’re going to be okay now. You’re safe here.”

    Lena wanted to say something but she didn’t know what. J’onn placed his hand on Alex’s shoulder and she looked up. Alex’s gaze met Lena’s and she felt so terribly seen in that moment but she couldn’t look away.

    There was a small terrified meow.

    As one, Alex, J’onn, and Lena turned to see Ripley standing in the open doorway of the med bay, R2 hovering behind her. There was a second as they all stared, then Ripley ran away followed by R2. 

    “Fuck!” Alex and Lena said at the same moment.

    Lena put her head in her hands angry tears filling her eyes as Alex looked up at the ceiling. Lena took a deep breath and prepared herself. She rose taking a step towards Alex.

    “Alex I-”

    “No.” Alex cut her off. “That’s not your fault. There’s always been a risk, and…” 

    Alex took a deep breath and looked back at Kara, who lay on the bed breathing slowly but evenly. Lena watched Alex softly brush her fingers over Kara’s face.

    “She’s taken in a lot and it’ll probably take a while for her body to be able to move it all out but… for now she’s safe.”

    Lena sighed and looked back at the computer. She started pulling up the data on the levels of kryptonite now in the air filtration system, and by extension, Kara. It took a few tries and she had to time it to get the numbers but once she did she was fairly confident.

    She turned around to see Alex had put an IV line in Kara’s neck.

    “It might take a few days before her system is clean,” Lena said quietly.

    Alex nodded and moved to the supplies cupboard. It was locked. Before Lena could provide the code J’onn put his hand in and removed something. He held it out to Alex, who nodded.

    “I’ll go collect your suit from the hallway,” he said.

    As he passed Kara he stopped and picked up one of the blankets on the end of another bed. He covered her with the blanket, tucking it gently around her unconscious form. Lena felt a sudden wave of tears threaten at seeing him gently squeeze Kara’s limp hand.

    Alex and Lena were left alone in the med bay with Kara. Alex ripped open some of the packaging and pulled out a bottle. Lena said nothing as Alex added the whole bottle to the IV bag. That done Alex stared down at her sister, her mind clearly going somewhere very painful as fresh tears rolled down her face.

    “Will you, will you be staying?” Lena asked awkwardly.

    Alex drew in a deep breath and gently rubbed away the crinkle in Kara’s forehead.

    “I can’t.”

    Lena looked around then back at her. Alex took another deep breath and turned to Lena. The mantle of DEO agent settling over her once more.

    “I am sorry to ask but-”

    “You don’t need to ask,” Lena said.

    She looked away from Alex back to the computer.

    “You know I’ll always look after her,” she said, her voice shaking more than she would like. Gritting her teeth Lena forced it back to normal. “This station is the safest place for her if the planet is polluted. I imagine Superman is…”

    “He went offworld the day after he tucked you into bed. We’ve already sent him a communication to let him know not to return yet and why.”

    Lena nodded.

    “I have…” Alex drew in another deep breath. “I don’t know how much you know about what’s been going on over the last two weeks. Sam mentioned she hadn’t had a chance to fill you in yet but… it’s been pretty busy and I’m sort of stuck working with a few people I don’t know very well. There’s…”

    Alex seemed to be struggling to speak. She cleared her throat again. Lena turned to see Alex Danvers looking at her, apparently nervous.

    “I would appreciate having your help in finding a solution. In finding a way to make it safe for her back on earth.”

    “Of course,” Lena said. “People underestimate me but I care. I really do.”

    Alex gave her a small smile.

    “I know you do Lena.”

    Lena found herself uncomfortable with the level of trust Alex was expressing in her. Especially after everything. She looked back at the screen in front of her pretending to be able to read the numbers blurred by unshed tears. 

    “Try to keep her sedated until the kryptonite is out of her system,” Alex said. “Once the needle is forced out you’ve probably got about a minute before she wakes up. I’ve added a sedative to her IV but even in her weakened state she’ll burn through it quickly. If she twitches add more. I’ll uh… I’ll try and get someone to bring you more because I have no doubt she’ll go through your entire supply pretty quickly. Once the needle can’t stay in just pour it down her throat. Fortunately you don’t really need to worry about dosage because it can’t kill her.” 

    Lena nodded quickly and not trusting herself she scrambled for a piece of paper to write it down. She realised her hands were shaking to much to write. Alex came over and took the paper from her and very carefully wrote the instructions down.

    “How-” Lena’s voice cracked and she forced herself to swallow and try again as Alex looked at her. “How can you be so calm right now?”

    Alex gave her a very sad smile.

    “It’s not the first time I’ve had to treat Kara this close to death.”

    Lena looked over at Kara lying motionless on the bed under the blanket. 

    “How many times?” she asked.

    “Too many,” Alex said.

    A warm hand touched Lena’s shoulder and she flinched. Alex pulled away apologetically but Lena grabbed her hand. Alex looked at their joined hands and pulled Lena into a hug. 

    Lena expected to burst into tears. She expected to breakdown as she was held for the first time in weeks by someone else. Instead she felt her heart rate slow and her whole body relax. New strength seemed to fill her as Alex held her tighter.

    They slowly pulled apart and Lena saw J’onn standing by Kara’s bed. She looked at her hands to see they were no longer shaking. 

    “Are you doing something to me?” she asked.

    J’onn nodded.

    “I apologise for not asking your permission first, but you don’t have any Valium on this station,” he said.

    Lena felt herself laugh and nodded. 

    “Um… thank you?” she said.

    He gave her an almost apologetic look. 

    Alex’s phone dinged and she pulled it out reading something and putting it away again.

    “People are making the connection already,” she said. “I need to get back and contain the situation where I can.”

    Lena nodded.

    “Again I’m- I didn’t think. Ripley was on the other side of the-”

    Alex waved a hand silencing her.  

    “It’s not your fault Lena. I’m the one who said her name. And honestly…” Alex seemed to be struggling with something. “It was going to come out one day. From the moment she caught that plane I knew it. Too many people know at this point and I’d rather the whole world found out Kara Danvers is Supergirl because her sister said her name while trying to save her life, than for her to die and they find out after.”

    Lena nodded.

    “Or have Children of Liberty use it against her,” J’onn said.

    Alex sucked in a frustrated breath. 

    “When I find them I’m going to-”

    Words seemed to fail Alex as she growled.

    Lena turned to look at Kara lying on the bed, the machines around her working to eradicate the kryptonite poisoning her from within. She looked at J’onn and saw his eyes flare red momentarily. She gave him an apologetic look as calm spread through her again.

    “Can you share her vitals with us somehow?” Alex asked. “Just so I can…”

    Lena nodded and held up her wrist to show the L Corp watch she’d been wearing from her first day on the station.

    “The watches are part of our life support system, they record all crew and animal life signs. The information collected is sent to R2 and from R2 to L Corp. I can have Sam give you access to monitor her.”

    Alex nodded.

    “R2 is…”

    “I can lock R2 up if you want but I need him on to be able to-”

    “No. No, I think people will need to see her. To know she’s still…” Alex cleared her throat. “She’s still here. When she wakes up can you- tell her to call me when she’s ready and that I owe her an apology.”

    Lena gave Alex a small smile.

    “I have a feeling she won’t be mad at you. Besides we can’t be sure that people have made the full connection yet. I have a PR team who can maybe-”

    J’onn made a soft noise to draw her attention.

    “It’s already trending,” he said. “Hashtag Supergirl is Kara Danvers.”

    Lena sighed. She’d been silently praying that Ripley had broken the mic again, or some other glitch had prevented the world hearing Supergirl’s name being said.

    “At least it’s not Supergirl is dead,” Alex said.

    Lena gave her a worried look. Alex wasn’t looking at her, she was staring at Kara. Lena cautiously placed a hand on Alex’s arm.

    “Alex… she’s alive. Kara is alive and safe. Everything else we can work with.”

    Alex nodded, her chest rising and falling as she took a deep breath, still staring at her sister.

    “She’s not immortal,” Alex whispered. “People think she is but she isn’t.”

    Lena nodded.

    “Neither are we,” Lena reminded her. “And we run into situations that are far more dangerous for us than anything she does.”

    Alex conceded with a nod.

    “I need to get back and deal with it,” Alex said, still not moving.

    Lena nodded.

    “I’ll keep you updated,” she promised.

    Alex nodded again, her eyes on Kara.

    “It’s going to be the first time she doesn’t have me watching over her when she’s hurt,” she whispered.

    Lena felt her heart breaking as she saw the tears rolling down Alex’s cheeks.

    “You can stay, this station does allow for up to thirty-”

    Alex shook her head.

    “No, there’s still so much that needs to be done. We still need to catch the bastards who did this, and enact a few protocols to keep everyone safe.”

    Lena wanted to say that could wait, that Alex ought to stay with her sister. But she knew that tone of voice, and she knew better than to argue with it.

    “Did you need anything from me?” Lena asked.

    Alex looked at her again.

    “No, I already know you’ll look after her.”

    Lena nodded again. 

    “Always,” she promised.

    Alex gave her a sad smile and reached out wiping away tears Lena hadn’t realised she’d cried.

    “It doesn’t get easier,” Alex said.

    “What doesn’t?”

    “Seeing Kara like this.”

    Lena let herself look at Kara, her skin still glowing a faint green as the mask did it’s work to pull the kryptonite from her with every rise and fall of her chest. Alex drew in a deep breath and wiped her face again before taking the three steps to Kara’s side and kissing the top of her head.

    “I’m going to go beat up some bad guys and send Mum on a surprise vacation to Australia until things calm down,” she said. “You’re- we both know this is the safest place for you. Lena would never let anything hurt you and we both know it.”

    Alex gently stroked Kara’s hair one last time then gave Lena a nod, then strode out without looking back. 

    J’onn came forwards and took Lena’s hands. She felt her emotions surge and then calm. J’onn squeezed her hands.

    “She will be okay, and so will you. Once you talk.”

    Lena felt a lump in her throat and could only nod before he too left. Leaving Lena alone with an unconscious Kara. 

    Lena went back to work on the computer but almost immediately turned around to check on Kara. 

    She felt tears gathering in her eyes and immediately wiped them away. She didn’t know how long she was there trying to stop herself from crying when J’onn reappeared. He gave her an apologetic look.

    “Could you let us out please.”

    Lena felt a small laugh escape her as she rose and went to let them out of the station. Alex was once again in the spacesuit and apparently not happy about it. J’onn had shifted back to his martian form. Lena realised she hadn’t noticed when he’d changed to human.

    Lena let them out, watching as J’onn carefully pulled a free floating Alex into the car and then sealed them in.

    Lena closed the airlock once more and returned to the med bay in time to see Ripley cautiously making her way down the corridor followed by R2. She sighed and moved to block her.

    “Sorry baby, the med bay is off limits for a bit,” she said.

    Ripley meowed a protest but Lena was able to slip inside the med bay before Ripley could follow and lock the door. She drew the curtains as well. Turning slowly she saw Kara still unconscious under the blanket, the mess of machine parts and wires around her leading up into the ceiling. 

    Lena carefully lined up her supplies, counting how much she had and running the numbers to work out how long she could keep Kara asleep. Probably not long enough.
    Remembering Kara’s scream she picked up her phone and sent Sam a message. Sam’s immediate response told her that she had been waiting by her phone.

     

    That evening a sleek alien ship, that Lena desperately wanted to drive one day, came to a stop beside the air lock. Lena opened the external doors and watched as a section of the ship opened and the space backpack floated towards her, coming to a stop in the air lock before carefully lowering itself to the floor. Lena closed the outer doors and pulled it into the station.

    It was far heavier than she expected. 

    When she opened it she realised why. It packed full, not just with the sedatives but also Kara’s things. Lena recognised the blanket from her couch and the collection of button up shirts. Under the box of medicines, neatly wrapped and cushioned by a collection of socks was a set of photo frames. 

    The first showed Eliza and Jeremiah smiling at some kind of party. The second Kara and Alex, their faces squished together and bright smiles on their faces. The third was of Kara and Lena. 

    Lena cautiously reached out and brushed her fingers over the picture showing them smiling for the camera their arms around each other. 

    She jerked her hand away and closed the lid. Jumping up she went to get a trolley to move it. 

    She put the box in one of the unused bedrooms, leaving it for Kara to unpack later. Returning to the med bay she carefully opened the box of medicines. Inside were ten vials, and a pair of needles that looked to be taped down. A note was included.

    These needles are made specifically for her and will be able to penetrate her skin as she recovers. Swap over to these now and every eight hours change her to a fresh IV. Add one full vial when you do. 

    Lena set aside the box and did as the note directed. Her hands felt remarkably steady. She silently thanked J’onn for that. Once she was finished she began taking notes on Kara's condition.

    Kara was sweating. 

    Lena lifted the blanket and saw the crest of the house of El staring back surrounded by darkening patches. She put the blanket back down and picked up her phone to message Alex. 

    The response came within the minute. Lena rose and with a look at Kara she went back out into the corridor. Ripley meowed at her, clearly upset. Lena picked her up and gave her a gentle hug. 

    Two paws pushed very firmly against her face and she gave Ripley an apologetic kiss on the head before she set her down again. She went into the second bedroom and looked in the space backpack again. Carefully avoiding looking at the photos she went through the clothes to find some she knew Kara used as pajamas. Closing the box once more she returned to the med bay. She drew the curtains over the door again and approached Kara. 

    It felt almost like sacrilege to be the one doing this. To be the one stripping Kara out of the suit piece by piece and wiping away the sweat before dressing her in regular clothes.

    Her task done Lena stood over Kara, holding the suit and cape, watching her chest rise and fall now covered by a soft white t-shirt. 

    “I’m sorry,” she whispered. 

    Kara remained asleep her heart beat and breathing perfectly even.

    Lena set the suit and cape aside then sat down. Her phone chimed and reading the message she rose again to cover Kara with the blanket. She went back into the corridor and turned on her tablet.

    It didn’t take long for R2 to appear in the corridor and come to a stop in front of her.

    Lena sucked in a deep breath and pulled on every bit of PR training she could to keep her expression neutral and her voice calm.

    “Last night a group calling themselves Children of Liberty used a device my brother made to disperse kryptonite through the atmosphere. As a result they have made the planet toxic to Kryptonians, just as we did with lead to prevent the Daxamite invasion.”

    She could feel a cold sensation all over her body. She chose to ignore the feeling.

    “As a result Supergirl, who had been patrolling the skies at the time was-”

    Lena knew her hands hand started to shake but could do nothing about that.

    “Was completely defenceless. She was caught by Martian Manhunter when she fell from the sky, and her allies attempted to save her from the effects of the kryptonite poisoning. Unfortunately they could not do so on earth. As her body deteriorated they brought her to this station.”

    Lena knew she was visibly shivering but she didn’t need to look at the station thermostat to know it was just her.

    “Due to the makeup of the protective layers of this station, it was outfitted with purifiers specifically engineered to filter- filter k-kryptonite. When Supergirl was brought to this station she was in a serious condition. She- She-”

    Lena was struggling to speak. She kept opening and closing her mouth in a desperate attempt to get the words out but they refused to come. She looked down and saw tears dripping onto her tablet. 

    “We were able to stabilise her. At this time she is sedated in order to- to spare her the pain of the kryptonite poisoning.”

    Lena discovered she couldn’t say anything else. So she opened the door and R2 followed her into the med bay. Kara lay in the bed, the mask still in place as the purifiers worked to remove the kryptonite from her body with every breath she took.

    Lena hesitated, wanting to touch Kara. Wanting to reassure herself with the warmth of her skin. But she had lost that right so she put her hand on the bed beside Kara’s.

    “Supergirl is alive,” she said clearly.

    She looked from Kara’s almost peaceful face to R2.

    “She is alive. You did not kill her. And once she is awake we will find a way to reverse the kryptonite radiation in the atmosphere and she will return. Until then she will remain here, where she is safe.”

    Lena's fingers gently brushed against Kara’s clammy palm. She sucked in another breath, aware of the tears rolling faster down her face.

    “If you think you have somehow won, you are mistaken. Supergirl is not just an alien. She is not just… a title. Supergirl is more than a hero. She is a source of hope for millions. She is our hero. She is a voice in the dark, and a hand to pull us to surface when we’re drowning. She is the one who can hear our pleas for help in the deepest cave, and on the furthest shore. She can catch a bullet before it has even left a gun. She- she has always believed in the best of us. In redemption. In the value of kindness. She always has hope. Hope, help, and compassion for all.”

    Lena let her fingers brush over Kara’s and she felt the warmth there. She saw the tip of Kara’s fingers were pink, the green slowly retreating. She drew in another breath and looked back at R2.

    “She is a refugee on this planet. The last daughter of Krypton, and, as all of you seem to know now, she is Kara.”

    Lena looked back at R2.

    “And any more of her story is hers to tell.”

    She tapped a command on her tablet and R2 turned away, heading back on patrol.

    Lena closed the door again and drew the curtains. When she turned around she saw Ripley had slipped in without her noticing and was looking up at Kara.

    Lena carefully approached and took a seat beside her on the floor. Ripley made a little trill noise as Lena stroked her head.

    “I’ll feed you in a minute. I just- I need a minute baby,” she whispered.

    Ripley walked away, leaving Lena on the floor looking up at Kara. Waiting for the sickly green glow in her veins to disappear.

    Chapter End Notes

    Okay, everyone take a breath and let it out slowly, feel your feelings. Kara will live, and now she'll live on the station with Lena, temporarily. Which I'm sure will lead to a good and proper adult conversation about everything that's been happening and they will resolve all their problems. It'll just be a couple of days before Kara wakes up to find out... everything.
    Do you think she'll handle it well? Will they have a proper adult conversation about everything? Will this lead to things getting better between them or not? Let me know your thoughts in the comments below.
    Until next time, stay safe y'all.
