    April 7 DAY 269

    Chapter Notes

    Kara woke slowly, her body feeling sluggish in a way she hadn’t experienced since childhood. As she tried to move she realised there was something heavy vibrating on her chest. She struggled to open her eyes and then immediately closed them against the harsh light. 

    The vibration stopped and then something warm and fluffy touched her face. It was soft then firmer followed by a soft meow. Kara frowned and opened her eyes again, squinting against the bright light. 

    She saw… Ripley.

    Ripley meowed again. 

    Kara frowned and struggled to sit up. She found herself trapped for a minute by the weight of Ripley until she jumped down. Kara forced her head to turn slowly, still adjusting to the bright light. She vaguely recognised the room as the med bay on the L Corp station. Then she saw Lena.

    She was a mess.

    Her hair looked like it hadn’t been brushed in days and the shirt she was wearing wasn’t even buttoned correctly. Dark circles were clear under her eyes and she was passed out in a desk chair in a position that would undoubtedly cause her significant back pain later. The desk around her was a mess of paper and weirdly plates, as though she had been eating at the desk and not taking the plates away. 

    Kara felt her whole body relax. Lena was there, whatever it was she was okay. Or was she? Everything felt fuzzy around the edges. She reached out towards Lena and found her arm sluggish to respond. It felt like she’d been run over by the entire Daxamite fleet. 

    Ripley went to Lena and meowed loudly. Lena didn’t react. Kara tried to call her name but her mouth was dry and she could barely make a noise above the constant hiss of the machines next to her. Kara tugged at the mask covering her face, succeeding in pulling it off. The moment she did something beeped and Lena jerked upright. She looked at Kara and froze. Kara watched the excitement and relief on Lena’s face change slowly to a something else as Lena retreated inside herself. Kara felt her heart sink watching it.

    Lena rose from her chair and approached. Kara let her head drop back onto the bed as she got closer. She tried to speak and Lena shook her head. 

    “Go back to sleep Kara,” she instructed. 

    Kara tried to shake her head but Lena placed a firm hand on her shoulder forcing her to be still.

    “You’ve got kryptonite poisoning.” Lena covered her face with the mask again. “Keep the mask on, it’s removing the kryptonite.”

    Kara slowly nodded. Lena looked at something on Kara’s arm.

    “It looks like you’re fighting the drugs but not the needles,” she muttered.

    Kara twisted her head to look down and saw the IV line in her arm. 

    “I’m not sure what that means but I’ll ask Alex,” Lena said.

    At the mention of Alex Kara’s head lifted again and she looked around trying to see her sister. 

    “She’s not here,” Lena said. 

    Kara frowned at her. Alex was always there. Lena looked away.

    “She- she had to go back and try to catch those who did this, and… You can’t go back yet.”

    Kara glared at her but Lena didn’t seem to care. She walked away and returned a moment later with a glass of water and a straw. Kara gratefully drank. 

    “Why?” she croaked.

    Lena sighed heavily and sank onto a chair. She pulled out her phone and began typing something. 

    “Children of Liberty got their hands on the lead dispersal device,” Lena said, not looking up from her phone. “They reversed it back to kryptonite and poisoned the atmosphere.”

    Kara felt sudden panic.

    “Superman-”

    “Still offworld. He’s fine. You however got hit pretty hard.”

    Lena still wasn’t looking at her. Her phone chimed and Lena started typing a response.

    “Alex and J’onn brought you here and I managed to rig the air purifiers through a ventilator to pull the kryptonite out of you. We had to sedate you because the pain was so great that-”

    Lena’s voice suddenly became choked. Kara reached towards her but Lena pushed her hand back. Kara felt her heart seize at the rejection. She’d been gone too long.

    “We sedated you while it removed the kryptonite. Apparently your body is bouncing back despite our predictions, you’re awake an hour earlier than you should be.”

    Lena briefly flicked her eyes over Kara then back to her phone.

    “Are you in pain?”

    Kara managed to slowly shake her head.

    “Sluggish,” she slurred.

    Lena nodded and typed out something on her phone.

    “That’s the sedatives, you still have kryptonite in your body so they’re keeping you from feeling too much.”

    Lena’s eyes moved critically over Kara’s body again.

    “Are you sure you’re not in pain?”

    Kara sighed.

    "I don't think so," she said.

    Lena scoffed and typed something else on her phone. There was a ding from one of the computers and she got up to go look. She sighed and started typing something when there was a meow at her feet.

    Lena smiled down at Ripley and picked her up giving her a kiss on the head.

    “It’s dinnertime for you isn’t it?” she cooed.

    There was another meow and Lena smiled a little brighter before putting Ripley down.

    She looked at Kara again then away. 

    “Alex can explain it all to you, there’s a lot that’s happened and I think the explanation should come from her.”

    She disconnected a phone from a charger and brought it over to Kara. Kara recognised it as her own. 

    “I’ll call Alex so you can talk. She’ll be happy to hear you awake. While you do that I’m going to have a shower.”

    Kara wanted to say something but before she could get her mouth to begin to form the words Lena put her phone on her chest and left. With no other option she looked at the phone that showed the call connecting.

    Alex picked up on the second ring.

    “Kara?”

    The relief at hearing her sister’s voice was immediate and nearly overwhelming. 

    “Alex,” she croaked.

    She heard Alex let out a long breath. 

    “I’m sorry I’m not there with you right now I- I want to be but well, you know how insane things are here.”

    Kara managed to scoff.

    “And now you don’t have Supergirl to help,” she said, her heart sinking at the realisation of how much harder her absence would make it for everyone else.

    Alex sighed heavily.

    “That’s um… Did Lena tell you what happened?”

    Kara felt her stomach sink and tears fill her eyes.

    “Who died?” she asked.

    “What? No, no one. We got everyone to safe houses or had extra security. Not that we really needed it after Lena’s little hope speech.”

    Kara frowned.

    “What- what happened?” she asked. “She- she said-” It was getting harder to form words as he body fought against the sedatives. “Atmosphere kryptonite.”

    “Yeah, we caught them, well two of them. There uh…" Alex sucked in an breath. "R2 caught some footage of when we brought you to Lena it- We didn’t know R2 was there and I said your name, a few times actually, and well… everyone now knows Kara Danvers is Supergirl.”

    Kara let those words sink in. She wasn’t really sure what to say. What was there to say?

    “Kara?” Alex sounded worried.

    “Yeah,” she said. “I’m here.”

    “How- how bad was it?” she asked.

    Alex was quiet for a while.

    “Do you remember anything about what happened before,” Alex said at last. 

    Kara shook her head then remembered Alex couldn’t see her.

    “I remember flying then everything hurt-”

    She tried to think back but her mind was so muddled. She remembered screaming...

    “I can uh, I can send you the footage,” Alex said. “But its a bit… it’s hard to watch. You were in so much pain Kara. We had to sedate you and J’onn had to use his powers on Lena and I because we were- I never want to see you like that again. I- Kara it wasn’t good. And…”

    “It was recorded,” Kara said.

    “Yes.” Alex cleared her throat. “R2 was on Ripley watch and she came to find us, R2 captured a lot of things we really would have preferred it didn’t. I’m sorry we were… I’m sorry.”

    Kara was struggling to think, to understand everything that had happened.

    “Send it to me,” she said.

    Alex was silent and then a moment later Kara got the notification. She forced her aching fingers to navigate her phone. She clicked on the message and saw the link. 

    Supergirl kryptonite poisoning footage.

    She tapped on it and the video popped up. 

    She saw Ripley running up the corridor towards the med bay. Through the door she could see Lena and Alex rushing to save her. 

    It was bizarre to watch her own body seize. To watch the panic of those closest to her as her heart stopped. Then the moment she screamed, then when she stopped.

    The video ended once R2 turned to follow Ripley away. 

    Kara didn’t know what to say.

    She went back to her call with Alex. She hadn’t missed the look on Alex’s face when they saw the camera.

    “It’s not your fault,” she said.

    Alex made a noise that could have been a choked off cry.

    “Thank you. I’m… I’m sorry.”

    “It’s okay. How… how has the world taken the news?” she asked.

    Alex let out a long breath.

    “Better than expected really. But it’s sort of been focused on how you’re dying. You should really watch Lena’s speech about you, it was quite moving.” Kara felt a brief spark of hope. Maybe Lena would forgive her after all. “You should also uh… You and Lena probably need to have that talk.”

    “I know,” Kara said. 

    She was struggling to keep her eyes open now. 

    “Sleepy,” she said.

    “Go to sleep Kara. Your body is still getting rid of the kryptonite,” Alex said, her voice soft and soothing.

    “I need to talk to Lena,” Kara slurred.

    “You have time,” Alex said. “Sleep Kara.”

    Kara felt her eyes close and the sedatives pull her under.

     

    When she woke next the fog of the sedatives was retreating quickly, replaced by her usual strength. Lena was a few feet away, putting something away in a box.

    “Lena?”

    Lena froze.

    Kara could hear the sharp increase in her heart rate. Lena sighed and it slowed again.

    “How do you feel?” she asked.

    “Back to normal,” Kara said, cautiously sitting up. 

    “Then you should call Alex again,” Lena said, her hands moving faster to put away the various medical apparatus.

    Kara gulped not sure what to do or say. There was so much she wanted to say, that she’d wanted to say for so long.

    “Since you’re awake now and apparently fine I’m going to get some sleep,” Lena said. 

    Lena still hadn’t looked at her and Kara felt her heart aching, longing for Lena to turn around. Lena gathered a blanket and sheets that had been piled on the end of a bed. 

    “Some of your stuff was delivered by… someone, I don’t know who. It’s in one of the bedrooms. I assume your powers are back and you’re capable of finding it yourself.”

    She still hadn’t turned around. Kara wanted to reach out, to touch her but she knew from the tension in Lena’s shoulders that wasn’t allowed at this time.

    “I can, thank you,” she said.

    Lena finished gathering the laundry and started for the door. 

    “Lena!”

    Kara was behind her in less than a second, her hand reaching out in an aborted attempt to turn Lena around. She needed to say something, to apologise, to explain.

    “I- I wanted to come. I wanted to talk to you and-”

    “I get it,” Lena said, not turning around. “No matter what we share, I’m a Luthor.”

    “No Lena that-”

    “Spare me your platitudes Supergirl,” Lena snapped. “Now unless you have some kind of medical problem I haven’t slept properly in three days and I’m exhausted.”

    Kara felt her heart sink at the venom in Lena’s voice. Tears gathered in her eyes and she watched Lena leave. She stood in the med bay, alone and heartbroken as Lena walked away. 

    Her phone rang. Kara ignored it at first, until it rang again and she recognised Alex’s ringtone. She forced herself to turn away and go answer it. 

    “Hey, Lena said you were up and moving,” Alex said. 

    Kara sank into a chair.

    “Kara?”

    She sucked in a breath, tears gathering in her eyes.

    “She won’t look at me,” she heard herself say. 

    Alex sighed.

    “She’s hasn’t handled your not being around well.”

    “It’s not like I wanted to leave her alone and not talk about-”

    Kara’s voice became choked and tears filled her eyes, a few escaping down her cheeks in a rush. Alex made a sympathetic noise.

    “Maybe start with your phone being destroyed when an evil version of you turned up at a wedding in the multi- Actually maybe start with the multiverse. She’ll probably be intrigued enough by the concept to let you keep talking.”

    Kara felt a tiny smile touch the edges of her mouth.

    “Not the time travel?” she asked.

    “If you tell her about time travel she’ll ask why you didn’t use it to come back earlier and try to talk with her.”

    Kara winced. She hadn’t thought of that.

    “Yeah, I’ll start with the multiverse,” she said. 

    Alex laughed softly, then sighed.

    “You need to talk to her, and the sooner the better.”

    “She’s asleep at the moment, but once she’s awake I will. I- I need to fix this.”

    Alex made a small noise of agreement.

    “You should probably also tell the world you’re awake and okay. There’s a lot of very worried people and I know at least a dozen churches in National City are full of people praying for your recovery.”

    Kara smiled and closed her eyes sifting through the sounds of earth to listen to National City. Sure enough she could hear them, the people of National City gathered together, waiting and hoping she was alright.

    “I’ll find R2 and… I’ll make sure everyone knows I’m okay now,” she said.

    “R2 is the fastest way to do it, if the last few days are any indicator. Um... I have a few things I need to sort out here but call me tonight and I can fill you in on some of the other stuff. Also… Maybe you should catch up on what Lena’s been going through before you talk.”

    “I will,” Kara promised. 

    She hung up and after a brief search among the pile on Lena's desk she found a tablet and sent R2 instructions. She made her way to the fish pond and sat in front of a waiting R2. She smiled at the camera.

    “Hello everyone.”

    She heard the response of thousands of people yelling about her appearance. Calling others to come see.

    “The kryptonite is now out of my system and I’m back to my usual strength, or near to. A few hours of sunlight are probably a good idea for me right now.” She took a deep breath. “So…”

    She knew she should probably say something profound, something inspiring and moving to renew people's spirits. A classic Supergirl quote that could be transcribed and translated and read all over the world. But she didn't feel like Supergirl right then. She felt like Kara Danvers. She felt nervous and clumsy without her cape and boots. She was wearing her softest t-shirt and cosiest shorts. She had the urge to put her hair up and get her glasses. She wanted to curl up on her couch under a blanket with her sister, and eat ice cream, and watch a musical. She felt tired and small and... human.

    She didn't feel prepared to be Supergirl as Kara Danvers. Would people still let her be Supergirl now, or would it be Kara Danvers who disappeared?

    She could feel tears itching in her eyes as she felt more and more people turning to watch her, to hear what she would say. All waiting for her to make the decision, was she Supergirl or Kara Danvers?

    She wanted her parents. She wanted Alex. She wanted someone come tell her who she was now, Kara Danvers or Supergirl? Just so she knew who she was in that moment, who she was supposed to be, what role to play. She wanted Winn or James or J'onn, or even Cat Grant. But she knew what they would all say. Kara. You are Kara.

    But which one? Could she be both Kara and Supergirl? Who was she?

    "Little one"

    The memory of her aunt filled Kara's heart with a sudden ache that caused the gathered tears to fall even as it gave her the answer she had known from the start. Who she would always be.

    “My name is Kara Zor-el."

    She wiped away her tears and sat up straighter.

    "My mother is Alura In-Ze and my father Zor-El. And..." She forced her voice not to shake as she went on. "My adoptive parents are Jeremiah and Eliza Danvers. My sister is Alex. I love my family very much. All of my family, human and Kryptonian."

    She looked past the edge of the pond and out the window at the earth. Then beyond it at the sun. She remembered Rao. She remembered Krypton.

    "When I was a child my planet Krypton was dying. I was sent to earth to protect my cousin but my pod was knocked off course and…”

    Chapter End Notes

    How are y'all feeling? Kara's awake, she wants to talk with Lena, we have a few hints about what might have been happening with her. All positive signs right? Surely she and Lena will be able to talk now and it will all be resolved... right?
    Until next time, stay safe y'all.

    April 9 DAY 271

    Chapter Notes

    Apologies for any formatting, editing, or pacing issues in this one, this chapter was written in the last 24hours... ish and I think I may be coming down with something. So I may need to go back later to make some minor edits but hopefully I got everything.
    Anyway, I hope y'all enjoy this one.

    “Alright Sam,” Lena said, checking once more that R2 was on patrol. “What did I miss?”

    Sam smiled at her.

    “Well if it isn’t sleeping beauty,” she teased.

    Lena attempted to glare but it didn’t work.

    “I couldn’t sleep,” she muttered.

    She’d spent ten hours turning over and over in her bed, half dreading Kara coming in to talk with her, and half hoping she would. But she hadn’t, and so Lena had given up on sleep and gone out. The moment she stepped out of her room she’d seen Kara sitting by a window staring at earth with a sad expression, and decided to try the sleep thing again the moment the animals were fed. She’d had about as much luck the second time as she had the first. 

    Lena decided to just let it be, telling Sam would only worry her and Lena wanted this conversation to be about Sam. Sam was already giving her that look of motherly concern that made Lena feel far too seen.

    “Tell me what happened to you, you never explained how your headaches led to a week off,” Lena said, jumping right in.

    Sam took a deep breath then gave Lena a bright smile.

    “I nearly got possessed by an evil Kryptonian.”

    There was a second of silence as Lena processed those words.

    “What!” she yelled.

    To Lena’s surprise Sam laughed.

    “That was my reaction too,” she said.

    Lena opened the draw beside her, feeling for her scotch, then she remembered she’d drunk it the week before and closed the draw again.

    “Further explanation is needed Sam,” Lena said.

    Sam sighed and scrunched up her face, thinking.

    “So, apparently my headaches were coinciding with the times a cult was trying to use some kind of cauldron to summon some evil witches from krypton, and one was supposed to posses me. I don’t really understand how they made the connection, but one night Alex Danvers turned up at my door, said she was worried about my being exposed to something when I was a baby and I got taken to the DEO. They ran a bunch of tests, told me I was the target of a cult and that they’d make sure Ruby was safe but they needed to run a bunch more tests and they would do everything they could to help me. Which... they did so...”

    Lena nodded slowly.

    “And…”

    Sam sighed.

    “Sorry I didn’t get a lot of details, you know how government agencies are. I got to watch your stream though and Alex set it up so I could talk to you after you got drunk but then everything was very… quiet. I spent three days in a very bland med bay half delirious on some weird green IV. Then at one point I opened my eyes to see Supergirl with a broken nose pinning me to the floor and looking very worried.”

    “You broke her nose?” Lena asked, impressed.

    “Yeah, she said it was her fault for not ducking in time. And she cracked two of my ribs so really I got the worse end of that.”

    Lena gave Sam a very concerned look.

    “I’m okay, they healed up remarkably fast,” Sam said, holding up her hands in a placating gesture.

    Lena watched Sam very carefully. Sam looked back. After a minute Lena nodded and sat back.

    “And Ruby? How did she handle it.”

    Sam sighed.

    “She spent a week at a fancy soccer camp where she got to meet the National Women’s team and play against them. She’s not stopped talking about how amazing it was ever since. She also now has a frenemy called Barbara, they were roommates the whole week but apparently couldn’t stop arguing over whether Supergirl or Batman was better.”

    “Well at least she was having fun,” Lena said, smiling despite her worry.

    Sam smiled back, then her face became quite serious.

    “She doesn’t know how scary the whole thing was, and I don’t want her to. As far as she knows I was just being tested after accidental exposure to an alien artifact and given treatment for possible side effects.”

    Lena nodded her understanding.

    “She got to have the best week of her life while you lay in a hospital bed being prodded with needles and watching daytime TV. Nothing more,” she said.

    Sam nodded, then it looked like a heavy weight was suddenly on her shoulders.

    “Do you want to talk about it?” Lena asked gently.

    Sam sighed.

    “You know… it just- I really had to think about what would happen to her if anything happened to me. My mother…”

    Sam seemed to curl in on herself.

    “Hey,” Lena said, moving closer to the screen, wishing desperately to be able to hug her friend at that moment. “No matter what, Ruby will be looked after. You know Jess and I would never let anything happen to her. She will always be loved and cared for.”

    Sam lifted her head and gave Lena a smile.

    “You know, Alex Danvers said something similar.”

    Lena felt a brief moment of surprise then she nodded.

    “I believe it.”

    Sam sighed and sat back, giving Lena a long look.

    “Now let's talk about you. Have you talked to Kara yet?” Sam asked.

    Lena crossed her arms.

    “About what? How awkward it is to be around each other. How she wants to leave as soon as possible.”

    Sam raised an eyebrow. Lena glared back.

    “That’s my trick,” she said.

    Sam smiled, it didn’t really reach her eyes.

    “It works though,” she said. Lena shrugged a shoulder and Sam sighed. “The stream has just been Ripley wandering between you and Kara begging for treats for the last two days.”

    Lena sighed. She could feel a headache threatening, all that lack of sleep, and the stress of her kind of ex being around.

    “Did she feed her?” she heard herself ask.

    Sam nodded.

    “Once, then not again after you did as well. Something about fool me once.”

    Lena rubbed her eyes. She felt so tired and yet she couldn't sleep. Sam made a sympathetic noise. Lena pulled herself together and looked at Sam again. Sam seemed to be weighing something up.

    “When I was at the DEO, the one time I saw her, she asked about you. She-”

    “I don’t want to hear it Sam," Lena vut her off. "If she’d really cared she would have at least sent me a text.”

    Sam sighed and conceded with a nod. For a time they sat in silence, Lena trying to decide if sleep was an option for her body again, and Sam seemingly thinking something over.

    “Are you okay?” Sam asked.

    Lena forced a smile.

    “Says the woman who was nearly possessed.”

    “Hey, I got a dozen lollipops and twice daily calls from my daughter. I was doing pretty good. I even got to watch Xena with a telekinetic woman called Imra at one point. She was lovely by the way, and really hot.”

    Lena smiled, and took the subject change being offered.

    “She single?”

    Sam laughed.

    “Married, and I think she was going back to her planet or something, she was a bit vague about it. Plus she was a brunette so not your type, otherwise I would have gotten her number for you.”

    Lena sighed heavily.

    “Just my luck,” she muttered.

    She rubbed her eyes again, she needed sleep. Now that she was sure Sam wasn't dying of some mystery disease she might as well try to work on her next problem, that being Kara.

    “Okay, I uh… I’m going to try and look over the files Alex sent me. The sooner we find a solution to the kryptonite the sooner Kara will leave.”

    Lena looked up and saw Sam giving her a concerned look again.

    “I’ll be fine,” she said.

    Sam didn’t look convinced.

    “Maybe have a nap first,” Sam said, her voice gentle. “You can see the circles under your eyes from space.”

    Lena felt a surprised laugh escape her. Sam’s face brightened a little hearing it.

    “That was terrible,” Lena muttered.

    Sam shrugged, clearly pleased with herself. They said goodbye and Lena went out to see Ripley napping on the kitchen counter. Kara was sitting in a chair nearby staring at the blank wall. Not that there was anything to see on it though, Lena reasoned, she could probably see through it if she wanted.

    Lena ignored her and headed for her room.

    “Lena?”

    Lena sighed, she had almost made it.

    “I’m going to look over the files Alex sent. If I don’t come out by six feed Ripley,” she said, not turning around.

    “Lena please, let me explain-”

    “I don’t want to hear your explanations,” Lena said, opening the door to her room and locking it firmly behind herself.

    She knew Kara could break through it if she wanted, but Kara was too noble minded to ever do such a thing. Lena collapsed on her bed and grabbed her tablet. She kicked the twisted blanket down and opened the files Alex had sent her.

    She’d forwarded them from someone called Brainy. Brainy seemed sceptical Lena could help, if his email was any indication. She scoffed at his comments of her being a mere third level intellect. She’d never been third.

    She started sketching ideas but her body was still exhausted. Shaking off the tiredness she kept working. She needed to solve this so she could send Kara home and go back to her life, or what remained of it in space. Her body however seemed to have finally hit the wall and she slipped off to sleep, her plans falling from her hand to the floor.

     

    Kara heard the change in Lena’s heartbeat first. She had been sleeping peacefully for an hour and Kara had thought she might finally be getting some much needed sleep. Then a soft whimper escaped Lena’s lips and Kara sped through the station. She had Lena’s door open in under a second and rushed to her side, her hand on her shoulder.

    “Lena. Lena wake up.”

    Lena jerked awake, her chest heaving and her eyes full of tears. Kara pulled her into a hug holding her tight. Lena melted into her embrace and Kara felt a moment of relief at having Lena back in her arms, then suddenly Lena was pushing her away.

    “No!”

    Kara let go confused as a furious Lena pushed her back again. Kara took a step back unsure what she had done. 

    “No, you don’t get to do that anymore,” Lena said glaring at her.

    “Lena please, you were having a nightmare-”

    “You don’t get to touch me anymore. You don’t get to come into my room. You made it very clear that you didn’t want to be with me anymore and you don’t get to change that because it’s now convenient for you to-”

    “That wasn’t- Lena I swear I wanted to come talk I didn’t mean to be gone so long and-”

    “I don’t want to hear it,” Lena growled.

    Kara felt like someone had grabbed her chest and was squeezing it tightly.

    “I was gone I couldn’t-”

    “If you’d cared you would have told me,” Lena said firmly. “If you’d cared about our relationship, if you cared about me, you would have told me. You would have contacted me. I wouldn’t have spent sixteen days alone without a single word from you. Alex has my number, if something bad had happened she could have sent me a message to let me know. You can fly from National City to this station in less than three seconds and yet you didn’t show up once. I was having a full on breakdown and questioning everything and you couldn’t even take a moment to tell me you wanted to talk. So don’t you dare say you couldn’t, you’re Supergirl!”

    Kara knew she was crying. She had no idea how to explain, how to even start explaining the last two weeks and everything that had happened. Lena seemed to take her silence as an answer.

    “As I thought,” she said bitterly. “Nothing to say.”

    Kara opened her mouth but the words wouldn’t come out, held back by the anger in Lena’s eyes.

    “I- I love you.”

    There was a long pause at those words. At first Lena looked shocked, then angry.

    “So you love me but you don’t trust me?” she snapped.

    “Lena I-”

    Kara didn’t know how to take it back, that wasn’t what she’d meant to say at all. She had never meant to confess her feelings like this. She just wanted to explain, to tell Lena everything and maybe, maybe then she would forgive her. If she explained everything that had happened since last they spoke.

    “Don’t bother,” Lena said, pushing past her. “And don’t follow me. I don’t want to see or hear you. You have superpowers and this station is built for twenty so you should be able to stay out of my way during your stay.”

    Kara watched her go with a broken heart. She felt fresh tears gathering in her eyes. Her eyes felt like they were burning. Kara quickly pressed her hands to her eyes fighting back the urge to scream. If she did she could break the station. The feeling got worse until she forced herself to move, going to find Ripley.

    Ripley was sitting by the fish pond, as usual, watching the fish.

    “Are you mad at me too?” she asked.

    Ripley turned to her and a moment later was purring and rubbing her face against Kara’s hand.

    The tears came again. Kara sniffed and felt cautious paws on her legs, then her lap was full of a soft purring mass. Kara smiled and cautiously began petting Ripley. A soft purr came from her and Kara’s body slowly relaxed. 

    “At least you will talk to me,” she said.

    Ripley didn’t reply and Kara held her close.

    “I wanted to come back earlier,” she whispered. “A lot of things happened.”

    Maybe if she gave Lena some space she’d let her explain. Then again the problem had been too much space. She felt fresh tears as she realised that, yet again, she’d messed up her chance of explaining everything to Lena. 

    “Why can’t I just talk to her?” Kara asked Ripley. “I used to be able to talk to her about everything. How did everything get so complicated so fast?”

    Ripley gave no answer. 

    Chapter End Notes

    How are y'all feeling? Is the angst still hurting? If only Lena wasn't so hurt that she would listen to Kara, and if only Kara could say why she hadn't been around for sixteen days. Lena really needs some sleep and Kara needs a hug.
    But at least Sam isn't in danger of turning into Reign, and Ruby had a good time. Alex however is probably in desperate need of a week off on a beach somewhere, and a meet cute with a certain psychologist.
    Let me know your thoughts down below and until next time y'all, stay safe.

    As always check out the incredible art being done around this fic by RustingCat and Starr, both should be linked just below. For those of you so inclined I am on Tumblr. I mostly post deleted scenes, head canon, and other ideas.

    April 15 DAY 277

    Chapter Notes

    “Monica at the Park street Big Belly Burger, I need you to grab your baseball bat and go into that alley. Yeah that one. There’s a guy threatening his ex and- thank you. Call Detective Sawyer after.”

    Kara shifted her eyes to the next incident and then for someone to help. She was tired of doing nothing and with the station floating over North America it was a good time to try something new. Which is why she had “borrowed” R2. It’s not like Lena was going to say anything, that would involve talking to her.

    “NC stadium security, please head into level three of the parking lot there’s a man getting threatened with an empty 9 mil for his car keys.”

    She heard the security scramble to respond and moved her eyes onto the next incident. She’d been at it for an hour and the response so far had been promising. It seemed the citizens of National City were eager to help where they could.

    “West end of Fountain park there’s three men in green jackets chasing an alien could someone- thank you.”

    A dozen people in the park had all turned and run for the west end. Kara blinked back tears as she moved to the next one. If she couldn’t be there at least someone could.

    “Amy Thompson, go home, your daughter needs you. Sergeant Vimes, your men are in trouble, as usual. Ian there’s a man in the alley out the back his heart just stopped, he needs CPR, Mary call an ambulance.”

    Kara took a deep breath. It didn’t feel like enough, but at least it was something. She was doing something, and people were listening to her.

    “Tucker, your grandmother just fell on the stairs, go help her. Kevin, your dog is under a bush through that fence. Yeah that one, go knock on Mr Wilson’s door, I know he looks scary but that’s just because he got blown up escaping a galactic war. I promise he’ll help you retrieve Pooki.”

    “What are you doing?” Lena asked.

    Kara turned from R2 to Lena then away again.

    “I’m trying to help people,” she said.

    “NC rescue gear up, you have a hiker who just fell down Hawk’s gorge. He’ll need a winch to get up, he’s got two broken legs.”

    Lena sighed heavily.

    “I need to sleep, feed Ripley in an hour.”

    “Of course,” Kara promised. “Air control call flight NA-165 the pilot just passed out and the learner is panicking.”

    Lena hesitated at the door for a moment but then she left. Kara turned again to watch her go, her heart aching. She reluctantly looked back at National City.

    “Manchester Black, you put that down right now!”

    She rubbed her head trying to ease the ache that seemed to be appearing recently. She wasn’t sure if it was the side effect of her recent poisoning or just stress. If she was honest it was probably Lena.

    “Izzy go check on your sister she’s crying.”

    She sighed and let her eyes rove over her city again.

    “Seaward street, there’s a rogue Krolotean hunting in your area. Stay indoors, the DEO is on their way.” 

    She forced herself to focus again.

    “Ivan, your apprentice just took off his harness again and I am not going to be able to catch him this- thank you.”

    Her phone chimed. She looked down and saw a message from Sam.

    If Lena’s asleep you should do the daily log.

    Kara was a little confused by the message but when the time came she got up and went to the lab taking Lena’s usual seat turning on the computer. It was all set up so all she had to do was press the start icon. Sam was waiting for her.

    “How are things going?” Sam asked.

    Kara sighed. Sam raised an eyebrow.

    “That good huh?”

    Kara sucked in a breath and then she sunk back in the chair. The last five days were catching up with her. Even for a Kryptonian it had been a tough few days.

    She’d seen her entire life be pulled apart by tabloid journalists, and if not for Cat’s intervention it likely would have been a lot worse. The number of men who claimed to have slept with her was getting ridiculous at this point. 

    Children of Liberty had been out in force, Ben Lockwood in particular had been doing his best to smear her name. Everyone she’d ever met was suddenly claiming to be a close confidant with exclusive information. It was a mess and she desperately wanted it over. Her apartment had been broken into a dozen times, despite the entire contents being removed by the DEO within hours of the world discovering her identity.

    Every one of her articles had been pulled to pieces for bias in one way or another, and her coworkers were all gossiping about her. But that was expected. 

    She was now effectively, if not technically, homeless and jobless (James hadn’t fired her but she knew she could never write under her name again). Her family and her few close friends were in hiding, and Lena had made it quite clear she was definitely single.

    Kara couldn’t work out why that last one seemed to hurt the most.

    “Lena still not talking to you?” Sam asked.

    Kara scoffed.

    “She’ll talk, but she won’t listen,” Kara said.

    Sam sighed.

    “Well it was-”

    “I told her I was in the multiverse and she told me if there was a multiverse I should go stay there instead of her space station.”

    Sam made a face.

    “Then I told her my ex boyfriend had come back from the future and he’s married and she made an exaggerated sympathetic face and said that makes so much sense, now your ex is married you’re crawling back to me. That wasn’t what I was saying at all!” Kara made a frustrated noise. “Why is she acting like a teenager? Why can't we just sit down and talk like adults?”

    Sam coughed politely. Kara quickly looked around but Lena hadn’t somehow crept in behind her. She looked back at Sam.

    “You do remember this is Lena right?” Sam said. “Enough emotional baggage to sink the Titanic, everyone she’s ever loved has betrayed her, most manipulative parents award, people are constantly trying to kill her, that Lena.”

    Kara sighed, defeated.

    “Yeah, she’s uh… I should probably be happy all she’s doing is being a bit mean.”

    Sam scoffed.

    “Considering everything, yeah. Yeah you really should be.”

    Kara sighed again.

    “I don’t know how long I can handle this Sam. I’m trying so hard to fix things but she won’t talk to me unless she wants to say something mean.”

    Sam made a possibly sympathetic noise.

    “Well you did leave her alone without a word for sixteen days as she fell into a deep depressive spiral.”

    Kara was struck with a sudden horrifying thought.

    “She won’t keep this up that long will she?”

    Sam gave her a pitying look.

    “Well… Lena does enjoy being petty.”

    Kara put her head in her hands. 

    “She’s never going to forgive me is she?”

    “You do have superspeed and access to email even if you did lose your phone so… yeah. You brought this on yourself Supergirl.”

    Kara groaned and put her head on the table.

    “I wanted to talk to her in person, not over text,” Kara said.

    “Well it would have helped if you’d told her that.”

    Kara looked up and saw Sam giving her a stern look.

    “You’re team Lena aren’t you?”

    Sam raised an eyebrow in clear imitation of Lena.

    “You have to ask?”

    Kara shook her head.

    “You’re right.”

    “Of course I am,” Sam said.

    Kara looked around the lab. Lena had been working in it near constantly over the last five days and it showed. The whiteboard was covered in equations and diagrams. Paper was spread around covered in notations. Three computers were all running some kind of simulation. An email from Brainy was open and waiting for her return. Kara had just slid a sheet of paper towards herself to read their latest plans when she heard Lena’s heartbeat pick up. 

    Her head turned automatically to check for danger. She looked right through the walls to see Lena twitching in sleep, her face buried in the pillow. Kara was across the room before she stopped herself. She sighed and turned her head searching for Ripley. It was thankfully an easy search.

    Ripley woke with a confused meow as Kara collected Ripley from the kitchen counter where she had decided to nap and carried her to Lena’s bedroom. She put Ripley inside and closed the door behind her. 

    With no other choice Kara returned to the lab and sat down once more. Sam was waiting with a concerned look.

    “What just happened?” she asked.

    Kara felt a tear roll down her face as she heard a meow and the soft thump of Ripley jumping on Lena’s bed. Lena woke at the second meow. Her heart was still racing but it began to slow. There was another meow as Lena hugged Ripley. 

    Kara closed her eyes, turning away. 

    “Kara?” Sam prompted.

    Kara looked back at the screen surprised she'd forgotten Sam.

    “She won’t let me wake her from her nightmares anymore,” she whispered.

    “Ah,” Sam said gently.

    Kara slumped further in her chair. She had no idea where to even begin fixing things with Lena. Every time she tried to talk with her, Lena either mocked her or tried to start a fight. 

    “How are you holding up?” Sam asked.

    Kara looked at her in surprise.

    “I thought you were team Lena,” she said.

    Sam smiled at her.

    “Who says this isn’t me being team Lena?”

    Kara shrugged a shoulder. Sam sighed softly. Kara looked around at the piles of notes and calculations.

    “I said I’d been trapped in the multiverse and she didn't even ask how I got there. She didn't even care about the existence of a multiverse.”

    Sam made a face.

    “Yeah… I don’t think she believed you.”

    Kara picked up a pen twirling in in her fingers then drawing Ripley in one corner.

    “Well it's real. I got attacked by a Nazi version of me who wanted to rip out my heart.”

    “That’s… wow.”

    Kara glanced up at Sam to see she was giving her a rather concerned look. Kara sighed.

    “It;s okay, she’s dead now. She was dying from disproportionate solar irradiance in her heart and so they were going to steal mine. But my friends managed to rescue me and then we fought and her heart went nuclear so I had to fly her into space before she exploded.”

    She drew another Ripley on a separate corner. This one curled up watching the fish. As she added the fish Kara realised Sam wasn’t talking. She looked up from her drawing to see Sam was staring at her.

    “Do you… see a psychologist at all?” Sam asked.

    “No,” Kara said, frowning. "I'm Kryptonian."

    “Seriously?” Sam asked.

    Sam was giving Kara a very concerned look. The same one she gave Lena actually.

    “Why would I see a psychologist?” Kara asked. “It’s not like I’m suddenly going to get over my planet exploding and the loss of everyone I knew.”

    Sam opened her mouth then closed it.

    “Especially now it turns out that some of them survived but they didn’t know I had. Even though they specifically sent me away to save me!”

    Sam nodded.

    “That would be-”

    “My mother said she never looked for me because she didn’t know where to look, despite deliberately sending me here. It's not like that was the whole point of sending me away, so that I would be safe. And now she does know I’m alive. We talked and she wanted to pretend like her being sorry that she sent my aunt to the phantom zone for telling the truth was her biggest regret, not like you know not saving our planet. i feel like I would regret not preventing the destruction of my planet more than I would sending my sister to prison.”

    Kara could feel something inside her chest expanding and growing. Her eyes itched and she wanted to scream all of a sudden. She decided to keep drawing. 

    “She wanted me to stay, but I came back here and I don’t know if that was the right choice. I know I didn’t want to stay with her, despite having been raised to always value my family and people above anything or anyone else. But here I am putting the people of Earth above that, and what happens? Some group of bigots turn the whole planet toxic to me because they blame me and people like me for the economy. It’s not like I can fix the economy! I’m not even paid to be a superhero. Oh and the whole aliens are stealing our jobs thing, there is barely ½ million aliens on the whole planet and only 300 000 in the US but apparently they are the ones wrecking everyone’s lives. It’s not like the insurance companies are the ones who refused to pay out people for the damage done by the Daxamites, or you know the government who refuse to provide a safety net. No, it’s totally the aliens ruining people’s lives, not corporate greed, corruption, and the accumulation of forty years of not doing maintenance on major infrastructure coupled with the erosion of workers rights. No, it's all the aliens' fault and they all deserve to die for the horrific crime of just trying to find somewhere safer to live.”

    The pen snapped and Kara realised she'd covered the whole sheet of paper in a miniature Ripley's chasing fish. She slumped back in her chair and looked at Sam. Sam continued to stare at Kara. Kara looked back at the paper, then the broken pen.

    “I should probably see a psychologist shouldn’t I?” she mumbled.

    “Yeah! You- you really should. You know what, I'm going to find you some names.”

    Kara put her head in her hands. Well that was going to be expensive.

    “And don’t worry, Lena will pay for it,” Sam added.

    Kara looked up, confused.

    “Why would she-”

    “Because all personnel on L Corp station have access to free psychological services of their choice at L Corp’s expense.”

    “I’m not personnel,” Kara pointed out.

    “I saw you shovel goat shit this morning. Trust me, that counts as personnel,” Sam said.

    The door opened and Lena walked in wearing a dark blue t-shirt covered in the crest of the house of El. Kara felt her heart stop seeing it. Seeing Lena dressed in her family crest. 

    Lena noticed.

    “This,” Lena gestured at her shirt. “Is because I haven’t done any laundry. I’ve been too busy trying to clean up the atmosphere so you can go back to saving Tucker’s grandmother or hikers, or whatever it is you do when you’re not in the multiverse with your ex.”

    Kara met Sam’s eyes and then got up.

    “I’ll do the laundry for you,” she said.

    “My hero,” Lena said sarcastically.

    Kara hesitated considering saying Something. Lena's furious gaze dared her to try it. Kara left, quietly closing the door behind herself. 

     

    Lena looked at Sam who was giving her a disapproving look.

    “Oh please,” she said, dropping into her chair. “She deserved it.”

    “Are you sure?” Sam asked.

    Lena looked away. 

    “She treated me like I was Lex,” Lena said softly. “Despite everything between us the moment I said I had used kryptonite she stopped seeing me. I wasn’t Lena anymore, I was just another Luthor betraying a Super. Even when I said it wasn’t supposed to be dangerous to her. The only reason she’s talking to me now is because she needs my help. Once I fix this she leaves and it’s a Super vs a Luthor again.”

    “Are you sure about that?” Sam asked gently. “It does sound like a lot has been happening for her that got in the way of coming to talk to you.”

    “It wouldn’t change anything in the long run. We were never going to last,” Lena said. “I’ve got work to do and a so-called twelfth level intellect to help. I’ll talk to you soon Sam.”

    “Talk soon Lena.”

    Lena ended the stream and turned back to her lab. It felt darker than before. She decided to blame Kara. She looked down and saw the paper covered in Ripley. Her fingers brushed over the increasingly deep lines. How much control did it take to not tear through the paper with every tiny stroke of the  pen?

    She carefully folded the paper over and slipped it into a draw. She didn't have time to think about Kara when she had to get her home.

    Chapter End Notes

    I promise it's not much longer until Kara finds a way to get through to Lena. Just hang in there.
    In the meantime who do you think should be Kara's psychologist? How many all caps texts do you think have passed between Alex and Sam about all this? How many memes do you think are now being made about Supergirl punching the economy?
    Until next time y'all, stay safe.

    April 19 DAY 281

    Chapter Notes

    In personal news. I have acquired a new editor. She's a bit bigger than the last one and she barks but I'm sure she still counts as a cat. So apologies for any typos etc, she has a lot of enthusiasm but I don't think she knows what a comma is yet.

    “Pack your bags Supergirl,” Lena said. “You’re cleared to go home.”

    Kara jumped up excitedly.

    “Really?”

    “Yes, numbers just came back and the nanites did their job. The atmosphere is no longer toxic to Kryptonians. If you hurry you can go rescue Fluffy from that tree.”

    Kara sped to her room and in a flash packed everything.

    Lena rolled her eyes when Kara appeared beside her in a rush of air. She roughly slapped a small box to Kara’s chest. 

    “Here’s your new suit, it’ll keep you safe for the journey and on earth in case the numbers are wrong.”

    Kara tried to say something but the suit had spread rapidly over her body from the moment it made contact with her chest. She pulled off the helmet as soon as it finished forming. 

    Lena apparently didn’t notice nor care as she tapped in the code to open the airlock. Lena turned to Kara with a defiant expression.

    “Now you can do what you’ve been wanting to do from the moment you got here, leave and never come back.”

    Kara looked from Lena to the airlock then back again. Lena jerked her chin towards the airlock in a silent order. Kara looked at the airlock again.

    The moment she stepped through it Lena was never going to let her back in. Kara dropped the helmet and the space backpack.

    “Then I’m not going,” she said.

    Lena gaped at her. Then she looked furious.

    “Yes you-”

    “No I’m not!” Kara insisted. “If I can’t come back then I’m not leaving!”

    Lena glared at her.

    “Lena, I’d rather be here with you hating me than not be able to see you again.”

    Lens threw up her hands.

    “If that were true then why didn’t you come back until you had no choice?” she demanded. “You were gone for sixteen days!”

    “I tried!”

    “Bullshit!”

    "For fucks sake!" Kara yelled. “I’m serious!"

    Lena blinked, she had not expected that.

    "I was only supposed to be gone for the weekend to attend Barry’s wedding. Which it just so happens is in another universe. I swear the multiverse is a real thing and I would love to explain it to you sometime. But the wedding got attacked by alternate reality versions of all the heroes, yes including me, which was a bit intense. So I was fighting an evil version of me across the multiverse because Nazis wanted to steal my heart, literally!”

    Kara took full advantage of Lena’s stunned silence to keep going. 

    “Alex and I got trapped there for five days trying not to get my goddamn heart cut out. Then when we got back we had no idea how long it had been and you were asleep so I went to bed. I was going to come see you first thing and talk things out but I was woken up by a whole bunch of earthquakes that were caused by my ex boyfriend coming back from the future.”

    Lena was still staring at her.

    “Didn’t you see the news?” Kara asked.

    Lena slowly shook her head.

    “I uh… had depression and seeing you on the news made it worse so I haven’t watched it in weeks.”

    “Oh,” Kara looked a little embarrassed at that. It hadn’t occurred to her that Lena might not have been watching the news. “Well once the earthquakes stopped and I had pulled everyone out of the rubble I still had to deal with my ex boyfriend and his new wife. Who is way too hot for-” 

    Lena raised an eyebrow. 

    “But that’s a side point,” Kara rushed on. “So I had to help them save the future because apparently Coville started a cult that was trying to summon some kind of Kryptonian apocalypse which would have started some kind of evil plague called the blight, which sounded awful. So to stop them I had to go to Fort Rozz to talk with an imprisoned witch who was apparently still there. And because it was orbiting a blue sun it’s toxic to anyone with a Y chromosome. Which meant I had to go with Leslie, Psi, and my ex boyfriend’s new wife. Which was the worst road trip of my life.”

    Lena nodded slowly, but Kara wasn’t done.

    “And when we got back from that Alex had discovered Sam and this really lovely woman called Julia, and this other woman called Grace, were supposed to be part of the trio of witches Coville was summoning, and so then I couldn’t go anywhere because I was the only person who could keep them contained. And they all kept taking turns punching me in the face whenever they started to turn. But then Winn found out about this stuff called Harun-el and an asteroid where there was a bunch of it. So I had to go on another road trip, this time with Mon-el. Then the asteroid turned out to be the remains of Argo city and my mother is alive!”

    Lena’s jaw was hanging open as Kara kept talking. 

    “I had to convince the Kryptonian council to let me take some of the harun-el so we could save the others. Then when I came back Winn had decided he’s going to the future and now we have this guy called Brainy instead. I think you’ve been talking. We dealt with the cult, cured Sam and the others, and then I was finally able to come see you when the whole poisoning thing happened.”

    Lena nodded, her mind working quickly through the deluge of information Kara had just put together. Kara drew in a breath and straightened up.

    “So in summary; right after our fight I nearly had my heart cut out by Nazis, my ex boyfriend appeared via an earthquake and is married, I got to spend three days getting roasted by Livewire about our relationship, my mother is alive and wanted me to abandon earth for Argo, Winn is leaving for the future, and now an anti alien group tried to kill me by poisoning the atmosphere.”

    Lena wasn’t really sure what to say to that, but apparently Kara still wasn’t done. 

    “All I’ve wanted to do is talk to you about it but you wouldn’t even look at me because I hadn’t seen you for 16 days since I got upset about you about having kryptonite and I’m really really sorry. I swear all I’ve wanted to do since Barry’s engagement party is come and apologise to you, make up, tell you I love you, and talk through it. But I haven’t been in the same world and by the time I was it had been a week and I knew I would need to explain myself, but I kept thinking it would be better to wait until I could explain it all in person, then there was the whole other set of problems and it’s- If I could have come to you earlier I would have. I was flying around trying to think how to explain all this to you when they poisoned the atmosphere. I’m really sorry okay. I should have texted or something I see that now. I promise never to do it again just please forgive me.”

    Kara was breathing heavily as she looked at Lena desperate for some kind of response. Lena’s mouth opened and closed a few times until she finally managed to get out one syllable.

    “Oh.” 

    Kara’s whole body seemed to sag in defeat. Lena looked around unsure what to do or say exactly. Ripley meowed near her feet and they both looked down. Kara picked Ripley up and buried her face in her fur for a moment, her whole body seeming to be trembling. Lena swallowed and desperately tried to get her suddenly dry throat to work.

    “Did you uh…did you say fuck?" she asked.

    Kara gave her an exasperated look.

    "That's what you're stuck on?"

    Lena gave her a small smile and a shrug. Kara had a suddenly hopeful look. 

    "Well... I've never heard you curse before."

    Kara sighed heavily and put Ripley down.

    "I've been trying to do the mature adult thing where I engage in honest, open communication in a calm and understanding way."

    "I'm-" Lena sighed. "I'm not really familiar with that. Except with Sam and she usually lets me get drunk first."

    Kara gave Lena a very concerned look. Lena looked away crossing her arms then forced herself to uncross them.

    "Sorry I didn't let you explain. I just... Relationships are still kind of new to me. Um... Did you want to have a cup of tea and- and talk about all the things that have been happening for you?” 

    Kara nodded.

    “Yeah, yeah I’d really like that,” Kara said.

    For a moment they stood smiling at each other, then Kara's face morphed into sudden horror.

    "Oh Rao, They attacked Spike. I have to go."

    Kara sped into the airlock.

    "I'll text you later," she said.

    Lena hit the button and the airlock closed. Kara put on her helmet and a second later was flying out of the airlock towards earth. Lena looked at the empty airlock then back at the space backpack and Ripley. Ripley let out a sad little meow.

    "Don't worry, she'll be back soon," she said to Ripley. "She left her stuff behind."

    Chapter End Notes

    Kara finally managed to get Lena to actually listen for two minutes and it only took her saying fuck. I hope y'all feel better now. They're back to talking and we all know it won't be long after the talking before there is all the kissing and making up.
    Anyway, let me know how you're feeling down below. Do you think there's going to be a bunch of people now trying to access the multiverse? How long until that clip of Supergirl saying "for fucks sake" gets used is a thousand videos? How smug do you think Imra is about Supergirl saying she's hot? What do you think will happen once the whole world realises Kara revealed the existence of a surviving Kryptonian city?
    Until next time, stay safe y'all.

    April 26 DAY 288

    Chapter Notes

    Lena heard the proximity alert and smiled. A moment later Kara was walking into the kitchen.

    “Hey, how are you?” Lena asked.

    Kara smiled and casually floated up to the counter sitting there with a smile. Lena smiled back. Kara wasn’t in the suit today, nor was she in her usual Kara Danvers button up and pants. She was wearing a flowing dress with the sigil of the house of El.

    “I think good,” she said. 

    Lena smiled and pulled down a second mug.

    “Coffee?” she asked.

    Kara nodded.

    “Can i have it with-”

    “I know, milk, sugar and caramel syrup,” Lena said, gently cutting her off as she pulled the usual items out of the fridge for her.

    Kara sighed softly. She was quiet as Lena made the coffee. Lena caught Kara's eye and gave her a small questioning look.

    “Perenna was really nice,” Kara said. 

    Lena gave her a smile. 

    “Not what you expected?” she guessed.

    “She uh… she can telepathically transport me to a safe environment to talk about things. It was… interesting.”

    Lena looked at her surprised.

    "Her powers work on you?"

    Kara nodded, straightening up a little as Lena approached her.

    “Yeah, it’s a… it helps to talk in an environment where I don’t have to worry. We just had a first session today and we’ve only just started but it…” Kara considered her words carefully. “We were looking for the place I feel safest, and um… It turns out that wasn’t what I was expecting.”

    Lena handed Kara her coffee.

    “May I ask where it was?”

    Kara nodded.

    “It was on the balcony at Catco, and Cat was at her desk.”

    Lena thought for a moment then nodded.

    “How did that feel?” she asked

    Kara shrugged and then took a sip of her coffee. 

    “It was strange to discover that the place I felt the most myself, the most… me, was on the balcony. I spoke to Cat there a few times but I didn’t…”

    Kara sighed softly.

    "It wasn't where you expected it to be," Lena said gently.

    “I expected it to be Krypton or my loft but… it wasn’t. Perenna suggested that may be because that was a place where I felt safe to be myself, to have doubts while not hiding who I am, at least in the beginning. It gave me a lot to think about.”

    Lena smiled and hopped up on the counter next to her.

    “So… maybe even a Kryptonian can get something out of therapy,” Lena said, her tone lightly teasing.

    Kara mumbled something into her coffee giving Lena a half hearted glare out of the corner of her eye. Lena smiled wider in response and sipped her coffee.

    “Do you… like talking with Harley?” Kara asked.

    Lena frowned. 

    “Did you not like Perenna?”

    “No I did!” Kara said quickly. “I just meant… not her really just- everything.”

    Lena sighed heavily.

    “Then no, and yes. I don’t think you’re really supposed to like it. Um…” She sipped her coffee trying to think how to explain her feelings about therapy. “Do I like talking to Harley, yes. Do I like unpacking my personal trauma and facing things I would like to avoid? No, no I don’t like it.”

    Kara made a small noise of amusement.

    “How did you feel after your session?” Lena asked cautiously.

    Kara sighed heavily.

    “Sadder, but in a good way?” she said, scrunching up her face. “I don’t know. It was just our first session and I know things are going to get easier and harder. She uh, she suggested I start by taking some steps to reclaim who I am. To see how it feels to be Kara Zor-El, but my version, not my parents idea of who I should've been.”

    Lena motioned to the dress.

    “So is that what this is?”

    Kara smiled and nodded.

    “Yes, I’m wearing the clothes I got on Argo, but I’m wearing them not for formality or because that is what I’m supposed to wear, instead it’s to- to show a piece of me. I don’t know. I just… I wanted to look different. I wanted to look… Kryptonian.”

    Kara was staring off into space. Lena gave her a minute then gently covered Kara’s hand with her own. Kara blinked coming back to herself.

    “You look good in it,” Lena said, offering a small smile.

    Kara ducked her head.

    “Are you blushing?” Lena asked, her smile growing as she saw the pink on Kara’s cheeks.

    Kara quickly drank more of her coffee mumbling something Lena couldn’t understand. Lena smirked and looked away letting Kara recover her composure.

    It felt both new and the same. Both of them nervous and not.

    She squeezed Kara’s hand. 

    “How is your new apartment?” she asked.

    Kara scoffed.

    “It’s your apartment,” she said. “And it’s great. Thank you for offering it to me. I’m not sure how I’d be handling everything if I had to go to a DEO safehouse.”

    Lena smiled and sipped her coffee.

    “Well it’s not like I’m using it at the moment. Besides First Degree Manslaughter, Racketeering, and Reckless Endangerment need company.”

    Kara snorted.

    “Your fish are fine, I cleaned their tank yesterday. I also added a little treasure chest because I felt like they needed more decoration.”

    “I’m sure Embezzlement liked it,” Lena said, smiling into her coffee cup.

    Kara laughed.

    “She did actually, so did Wire Fraud. Oh, and Alex said she’s very confused how you can tell them apart.”

    Lena drank the last of her coffee. 

    “Did you tell her they’re identical so I don’t have to?”

    “Except for Money Laundering, he has that little spot on his tail.”

I don’t know if people’s opinion will still be so… neutral to positive when I’m not constantly on their computer shoveling shit everyday.”

    “Does this mean I’ll be getting kicked out then?” she teased.

    Lena’s heart started racing and then she was playing with the buttons on Kara’s shirt.

    May 23 DAY 315

    Chapter Notes

    Lena glared at Darcy as he screamed at her. 

    “One day,” she promised him.

    He screamed back. Lena sighed and picked up the bucket of goat shit, carrying it out of the pen and over to the fertiliser tank. She unscrewed the lid and poured it in, wrinkling her nose against the smell.

    “No one ever talks about this part of space,” she muttered.

    Her daily chores finally done she sealed it up again and made her way through the fish pond. She smiled at Ripley sitting in her usual spot. 

    “Hey baby,” she cooed, stopping to give her a pat.  

    Ripley rubbed against her legs. Lena smiled and picked her up. 

    “Did you want some attention baby?”

    Ripley meowed and tapped a paw against Lena’s face. Lena smiled back at her.

    “You’re my favourite, cat,” she said carrying her through the station. R2 following behind them.

    Lena's phone beeped and she smiled seeing a message from Sam. 

    It was a picture of Kara mid fight, her cape swept to one side. Someone had photoshopped a stamp on her ass saying “Property of Lena Luthor.”

    Lena was halfway through typing her response when alarms went off all over the station. 

    “Impact imminent,” a computerised voice said. 

    Lena dropped her phone to keep hold of Ripley as the station lurched under her feet. 

    Ripley let out a distressed noise as Lena flew to the side, cashing against the wall. The world became fuzzy for a moment and something she strongly suspected was blood was running down her face. Lena blinked trying to clear her blurring vision.

    “Impact imminent,” the computer said again. 

    Lena swore and keeping a tight hold of Ripley ran for the door. 

    The station shook again throwing her off her feet at the impact. Lena fought to keep hold of Ripley as the world spun under her feet. She wasn’t sure if that was her balance or if the station was actually moving.

    There was a lot of flashing red lights around her and Lena realised the sections had began sealing themselves off for protection. Which meant-

    Ripley was yowling and scratching her in a desperate attempt to escape. 

    “Impact imminent.”

    Lena took a firm hold of Ripley and silently apologising for what she was about to do, threw her through the closing doors. 

    The third impact sent her crashing into the ceiling. The sound of metal tearing finally registered as her hearing returned. 

    Lena could hear the rushing of air leaving the area as the metal began to tear. She tried to get to her feet but fell immediately as her right foot gave out under her.

    She was having trouble breathing and she could feel fresh blood on her face. She forced herself up again. The protective suit was no longer hanging in place by the door it was-

    Lena blinked to clear her vision. She tried to take a breath but there was nothing to breathe in.

    Turning she saw the suit floating a few feet away. She just had to get to it. 

    She didn’t need it all, just the helmet then she could.

    “Impact imminent.”

    If she could get to the airlock-

    As the impact hit Lena threw herself towards the suit. The screech of metal grew louder and she could feel the station gravity disappearing completely. 

    She couldn’t breathe. She couldn’t see. 

    She reached out with her fingers trying to grasp the suit. 

    She was so cold…

    Her fingers brushed something. She tried to grasp it but her fingers weren’t responding. She struggled to maintain consciousness as her lungs screamed for air. Somewhere in the distance she thought she heard a different alert. 

    Kara.

    Chapter End Notes

    May 27 DAY 319

    Chapter Summary

    Apologies for any pacing errors in this one y'all, the characters decided to add a few extra bits at the last minute.

    Chapter Notes

    Lena heard a soft meow and wanted to turn her head but it felt so heavy. There was a second meow and then Kara’s voice.

    “No baby. I know you miss her but you can’t be in here.”

    There was another meow, this one sounded further away. Lena forced her eyes open and turned her head towards the sound. She saw Kara gently drop Ripley out of the room and close the door.

    “Hey,” Lena said, but her throat was so dry it came out more as a quiet croak.

    Kara jumped and spun around.

    “Hey,” she said, rushing to her side.

    Lena tried to lick her lips but her throat was dry. Kara seemed to understand and gently offered her a straw. Lena felt instant relief as water slid down her throat, easing the burning ache she was becoming increasingly aware of. It felt like her throat had been scraped raw.

    “Oh God,” Lena croaked. Her throat burned with every word. “I feel like I got hit by… well you, or a truck.”

    Kara managed a short laugh as she put the water aside.

    “How long was I out?” Lena mumbled, fighting the urge to close her eyes again.

    “Almost four days, they had to induce a coma to protect your brain,” Kara said.

    Lena remembered hitting her head, a few times actually. That was probably a really bad thing. She was sure that was a very bad thing. It hurt. She replayed Kara's words trying to figure out what about them felt wrong.

    She was distantly aware of the door opening again.

    “Who’s they?” Lena mumbled, her eyes closing briefly.

    An unnatural fatigue seemed to rush over her all of a sudden. It felt... Was she being drugged? A cool hand carefully turned her head back to it’s previous position. Lena fought the lure of sleep to open her eyes again. 

    A new person had appeared in her line of view.

    “Hello Lena,” Alex said.

    Then another face bent over hers. This one an unfamiliar man. One who based on his glasses was blind, much to Lena’s confusion.

    “Ms Luthor I am Dr Charles McNider. I’ve been your surgeon,” the man said.

    Lena looked from Dr McNider to Kara.

    “How many people are on this station?” she asked.

    “Five,” Kara said. “There was ten when they operated, but I took the others home after.”

    Lena sucked in a breath, that felt like a lot of people. Dr McNider gave her a friendly smile. 

    “Welcome back Ms Luthor. You gave quite a few people a scare.”

    The door opened again but Lena was prevented from looking by Dr McNider’s gentle grip on her face as he shone a light in her eyes.

    “Just so you know, we made the unanimous decision to drug Darcy because he was making it difficult to work,” Dr McNider added.

    “Why didn’t I think of that?” Lena muttered.

    There was a laugh from several people. Dr McNider's grip kept her from looking but now she could hear them moving around her the number of people felt quite overwhelming. Lena grabbed Kara’s hand with clumsy fingers and held as tight as her weakened body would allow.

    Kara held her hand gently, giving a small reassuring squeeze back. Lena forced her eyes to stay open so she could look at Kara as Dr McNider and the team of medical personnel began asking her questions and running tests. Lena did her best to follow it all but soon she was falling asleep again. They let her. 

     

    Lena woke an unknown amount of time later to see Kara sitting in a chair beside her bed. She was playing with a small metal ball, rolling it between her fingers at increasing speeds until it became a blur, then stopping it and starting again. Lena was mesmerized for a few minutes as she blinked away the edges of whatever drugs she’d been on.

    “Kara,” she whispered.

    The ball instantly disappeared and Kara was looking at her with a hopeful smile.

    “Hey, how are you feeling?” she asked, gently taking Lena’s hand in her own.

    Lena sighed.

    “Overwhelmed,” she confessed. Kara smiled and stroked her cheek. Lena did her best to lean into the contact. “What- what happened?”

    Kara sighed softly.

    “Apparently Ben Lockwood got a call from your brother and… he really doesn’t like that you slept with an alien. He gave Ben a way to fire a series of specialised missiles at the station. The DEO is still trying to work out what exactly they were made with to break through your net while avoiding the sensors until the last minute.”

    Lena groaned. The station was probably in pieces if her brother was involved. Of course he couldn't let her be happy, and of course he would destroy the one thing she had managed to make herself. No matter what she made her family would find a way to destroy it eventually.

    “How much damage?” she asked.

    “It turns out you’re a better engineer than your brother expected because the majority of the station is fine,” Kara said.

    Lena felt her previous despondency turn to hope as she waited for Kara to go on.

    “It seems the missiles were targeting you, so the rest of the station was undamaged. They used the footage from R2 to know exactly where you were on the station and then target it. They’d just been waiting for you to be in the right place at the right time. The moment you were they launched the missiles. I destroyed a further five on my way to save you.”

    Lena wanted to rub her head, but she had a feeling that would hurt. She was trying to remember how many had hit the station. If she could calculate-

    “Please tell me I wasn’t dreaming and Ripley is okay,” she said, suddenly worried she had dreamed Ripley’s earlier appearance.

    Kara smiled and nodded.

    “She was a bit upset about being thrown like a football, there might be a few jokes about it online, but she got over it. The other animals are all okay. A few minor scratches and bruises. Oh and one unlucky fish died.”

    Lena wanted to nod but she suspected that may trigger some kind of nausea response, so she just said “Good.”

    Kara took a deep breath and squeezed her hand very gently. Lena wasn’t sure she wanted to hear whatever had Kara looking at her with such a sombre expression.

    “R2 didn’t make it in one piece.”

    Lena felt a wave of sadness at the loss of the droid. He might have been a violation of her privacy more often than not, but she had been rather fond of him. Tears gathered in her eyes as Kara squeezed her hand again.

    “But the sacrifice wasn’t in vain,” Kara went on. “R2 saved your life.” 

    Lena felt a tear roll down her face.

    “He got caught in the hole the missiles made and then a lot of other debris got stuck on him, it gave you an extra two seconds.”

    Lena sniffed.

    “I did wonder how long before he would be along to share my personal business with the world,” she mumbled.

    She’d tried to make it a joke but her throat was closing off with a wave of tears.

    Kara gave her a small smile.

    “I put him back together as best I could but then… I figured it was better to give R2 a new place of honour rather than risk further damage to the station.”

    Lena gave her a puzzled look. Kara smiled back.

    “He’s now part of the wall. I saw you used a smaller kryptonite net inside him as a precaution, and since we couldn’t really make replacement sections for the main net without your help, I sealed the tear with that. I did try to retain as much as I could but without a complete net over the tear the integrity of the whole station would be compromised. So R2 is now a stationary camera broadcasting a view of that hallway.”

    Lena sniffed as fresh tears rolled down her face. She couldn’t understand why she was crying over this. It’s not like R2 had been alive and it certainly sounded like the decision had been a sound engineering one. She just felt… a lot.

    Kara gently wiped away her tears.

    “I’m glad you found a use for him,” Lena mumbled. “I- I-”

    She felt a need to see the droid, to see the place where she’d almost died. She needed to know if there was a danger to them all. She needed to know that the tear was safe. If there was a fault then the whole station could be torn apart from the inside.

    “I need to see,” she whispered.

    Kara hesitated.

    “I’m not sure that’s a good idea, you’re barely out of surgery I-”

    “Please Kara,” she said.

    Kara looked away and Lena realised there was another person with them.

    “I’ll consult with Dr McNider,” Alex said.

    Lena mumbled her thanks as she heard the door open and close.

    Kara was giving her a worried look. Lena did her best to look determined but she wasn’t sure if she succeeded. Kara began to very gently stroke her hair.

    “What else happened?” Lena asked, her eyes slowly closing.

    “I arrived, got you into the suit and went through the other airlock to get you into the med bay. Alex was brought up by J’onn then the moment she had you stabilised Sam had a full medical team assembled. I brought them up in a rocket and they got to work pretty much immediately. You had a lot of swelling…”

    Kara’s voice broke off in a choked sound. Lena did her best to make a soothing noise through her aching and bruised throat. She had a feeling she’d been intubated at some point and decided she really didn’t want the details. She did her best to pat Kara’s hand despite the stabbing pain from the IV in her hand.

    The door opened again and Alex reappeared in view. She gave Lena a reassuring smile.
