    Lena tried to nod but Alex stopped her.

    “Nope, no moving. Especially not your head. You just lie there and think healing thoughts.”

    Lena felt herself smiling as the Danvers sisters moved around her. The bed moved and she felt a little nauseous looking at the lights blurring overhead. She closed her eyes. She wasn’t sure if that helped or not but before she could come to a decision they stopped. 

    Lena opened her eyes and Alex carefully turned her head to one side then moved out of her line of sight.

    Lena stared at the wall.

    A thick metal seam ran in a jagged line down the wall. In the middle was a large section that Kara had clearly pulled together with her hands and melted several bits of metal to form the seal. Lena could see the markings from some of the external panels mixed in with a what looked to be a few tools and a spare wall panel. 

    In the middle of it all was R2. 

    The droid appeared to be half in half out of the wall, the protective shell peeled apart in sections, each disappearing into the wall to join up with the rest of the net. 

    “Who resealed the net?” she asked.

    “I did,” Kara said softly. “You were right, it didn’t hurt me. It’s safe for Kryptonians.”

    Lena felt fresh tears in her eyes at the confirmation. She had been right. 

    A hand gently squeezed her shoulder and Lena realised it must be Alex. She felt more tears gathering in her eyes. She tried to blink them away. Kara glanced down and gave her a small smile.

    “I wound together all the strings to the correct tension and secured them with titanium rings. R2 is filling the gap where the impacts pulverised the net itself. It’s how the missiles managed to break through. They all hit the same place, progressively crushing the kryptonite in that area.”

    Lena reached out and her hand touched the familiar metal casing. She forced her trembling fingers to trace the markings she’d etched herself.

    R2.2

    “Remember R2 is live,” Alex said. “Visual only though.”

    Lena found herself smiling at Alex’s warning. 

    Her hand moved lower to touch the seam between R2 and the wall. The welds were perfectly uniform. She couldn’t help but admire Kara’s work. The pieces may look strange in their jigsaw shapes but they were all fused together in a way human hands never could.

    A small indent in the metal drew her attention. She slid her fingers over the imperfection and smiled when she realised what it was.

    Kara had left a fingerprint in the metal as easily as she might a piece of clay. There was something reassuring about that tiny dent. Something so sweet about the small mark noting a tiny slip in Kara's focus.

    Lena looked at the camera with it’s little red light showing R2 was still active. She gave the viewers a tiny wave.

    There was a meow and a small rush of air. Lena tried to turn her head only to be stopped by Alex, who carefully completed the motion for her with a gentleness Lena had never experienced from anyone beside Kara. 

    She looked up at Alex and saw her expression was one of professional politeness. But when Alex saw Lena looking a small smile appeared.

    “Slowly," Alex said. "You don’t want us to need to shave your hair just to redo a few stitches.”

    Lena smiled and looked away to see Kara had caught Ripley right before she could land on the bed. Ripley did not seem to like this as she was wriggling determinedly in an effort to escape.

    Lena held out her hands and, after a quick check with Alex, Kara placed Ripley on the bed. Ripley immediately started rubbing herself against Lena’s hands. Lena felt fresh tears in her eyes as she stroked Ripley’s fur.

    “Hello baby,” she croaked.

    Ripley flopped against her side with a little meow. Lena felt her eyes closing at the familiar weight.

    Alex said something to Kara in a hushed voice and Lena felt sleep pulling her once more. The bed started moving and Lena smiled as she heard Ripley start purring. Her whole body sank deeper into the bed as the tension flowed out of her at the comforting vibration against her ribs. 

    She was safe. Ripley was safe. Kara was safe. 

    Her breathing eased as she let sleep slowly pull her down. She could rest. 

    A scream reached their ears and Lena’s eyes flew open again. All sense of peace shattered by the far too familiar noise. 

    “That fucking goat,” Alex growled.

    Chapter End Notes

    They made their way to the dining room and settled in. Ripley made her way over to curl between them as Alex cued up the Thing.

    Lena gave her an approving nod.

    “Kara refused to watch it the moment they shot at the dog,” Alex said. Lena scoffed. “She also refused to watch Roadhouse with me.”

    Lena gave her a sympathetic look.

    “She misses all the best movies.”

    Alex nodded her agreement as the movie started.

     

    Lena found Alex late in the night standing in the kitchen, staring longingly at the knives.

    “I’ve thought about it too,” Lena said. “Kara sent me a text within a second to tell me to put the knife back.”

    Alex grunted.

    There was another scream from Darcy and they both winced.

    “How do you sleep?” Alex muttered.

    “Honestly? He’s normally quieter at night. I think the repeated drugging might have messed with his internal clock a bit,” Lena said.

    Alex groaned and covered her face with her hands as Lena hobbled over to a seat.

    “But I do know a way to help,” she added.

    Alex turned to her and Lena pointed to one of the cupboards.

    “It’s hidden behind the soup pot.”

    Alex opened the cupboard and retrieved the bottle of scotch from it’s hiding place. Lena poured them both a generous couple of fingers and Alex sat beside her.

    For a few minutes they sat in the mostly silent kitchen sipping their whiskey. Then Darcy screamed again.

    “I bet you could distract her long enough for me to kill him,” Alex said.

    Lena smiled and shook her head.

    “If anyone gets to kill him it should be me,” she said.

    Alex conceded with a nod.

    “I’ll tell her it’s for your mental health,” she said.

    Lena chuckled.

    “She was very worried about it early on,” Alex continued. “She sent me a average of three texts a day trying to convince me to let her come see you. I was sent a ridiculous number of articles about the importance of hugs for humans.”

    Lena found herself smiling. That sounded very Kara.

    “What was your argument against such convincing science?” she asked.

    Alex sighed heavily.

    “You didn’t know she was Supergirl, you were constantly recorded, she’s really bad at keeping her identity a secret. Or she was.”

    Alex paused, then took a long sip of her whiskey.

    “Trying to keep her identity secret was the full time job of three different people. We had four body doubles around the world, five different conspiracy theories we were encouraging, and… well it’s out now.”

    Alex looked into her glass.

    “How are you handling it?” Lena asked.

    Alex shrugged.

    “It’s not my life that was front page news.”

    Lena cautiously reached out and squeezed Alex’s hand. Alex looked at her and gave her a small smile.

    “Did you know I yelled at her for saving my plane?” Alex asked.

    Lena shook her head. Alex looked back at her glass.

    “I was so scared, then I was relieved, but… the moment I recognised my little sister on the wing of that plane. I-” 

    Alex sighed and the weight of the world seemed to settle over her shoulders. 

    “I was so afraid of what would happen to her. I didn’t want to lose my baby sister to the world. I was told it was my job to protect her, to keep her safe. And there she was exposing herself to millions of people to save me. It felt like I failed.”

    Alex fell silent and Lena contemplated her own whiskey.

    “I felt like I failed when Lex turned the sun red,” she confessed.

    Alex looked at her, waiting. Lena took a deep breath.

    “Lex didn’t listen to a lot of people. I was one of three whose opinion he would actually listen to. He was my big brother and he considered me to be almost as smart as him. He had after all taught me everything I know, or so he would tell me. Lex would let me look at his work, he wold let me make changes or corrections when he would second guess others. I was… someone he trusted.”

    Lena carefully put down her glass.

    “I was the only person who could make bald jokes and he’d laugh. I was the only person who could tell him he was an idiot. I used to…”

    Lena felt tears in he eyes.

    “I loved my brother so much. All I wanted to do was work with him to save the world.”

    She drained her glass.

    “It just turned out he had a very different idea of what saving the world meant.”

    Lena gently pushed the now empty glass away. Alex’s hand tightened on hers. Lena looked over and saw Alex giving her a look, one that she couldn’t quite decipher.

    “My father worked for Cadmus.”

    Lena opened her mouth to say something but she had no words. 

    “He lied to us, Kara and me. He manipulated us and… Even though I know he loved me and Kara he still chose to work with the people who we fought against every day. But he was still my dad, and I loved him.”

    Alex took a deep breath.

    “I nearly got shot into the far reaches of space on a ship filled with aliens your mother had rounded up as a result.”

    Lena choked.

    “She what?”

    “Yeah, Kara saved me. I found out I couldn’t rely on my father but I could rely on my sister.”

    Lena gave Alex a weak smile.

    “I’ve never had family like that,” she said quietly. Tears filled her eyes. “I wish I had a sister.”

    Alex put an arm around her shoulders and pulled her close.

    “You’ve got one in me if you want.”

    Lena felt the tears escape as she hugged Alex.

    “And I have a feeling Sam would find a way to march in here and kick your ass if she ever heard you suggest she wasn’t your sister,” Alex added.

    Lena found herself laughing.

    “I’m not sure Sam considers me more than a friend and colleague,” she said.

    Alex made a noise of disagreement but let her go with a final comforting squeeze. Lena poured them both another finger of scotch. 

    Her phone rang a moment later. Sam.

    She answered.

    “Hey what-”

    “What the fuck is this about you saying I’m not your sister?” Sam demanded.

    Lena looked around wildly for R2, but the droid hadn’t magically managed to remove itself from the wall to record them.

    “What-”

    “And don’t even think of denying it Lena. I have it on good authority that-”

    Lena looked at Alex who gave her an apologetic look.

    “Sorry, Kara always automatically listens in whenever I say kick your ass. Something about dragging me out of bar fights before they escalate.”

    Lena found herself smiling as she put her phone back to her ear to discover Andrea had now joined Sam in the yelling about her not acknowledging their status as her sisters. 

    “I taught you how to dress!” Andrea yelled. “And gave you your first fake ID.”

    “We only started talking again this year,” Lena said, smiling despite her words.

    Andrea scoffed.

    “I haven’t spoken to my half brother in a decade, you can’t use that to get out of this,” Andrea retorted.

    Lena’s smile seemed to be growing with the warm feeling in her heart. 

    “I suppose you have a point,” she conceded.

    “I had you give my daughter sex ed!” Sam yelled. “And I looked after you when you had the flu last year. Are you seriously saying I’m not your sister after I cleaned the snot off your face and spoon fed you soup during that conference call?”

    Lena felt her smile growing further as the reprimands continued. 

    “How can you both be my sisters if you’re dating,” she teased.

    “Ever heard of a sister in law,” Andrea said immediately. 

    “You’re not married,” Lena pointed out, laughing.

    “Semantics,” Andrea said. 

    Alex bumped her shoulder gently to get her attention. Lena looked over and Alex smiled at her.

    “I think you’re going to have to accept you have sisters,” she said. 

    Lena had to agree as she listened to the now growing argument on the other end of the phone over which one of them was the in law. Lena picked up her scotch with a smile. Alex held out her glass and they clinked them together in a silent toast as Andrea started yelling about how she could've made Lena an aunt too, she was just busy being a badass.

    She had sisters. 

    Chapter End Notes

    June 13 DAY 336

    Chapter Notes

    Kara arrived with the space backpack and a smile. She gave Lena a kiss and hugged Alex tightly. 

    “How does everyone feel about poker?” Kara asked.

    “Absolutely not,” Alex said immediately. “You always cheat.”

    Kara’s face fell into a pout.

    “No I don’t,” she insisted.

    Alex rolled her eyes.

    “Kara, you and I paid off half my student loans after one weekend in Vegas. I know exactly how good you are. We are not playing poker.”

    Lena looked at Kara intrigued.

    “Well now I’m curious,” she said.

    Alex sighed in resignation leading them to the dining table. 

    Kara smiled and a few moments later Lena stared in amazement as Kara took a fully shuffled deck and had it in number and suit order in the time it took her to deal out the cards.

    “I can see how you made so much in so short a time,” Lena said.

    “Kara can both count cards, see through them, and hear everything going on in the security room. We walked out right before security came to check us six times. I think we were almost put on some kind of watch list,” Alex said.

    Lena smiled at Kara.

    “They’d have never caught you,” she teased.

    “They haven’t yet,” Kara said.

    Alex glared at her.

    “Kara, please tell me you haven’t been playing for charity again.”

    Kara sighed.

    “Not since the last media conference." Alex's eyes narrowed.  Kara held up her hands. "Blame Lois. We went to this underground poker game and I made six figures. Lois then did a giant expose on it after they tried to rob us that night in our hotel rooms.”

    Alex gave Kara an exasperated look.

    “Then Cat found out and told me next time I was working for her.”

    Lena started laughing and Alex gave in. She smiled at her sister then looked at Lena.

    “She’s also banned from fifty two pickup.”

    Alex tossed the cards in the air. They landed on the table in a perfect line that was again in exact suit and number order.

    “Okay, no poker,” Lena said.

    Kara winked at her. 

    “Go fish?” Kara suggested.

    “Jenga,” Alex said firmly.

    There was a meow at Kara’s feet and Ripley jumped onto the table. They looked at each other.

    “Uno?” Lena suggested.

    The Danvers sisters sighed and Lena got up to get the cards from her bedroom.

    She came back to see the sisters glaring at each other over the table.

    “You can’t murder Darcy,” Kara said firmly.

    “You don’t understand how loud he is,” Alex said.

    “I can hear him on earth,” Kara said, with a smirk.

    Alex threw up her hands.

    “So let us-”

    “Sam said he’s important for science,” Kara said firmly. 

    “How important could one goat be?” Alex demanded.

    Lena sighed as she sat down.

    “We need proof that a man won’t lose all erectile function being here,” she said.

    “Seriously?” Alex demanded.

    Lena nodded and began shuffling the Uno cards. Ripley jumped up on a chair looking at them all.

    “A few of the engineers were worried so we agreed to add a male goat. I think after all the trouble he’s caused it has been proven but apparently we need a full year for certification, or at least that’s what the engineers were saying.”

    “That sounds like bullshit,” Alex said.

    Lena shrugged and began dealing out the cards.

    “I’m not arguing it isn’t,” she said. “But my lead engineer was very concerned about the possible impacts of the artificial gravity on men’s long term reproductive capacity, and the board agreed with him.”

    “They’re all men too aren’t they?” Alex muttered, carefully examining her cards.

    Lena sighed.

    “Eighty nine per cent.” She turned over a card to start the game. “You can’t get or maintain an erection in zero gravity, but that’s different and so I have spent ten months being screamed at by Darcy to prove his dick still works.”

    Alex put down a card, grumbling under her breath.

    “Why not just send all women then?” Kara asked.

    Lena smiled at Kara as she put down a card.

    “That was my plan, but that was apparently discrimination.”

    Alex made a disgusted noise as Kara put down a draw two.

    “You added one man didn’t you?” Kara said smiling at Lena.

    Lena smiled a little wider.

    “Among others…”

    Alex and Kara looked at her waiting. Lena pretended not to notice as she rearranged her cards, casually playing a six on her turn. She looked up at the sisters and smirked.

    “I added a black man, which apparently wasn’t good enough. They needed a more diverse sample of people with penises to be sure. So I conceded the point, and added a trans woman, who just happened to be the most qualified programmer I have. The board started screaming about diversity gone mad, and so I said I could always add another man. They demanded it. They insisted there be two men minimum on this station. So I added Seb.”

    “Trans man?” Alex guessed.

    Lena nodded, and Alex snickered. 

    “Then they gave up on the pretence and said it wouldn’t proceed unless there was one white cisgender man on the team. So I found Quinn. Who’s incredibly qualified for the job, and coincidentally flamboyantly gay.”

    Alex was looking at Lena with the deepest respect.

    “Did they stop there?” Kara asked.

    “Yes,” Lena said, putting down a draw four. “Yellow. But only because I said any more people would add another sixty million to the cost. In just under two months the most gender diverse space crew in history, at least that we know of, will be taking a rocket up to this station. My PR team is very excited about it. My board still hasn’t forgiven me.”

    Alex put down a card.

    “I can’t believe you managed to do that,” she muttered. “Every time I try to so much as put in place a diversity policy I have to deal with a mess of paperwork an inch thick about why I want this new policy, and then I get fifteen complaints about hiring based on merit.”

    Lena gave her a sympathetic look.

    “If it helps I didn’t do it as an diversity policy, I did it as an anti corruption hiring program.”

    Alex looked at her curiously.

    “Reverse,” Kara said.

    Lena stayed silent as she put down a card. Alex did so as well. Alex caved after another round.

    “Come on,” she said. “Share your corporate wisdom.”

    Lena smiled at her.

    “I’m sure you’re familiar with blind auditions for the orchestra.” Alex nodded. “Well I applied that to the hiring process. With a few additional changes. For example all our applicants are first a number, no name, no personalised details in their resumes. Then they have to pass tests of competency, if there is one for the role. They get ranked, then there’s the reference checks, then standardised interviews. Then preference is given to diversity. So I only needed one diversity policy, officially anyway. That being if there is two equally qualified candidates we hire the one from a minority background. All the rest however I put in place under a set of policies to stamp out possible nepotism and corruption in the hiring process.”

    Alex was starring very intently at Lena. 

    “Can I-”

    “I’ll have Jess send you a copy. Also Uno.”

    “What!” Kara and Alex yelled.

    Lena smiled back.

    "Kara's not the only one who knows a few card tricks. I've also been to Vegas."

    Chapter End Notes

    Lena being the sneakiest bitch in space managing to do sleight of hand on a Kryptonian and a DEO agent. Darcy's contribution to science really does make him just a prick. I hope y'all enjoyed this moment of the three of them hanging out and playing games.

    June 20 DAY 343

    Chapter Notes

    Alex gave Lena a tight hug.

    “I’ll call you later,” Alex promised.

    Lena smiled and nodded. Alex narrowed her eyes.

    “I mean it,” she said. “We’re doing a joint watching of the silence of the lambs tonight.”

    Lena laughed and hugged her again. 

    “Yeah, we are,” she said.

    Alex gave her a final look that Lena couldn’t quite decipher before putting on the helmet. Kara gave Lena a kiss and promised to message once they were back. Lena nodded and a minute later they disappeared Kara taking Alex in some kind of pod she pushed ahead of her.

    Lena found herself drifting through the station, well as best she could drift with her ankle in a large boot that clunked and slowed her normal walk to more of a drag. She had a feeling there was a lot of people making pirate memes about her current walk. Then again they couldn’t see her anymore, unless she did the log or walked past R2.

    Perhaps she should set a camera by the fish pond for people to watch Ripley… then again they had seen her at the fish pond as well and that was still apparently earning her quite a lot of fans.

    Lena made her way over to R2, her hand running down the perfect welded lines and then stopping over the indent of Kara’s fingerprint. She had felt quite overwhelmed with the others on the station but now it felt… lonely in a way it hadn’t before. 

    She smiled at R2, aware that somewhere dozens of people were probably watching her. She wasn’t sure if that made her feel better or not. Her phone chimed and she saw a message from Kara.

    Safely back on earth, Alex says she has motion sickness and needs to go sleep to recover. I think she just wanted to continue Ripley’s nap schedule.

    Lena smiled and, as if summoned, Ripley came to meow at her. Lena followed Ripley to her bedroom where she lay down at the end of her bed staring intently at Lena. 

    Lena smiled and lay down on her bed. Ripley flopped to the side so she was pressed against Lena’s leg and started purring. Lena felt the loneliness ebb away at the familiar sound and feeling. She wasn’t sure what she was going to do when she finally went home and no longer had the time to take thirty minutes in her afternoon for her cat imposed lie down. 

    Her days as a CEO were rather hectic, somewhat by design. She needed to keep an eye on everything because the world was always waiting for her to make a mistake. Taking an afternoon nap or reading for a half hour would not be permitted, even if she did schedule the time. Something else was always more important like…

    Lena let her mind wander remembering all the cancelled lunch dates, late afternoons fixing mistakes, and just constantly having to manage everyone. It had been exhausting but invigorating, she did enjoy the challenge… didn’t she?

    Yes, she did she loved the challenge of fixing bugs, and reworking plans, and staying ahead of everyone else and… and making the world a better place.

    Lena picked up her phone and sent Sam a message.

    Do you like being CEO?

    It was twenty minutes before she got a response.

    Overall yeah. I like being the boss. But don’t worry you can have your office back when you get home.

    Lena lay in her bed thinking. 

    When she got back she would be CEO of L Corp again. Granted one whose naked body had apparently been front page news for a few days, but the job was still hers. The board couldn’t get rid of her that easily, her mother and her had the controlling interest, granted if her mother decided she didn’t like her in the role there could be problems but Lena was quite sure at his point that Lillian was of the opinion that only a Luthor could control the family company. 

    Then again, Lena had slept with a Kryptonian and her mother’s attitude to homosexuality and aliens was very well known to her.

    Not that she’d said anything about it. 

    Granted Lena hadn’t heard from her mother since she left the planet. She had briefly wondered if her mother had been part of Lex’s attack but Alex had investigated personally and believed Lillian when she said she was outraged by it. Probably because her outrage had seemed to be more about the possible affect of the station’s destruction on the company stock price rather than her daughter’s health and safety. 

    Lena let her mind go over the options, should she go back to being CEO. Sam was doing wonderfully and the number of threats to her employees since her stepping away had significantly improved. So long as she wasn’t involved in those statistics.

    Sam did consult with her fairly regularly about what R&D were up to, though that had lessened a bit as Sam got more familiar with what exactly they were up to. Sam did say Lena was needed to steer the R&D…

    Maybe she could take over that department, step back from being the CEO and instead just do what she’d always been supposed to do and run the R&D. Granted that had been the expectation with Lex as the CEO. A Luthor was always supposed to be in charge should one be available. 

    Or was it better for her to step away completely, leave L Corp to it’s people and let them determine the course. Should she leave to do something new. Something all her own…

    She was still considering her options when her phone rang. She answered automatically.

    “You have five minutes to get yourself some popcorn and set up that projector because we’re starting this before Kara gets back from that earthquake and pouts until we watch Lassie or something,” Alex said.

    Lena grinned and jumped up. 

    “I’ll be ready,” she promised.

     

    They had barely started the movie when they had to pause because Sam was calling Lena. Then hearing what she was up to Andrea wanted in. Ten minutes of back and forth by them all and they were all starting again, Lena longing in the dining room with her laptop open on the able in front of her.

    The screen was split in three with Alex on her couch eating take away noodles, Kelly occasionally visible beside her as she went over some papers. Below them, Andrea, Sam, and Ruby, were eating burritos on Andrea’s couch. On the right was the news, which had been covering Kara rescuing people from the earthquake, but was now giving the stock report. In front of her Lena had her tablet showing the livestream chat. Apparently R2 was close enough that they had been able to pick up on the sound of the movie playing and people had already found the exact timestamp to join in.

    Lena smiled as she gently stroked Ripley’s head, her eyes regularly drifting down to the two screens in front of her. Listening as Alex explained to Ruby why this movie had such an impact on cinematic history. Sam was apparently distracted looking down Andrea’s shirt and Andrea was pretending not to notice as she subtly made that area more prominent. 

    Lena looked away from her laptop to see a rather informative debate in the chat about how the camera angles in certain shots were used to convey meaning. 

    The proximity alert for Kara beeped and she felt grateful she’d shifted it to her phone so the chat didn’t hear. 

    A moment later a pair of strong arms wrapped around her shoulders and Kara kissed her neck.

    “Hello, I thought you were busy,” Lena murmured.

    “It’s all cleanup now,” Kara whispered. “I wanted to make sure you weren’t lonely.”

    Lena indicated the screen in front of her then Ripley in her lap.

    “No, everyone’s been very good at keeping me company.”

    Kara smiled and sat beside her. 

    “Do you mind if I join you? I won’t watch the movie but I would like to meditate next to you if that’s okay,” she said.

    Lena smiled and indicated the seat next to her with a smile.

    “You’re always welcome darling,” she said.

    Kara smiled and sat, carefully arranging herself then closing her eyes. Ripley abandoned Lena’s lap for Kara’s. Lena turned back to the movie. She wondered if this would be what it was like when she was back on earth. Or would everyone drift away again?

    Kara’s hand slipped into hers and Lena felt her heart warm with a little spark of hope. Hope that she could have this, that it wouldn’t all disappear the moment she was back on Earth and no longer in danger. 

    Chapter End Notes

    They all worked together to make sure Lena wouldn't feel lonely again, and as a bonus Alex is teaching Ruby about film history while Rojarias flirt.

    June 27 DAY 350

    Chapter Notes

    “Hey, what are you looking at?” Kara asked.

    Lena turned away from her tablet to smile at Kara. 

    “Nothing important,” she said, casually tossing it onto the desk. 

    Kara raised her eyebrow, a perfect imitation.

    “That wouldn’t happen to be the board’s offer would it?” Kara asked.

    Lena’s smile fell.

    “Can’t hide anything from Supergirl,” she muttered.

    Kara smiled and kissed her.

    “Nope, not when you’re been sitting here for a half hour thinking about it?”

    Lena sighed and pulled her in for a second kiss.

    “Been spying on me have you?” she teased.

    Kara shook her head, her smile growing. 

    “No, I just saw Ripley hadn’t had her dinner yet and wondered why.”

    Lena groaned and seeing the time jumped up only to be stopped by Kara’s hand on her arm.

    “I fed her when I arrived.”

    Lena sighed and Kara wrapped her arms around her from behind. Lena let herself lean backwards into her. 

    “I just…”

    She didn’t know what. Some part of her felt reluctant to sign the contract. It was more generous that her last one, far fewer “fuck you” clauses as Jess liked to say.

    It was a good offer, and really she should take it. That was the plan, a year in space and then she would come back to retake her position, hopefully with less death threats. 

    As far as plans went this one had gone remarkably well. Her brother was under even tighter security than before, her mother was apparently unwilling to kill her, even after she found out she was in a relationship with a Kryptonian; which Lena had been sure would drive her mother over the edge. It had all gone so much better than she could ever have imagined, so why was she hesitating.

    Kara held her a little tighter pulling Lena back into the moment. 

    “I don’t know if I want to be CEO again,” she confessed. “This year has been… utterly unpredictable and beyond my wildest dreams.” Lena tilted her head up to kiss Kara’s neck. “I thought it would be harder for me to give up L Corp, to give up control. But in the end it hasn’t been. I haven’t wanted to go back. At no point have I felt like I needed to take back control. Sam is doing a great job and yes, she consults me about the R&D stuff but that’s all.”

    Lena looked over at her tablet again.

    “What do you want to do?” Kara asked.

    Lena smirked at her.

    “You.”

    Kara rolled her eyes but she still swept Lena into her arms and carried her to bed. 

     

    Lena woke late that night and looked around. She saw Kara sitting in a meditative state in the middle of her room. Lena listened carefully and heard Kara was going through some kind of chant. She decided it was best to leave her to it and picked up her tablet again. 

    She opened it to the offer again and felt a gnawing feeling in her stomach. She dismissed it and went to the other reports she had. She could make the decision another day. Halfway through a report on upcoming projects Kara slid back into the bed.

    “Hey,” Kara murmured. “What are you doing up?”

    “Just going over a few projects,” Lena said, tilting the screen so Kara could see. 

    “Yes, but why are you doing that at three am?” Kara asked gently.

    Lena shrugged a shoulder.

    “I couldn’t go back to sleep.”

    Kara fixed her with a stare. Lena tried not to squirm.

    “Lena, talk to me,” Kara said, her arms wrapping around Lena pulling her back into a warm and comforting embrace. 

    Lena let Kara guide her under the covers again, their bodies wrapped around each other in the dark. Ripley rose and flopped down again in a slightly different position. Lena suspected this was her version of a complaint at having her sleep disturbed. Kara nudged her gently to pull her attention back to their conversation.

    “I liked being CEO,” Lena said.

    “What about it did you like?” Kara asked.

    Lena shrugged a shoulder.

    “The challenge. I liked being in charge.” Kara scoffed. Lena hit her, even as she smiled. “Not like that.”

    Kara giggled pulling Lena closer.

    “Okay, so what didn’t you like?” Kara asked.

    Lena sighed heavily.

    “All the meetings, the investors, the numbers. I liked working to outsmart everyone but it did get tiring at times. I didn’t like having to do press conferences and go in every weekend the moment something went wrong, because something was always going wrong. I didn't like being the face of the company.”

    Lena fell silent. Her fingers traced random lines over Kara’s shoulder.

    “I miss the lab,” she said. “I miss fixing things.”

    Kara kissed her head.

    “Well, could you maybe take over the R&D department then?” Kara asked.

    Lena sighed and shook her head.

    “Quinn still has another year on his contract. Besides I haven’t been working with others for a while. I don’t know how I’ll go in a position like that.”

    Kara started playing with Lena’s hair. 

    “Why not just take over as project lead on a few things for the next year, see if you can work in a less... bossy position and then when the contract finishes you take over.”

    Lena opened her mouth then closed it. 

    “A lot can change in a year,” she said.

    Kara hummed her agreement and then a gentle hand was tilting Lena’s face up. Kara kissed her softly, slowly, and with utmost tenderness.

    “Take over a small project, work your way back into the normal world. See where it takes you,” Kara said gently.

    Lena sighed.

    “Are you seriously suggesting I not plan everything out and instead just see what happens?” she asked.

    Kara smiled and nodded.

    “If this year has taught you anything Lena, it should be that you can’t plan for everything.”

    Lena sighed.

    “A goat becoming my arch nemesis was a surprise,” she admitted.

    Kara laughed. 

    Lena sighed and rested her head on Kara. Her mind going through all the possibilities and settling on none.

    Her phone beeped and with a sigh Lena reached for it. Andrea.

    Lena groaned and opened it. She fully expected it to be Andrea losing her mind over her feelings for Sam again. It was not. She frowned and sat up.

    What would you say if I told you I was working on VR tech that could allow you to enter an augmented reality?

    “What?” Kara asked, on the verge of pouting at losing Lena's attention.

    Lena didn’t answer, already excitedly typing her response. Kara read over her shoulder and sighed.

    “Humans will use it for porn,” she said.

    Lena paused in her typing and looked at Kara. Kara stared back. Lena felt like she should argue. 

    “Do I need to bring up what happened with the live feed?” Kara asked.

    Lena conceded with a sigh. 

    Chapter End Notes

    Poor Lena, still struggling to decide what to do in the future. Luckily she has Kara to distract her, and Ripley to comfort her. Also, how long do you think before Andrea is trying to work out how much money they could make using Obsidian Platinum for porn? And do you think Sam would talk her out of it?
    Until next time, stay safe y'all.

    July 2 DAY 355

    Chapter Notes

    Kara kissed her way up Lena’s neck.

    “You’re being distracting,” Lena murmured.

    “You’ve been working for hours,” Kara whined.

    Lena laughed. 

    “Well forgive me for wanting to properly plan the station upgrades for the health and comfort of my incoming crew,” Lena said.

    Kara huffed and peered at the plans Lena had drawn on a copy of the station blueprints. Her eyes easily taking in all the neatly marked changes and their carefully written out notes. There was a lot of upgrades.

    “That will take ages for them to do,” Kara said.

    Lena laughed softly.

    “Yes, I have a feeling they’ll need something to keep them busy once they get over the novelty of being here.”

    “And you think carefully covering a wall with a thousand sound absorbing bits of foam then covering it with a wire mesh will achieve that do you?” Kara asked, tapping one particular section and bringing up the three pages of notes Lena had made.

    “It’ll be like a wall sized puzzle,” Lena said, grinning at Kara.

    Kara flicked through the notes to look at the various wall designs Lena had made that would best absorb the sounds of Darcy screaming. 

    “And why are they not just installing panels, which would take far less time?” Kara asked.

    Lena sighed.

    “Because if we do them like this it will weigh less and be easier to fit into the rocket.”

    Kara pouted.

    “I can carry-”

    Lena cut Kara off by gently tapping her with the stylus.

    “No, you have to let us actually use our technology so we can develop improvements and progress on our own.”

    Kara sat back with a sigh, if one could call it sitting when she was technically flying.

    “Fine, but if it goes wrong then I’m bringing up panels and installing them,” Kara said firmly.

    Lena smiled at her and nodded.

    “How much longer will it take you?” Kara whined.

    Lena smiled at her impatience, though she was trying not to.

    “I would have been done by now if you hadn’t been distracting me,” Lena said, turning to look at the pouting Kara over her shoulder. 

    Kara mimed zipping her lips. Lena shook her head and turned back to her plans with a smile. She saved the changes and sent them to Sam. 

    Her work done she turned to Kara.

    “Okay, now I’m done,” she said. 

    Kara’s face brightened and she swept Lena into a kiss.

    Lena tried to focus on kissing Kara back instead of smiling at her eagerness.

    “I’m so excited to have you home soon. I’ve already prepared the penthouse for when you return,” Kara said, pulling back a little. 

    Lena laughed.

    “So you restocked the bar?” she teased.

    Kara rolled her eyes.

    “No, you already have a staff to do that.”

    “Very true,” Lena agreed.

    Kara kissed her again.

    “I got you plants,” she declared.

    “Plants?” Lena asked.

    Kara nodded.

    “You have spent the last year in a space station surrounded by plants and animals. So I had a chat with Jess, who knows a surprising amount about plants.”

    Lena kissed Kara. She couldn’t help it, Kara just looked so kissable when she was excited.

    “Jess’ parents are horticulturalists,” Lena said.

    “Oh,” Kara frowned for a moment, apparently thinking hard about something. “That explains a lot actually.”

    Lena laughed and kissed Kara again.

    “Well she ordered a whole bunch of plants and then I brought them in and we put them all over your apartment. Your staff have already said they’re happy to add watering them to their, and this is a quote from your housekeeper, ridiculously light duties.”

    Lena scoffed.

    “Light? I have her do a white glove test on every surface.”

    Kara sighed.

    “You know, sometimes I forget you didn’t learn how to clean anything until you went to college.”

    Lena frowned at her. Kara kissed it away.

    “Lena, if you don’t go into the majority of your rooms, rarely cook, and barely do more than sleep in an apartment it’s really not that hard to keep it spotless. Also there was only one of you. According to your housekeeper my moving in resulted in the longest work day they ever had, which was three hours. Two of which was spent organising the closet.”

    Lena fought the urge to pout.

    “Well…” She tried to think of a retort. “Ripley will probably make it harder for them.”

    Kara scoffed.

    “Ripley isn’t going to make enough work for three people all by herself.”

    Lena wrapped her arms around Kara.

    “Well then there’s also you, and soon there will be me. So I think combined we can make enough of a mess to justify my housekeeping staff.”

    Kara pulled her closer. 

    “Is that your plan? Using Ripley and me to justify your excessive number of employees.”

    Lena nodded.

    “I have many plans. A few of them could even be called villainous.”

    Kara’s smile grew.

    “Villainous?” she asked.

    Lena nodded and pulled Kara in for a slow kiss.

    “I intend to make Supergirl beg,” she whispered.

    A shiver went through Kara and a moment later they were flying through the station to Lena’s bed.

    Chapter End Notes

    We're getting close to the one year mark and with it Lena's return to earth. Kara is certainly excited to have her girlfriend coming home. So excited that she's already filled Lena's house with plants in preparation. What else do you think Kara will "prepare" for Lena's return?
    Let me know in the comments down below and I'll see y'all next time.

    July 13 DAY 365

    Chapter Notes

    Lena smiled at R2. Ripley cradled in her arms. 

    “I thought it might be symbolic to make the last log post with R2,” she said. “The first proper crew is due to arrive soon and I’m going to be leaving not long after they arrive. And yes, I’m taking Ripley with me.”

    She gave a dozing Ripley a little scratch behind her ears.

    “I’m not leaving her behind. She’s mine and I love her.”

    A paw twitched. Lena had given Ripley a light sedative with her breakfast to make their leaving easier. Ripley did not like being confined and Lena didn’t want to stress her out when she had to go into a container to be safely transported to earth. She had no intention of providing viewers a final show of her chasing her cat all over the station.

    “In the interest of environmentalism I will be getting a ride back from my favourite Kryptonian. She said she’s got a ride, so I guess J’onn loaned her his car again.”

    Lena looked down at Ripley again then back at R2.

    “This year has been… very different from what I expected. The plan was basically to humanise me and show me more as a science nerd than a Luthor and well… According to my PR people my reputation is now badass cat lady who fucks Supergirl. Which is a definite improvement over Lex but a girl.”

    She took a deep breath.

    “I could never have imagined going to space would have changed my life this much. Or that I would reveal so much about myself as a result.”

    She laughed softly.

    “Physically and emotionally. But… I’m so glad I did this. Despite the numerous times I nearly died. Though if I was making a do over I would have made sure R2 wasn’t constantly following me, and we wouldn’t have selected Darcy as the male goat.”

    Darcy’s distant scream registered to her ears and she twitched. She was leaving the ear plugs behind for the next crew. 

    “I guess he did survive the year, lucky bastard…”

    As Darcy screamed again Lena briefly contemplated correcting that, but after a year she could handle a few more minutes.

    “I know a lot of you have been wondering if I’ll be going back to the CEO position at L Corp and I can say now, no I won’t. Sam is doing an amazing job and while I will be keeping a position on the board I have decided to give myself a little time to re acclimatise to being back around people before I make any concrete decisions about my career. I have a few L Corp projects I will be consulting on and a few personal projects I intend to work on in the mean time.”

    Her phone beeped, telling her the replacement crew was almost there.

    “Thank you all for following along. I hope you treat the incoming crew with just as much love as you have shown me, though I do ask that you stop the horny commentary. I doubt any of them will be stripping down because of the AC or having a very public argument with their girlfriend, and then a just as public… reunion.”

    Lena heard the second alert, this one telling her docking procedures would need to start in a moment. She struggled for a moment to think of something to say. Then another alert told her she really had to get moving. She ran to the airlock.

    Everything was waiting for her to initiate the docking. She carefully shifted the now properly unconscious Ripley to one arm. She picked up the tablet and, after a moment’s hesitation over whether to put Ripley down, she started the docking procedure, balancing the tablet on Ripley. 

    Five minutes later the airlock opened and the fifteen person crew came through with bright smiles and offering handshakes. Lena greeted them all and they in turn greeted Ripley then her.

    They decided that their first order of business should be to wave to the livestream and she had no objections. It wasn't long before Lena was trying not to laugh as one by one the crew went past R2 doing some kind of over the top pose in front of the camera as they introduced themselves by name, pronouns, and position. All ending with signing in. Lena could only assume was some kind of reference, or multiple references given the specificity of their poses. She would ask Kara about it at some point.

    Lena had a feeling the viewers were loving it.

    She looked out the window and saw Kara arriving in J’onn’s car. 

    Kara came in through the other airlock slipping in at the end of the crew introductions in full Supergirl regalia to take her classic Supergirl pose.

    “Kara Zor-El Danvers, she her, Supergirl!”

    The crew laughed and Lena couldn’t help her smile as she looked at Kara shaking hands with the team she’d picked out. Then Kara was smiling at Lena. She sighed and seeing the enthusiastic encouragement of the crew walked in front of R2 for the last time. 

    “Strike a pose!” the crew called.

    Lena sighed in feigned exasperation but then, keeping a careful hold of Ripley in her arms, did a Kryptonian salute. 

    “Lena Luthor and Ripley, she her and cat, captain and first mate. Signing out.”

    Chapter End Notes

    I hope y'all enjoyed her final sign off to her fans and the introductions of the new crew. Which anime transformation would you have done for your crew introduction? How long do you think the new crew will last before they're begging to kill Darcy too?
    The last chapter will be an epilogue in ten days. Until next time, stay safe y'all.

    July 23 DAY 10… ON EARTH

    Chapter Notes

    Lena was enjoying a lazy morning in bed scrolling through project offers. She hadn’t quite settled on one yet. She already had a few consulting things lined up, both for L Corp and the DEO but she wanted something else. None of them really seemed to be… challenging. She wanted something unique, something hard, something that she could really lose herself in. 

    She’d adapted to earth and people fairly quickly. Though her first time walking down a National City street again had been such a shock to her system that Kara had raced over worried she was having a heart attack.

    That had only been her second day though so Lena felt it hardly counted. She had managed a whole trip to the supermarket yesterday, granted it was at ten at night and she only went for Kara’s favourite ice cream. But she’d still handled the half dozen people there moderately well. 

    Kara had pointed out that it wasn’t like Lena had been around people a lot before she went to space. What with the assassinations and all that. So overall Lena felt she had readjusted quite well. 

    Ripley of course had no trouble once the sedative had worn off, and even before then if Lena was honest. The moment Kara had taken her out of the carrier Ripley had been exploring the penthouse on wobbly legs, happily investigating the small forest worth of plants that now filled every room. 

    Lena had taken one look at number of plants, then a sheepish Kara, and decided to spend a few hours making a dozen updated versions of the agricultural droids on the station to take care of the plants. 

    Ripley, in response, had decided that just like the droids on the station they all needed to be hunted and eliminated. After the fifth destroyed droid Lena had given them all a speed boost. She gave it a week before Ripley found a way to overcome it.

    Perhaps she could give them armour. Or maybe something to warn the droids that Ripley was nearby and they should move. Perhaps if she put a sensor on Ripley’s collar that would put out a signal the droids could react to. 

    Lena had just pulled a notepad over to start drawing ideas when her phone rang. Jess.

    “Hey Jess,” she said. "How's L Corp today?"

    “Sam needs your help to convince the new crew not to murder Darcy.”

    Lena laughed and Kara’s head appeared around the door. She gave Lena a small wave to let her know she was back. 

    “Why would I be of any help? I’m firmly on team murder Darcy,” Lena asked.

    She motioned for Kara to join her. 

    “You managed a year, and several breakdowns, without killing him,” Jess said.

    Lena had to concede she was probably the closest there was to an expert in not murdering Darcy. Kara floated over, settling on the bed beside Lena.

    “What happened?” Lena asked.

    She had a feeling her lazy morning was not about to include making out with her favourite Kryptonian until one of them turned into a needy puddle.

    “The current team is insisting, on camera, that they don’t care if their dicks drop off with no warning, they want to kill him,” Jess said.

    Lena met Kara’s eyes and sighed. Kara kissed her cheek and got up. She’d have to postpone their make out session it seemed.

    “I’ll send Kara to rescue him before they remember where the knives are,” Lena said.

    Kara was already in her suit.

    Lena tilted up her chin to get a proper kiss before Kara disappeared in a rush of air. 

    “Thank you,” Jess said.

    “No problem. Oh, did you fix Sam’s schedule?”

    Jess scoffed.

    “Of course. We shall both be free at seven on Thursday to join the rest of you for game night. Did you need me to have anything delivered?”

    Lena hummed thoughtfully.

    “No, but if you could make sure there’s an order for pizza and potstickers-”

    “Already done,” Jess said.

    “Still the best assistant ever,” Lena said.

    Jess laughed.

    “Well you were the best boss.”

    Lena couldn’t help her smile at that.

    “I thought Sam was now the best boss?” Lena teased.

    “Not this week she’s not. Alex decided it wasn’t fair for all the couples to play together so she had Kelly draw us all out of a hat to make the teams. You, me, and Nia are going to crush them.”

    Lena laughed.

    “They have no idea what’s coming for them,” she agreed.

    They hung up and Lena rolled onto her side looking over at Ripley hunting through the ferns. She contemplated what to do for the rest of her day. Checking the time she saw it was barely nine am. Normally she’d still be shovelling goat shit at this hour.

    Sighing she got up and put on some workout clothes. While the station did have it’s own gravity it wasn’t as strong as Earth’s. It had been close enough that Lena hadn’t really noticed the difference at the time but on her return she had become painfully aware she’d lost a lot of her previous strength. 

    Ripley had gone from a comfortable weight to carry in one arm to needing her to use both arms and what felt like the full OHSA lifting procedures just to put her on the bed. Lena spent two days researching exercises and consulting with a few doctors to develop a plan to rebuild her strength. A plan that involved three hours a day of strength exercises. 

    The doctors had assured Lena she didn’t need to do that much and that her body would return naturally in it’s own time with regular exercise, but Lena had a feeling she could halve the predicted recovery time. 

    She was halfway through her first set of exercises when she got a text from Clark Kent.

    He’s safely in his new home.

    Attached was a picture of Darcy with a half dozen other goats. Kara stood behind them holding a wooden sign in which she’d burnt the words asshole enclosure with her heat vision. In quite lovely calligraphy too.

    After quitting Catco Kara had decided to try a different type of writing. She'd already written several children’s books about growing up on Krypton and was now working on illustrations to go with them. She’d taken over Lena’s library, with Lena’s blessing, and turned it into her studio. 

    The last time Lena had gone in there Kara had been struggling to decide between making the pictures realistic to actually show what Krypton had looked like, and going with a more stylised visual that would appeal to children. 

    Lena however was looking for a big project. Not that she really needed to. She could spend her days lounging around her penthouse doing pilates and watching Kara paint forever if she really wanted to. Besides she hadn’t been all that busy on the station there was no reason she had to jump into work now she was back on earth. She just wanted something... different. Something new. Something she would be challenged by. Something she didn't know if she could do...

    The doorbell rang and Lena jumped up immediately to go answer it. 

    It was J’onn and he had a folder. Lena was intrigued.

    “Hello J’onn, how can I help you?”

    J’onn smiled as he entered the penthouse. 

    “I think I may have an interesting challenge for you,” he said, indicating the folder he had.

    Lena motioned for him to join her in the kitchen where they both sat at a small table flanked by large ferns. 

    “What do you know about flying space ships?” J’onn asked.

    “I’ve never flown one,” Lena said. “But I would like to.”

    J’onn slid the folder across the table towards her. Lena’s eyes widened as she looked at the pictures of the clearly alien craft sitting in some kind of warehouse.

    “Let me rephrase, do you think you could fix and then fly one?” J’onn asked. 

    Lena frowned as she examined the space craft in the pictures more closely. It had clearly been damaged, likely on landing. Lena put a few of the photos side by side, looking more critically at the damage. It was hard to tell from the photos what was just cosmetic damage and what might actually be irreparable. 

    “Do you know where it’s from?” she asked.

    J’onn shook his head. 

    “Kara had no idea, Brainy said he had a few theories which I think means he doesn’t know. Considering a few of the possibilities were pop culture references I’m 99% sure he has no idea. And I’ve never seen it’s like.”

    Lena looked from the pictures to J’onn.

    “So you have a damaged spaceship of unknown origin, and you want me to repair and fly it?” she asked.

    "We would likely need to fly it together for safety, but in essence, yes," J'onn said. “Kara mentioned you might be interested in an intellectual challenge, and I confess I could use the help. It’s been a personal project of mine for quite some time and I haven’t made progress on it in a few decades. A fresh set of eyes and hands from someone like you is just what this project needs.”

    J’onn smiled at her and Lena felt herself smiling back. She had no doubt Kara had noticed her recent boredom and asked J'onn if he had any suggestions of a project she could do. That was just something Kara would do. That J'onn would be willing to not only find one so suited to her interests, but also be willing to spend what would likely be thousands of hours over what could be years working with her on it... Lena had never anticipated an alien would be willing to do that.

    Lena looked back at the photos before she got too emotional.

    “How long exactly have you been working on it?” she asked.

    J’onn sighed.

    “Over a hundred years.”

    Lena felt her face split into a wide grin. 

    “So it’s near impossible?” she asked, her excitement growing.

    J’onn shrugged. Lena looked back at the photos. Her hands itched to go touch it, to pull it apart and find out what about it was so mysterious. To see if she even could fix it.

    “Can I see it?” she asked.

    J’onn smiled at her.

    “We can go now if you want,” he offered.

     

    Kara came home and saw the mess that was now Lena’s office. When she’d left the place had been pristine. It was now covered in photos and schematics, all of them covered in Lena’s notations. She’d even wheeled in a whiteboard and covered it in a series of equations. 

    “What’s all this?” Kara asked, frowning at the pictures.

    She recognised that ship. J’onn had it stored in a warehouse on the edge of the industrial district gathering dust. He'd shown it to her once to see if she knew where it came from, she had not.

    “J’onn and I are rebuilding a space ship!” Lena said excitedly.

    Kara looked at Lena and couldn’t help her smile in response. Lena’s hair was a mess, her hands stained with ink, and her eyes bright with excitement over this new challenge.

    “We already discovered the engine is actually three engines and it completely defies both human and martian physics,” Lena said. 

    Kara laughed.

    “How does it do that?” she asked.

    “It sends force into the ship instead of out to move it around,” Lena said, pointing to one diagram that seemed to be the most heavily annotated.

    Kara frowned.

    “What? That makes absolutely no sense.”

    “I know!” Lena said, jumping up to wrap her arms around Kara. Kara quickly steadied her, well aware of Lena’s lowered athletic capabilities. “We have absolutely no idea how it works.”

    Kara found herself laughing.

    “So J’onn has you helping him with a ship that is not only damaged but you have no idea how it’s supposed to work.”

    Lena nodded and enthusiastically kissed Kara.

    “I’m having so much fun!” Lena said, pulling back a little breathless.

    Kara’s smile grew.

    “Are you going to go play with the spaceship again tomorrow?” she asked.

    Lena sighed sadly, the edge of a pout appearing.

    “No, I promised Alex I’d help Brainy with the force field upgrades tomorrow. But J’onn said we could go back to it on Thursday and I can take a piece of it home to play with if we could get it out before game night. He also promised to take me for ice cream again.”

    Kara’s jaw dropped.

    “What? He took you for ice cream? He never gets me ice cream!”

    Lena laughed and kissed her again. 

    “Probably because you can eat a hundred gallons of ice cream and still want more,” she suggested.

    Kara sighed, the beginning of a pout starting. Lena smiled wider and pulled Kara in for another kiss.

    “I promise once it’s ready to fly we can go for a race around the solar system,” Lena said.

    Kara felt her heart warm at the idea. She couldn't help imagining racing Lena to Mars, or maybe around the rings of Saturn.

    “I can’t wait to show it to you,” Kara said. "You'll love it."

    Chapter End Notes

    Well Darcy survived it all in the end, and will live out the rest of his life in Smallville. Lena's actually giving herself time to just be a person and work on different projects with the people who care about her. And now she gets to also spend time with everyone's favourite Space Dad who will get her ice cream after they work on the impossible space ship to possibly unlock new secrets of space travel.
    Kara is still Supergirl but now she gets to be entirely herself and share her story in a way she is comfortable.
    And Ripley is still the real star of the show as she fights the increasingly advanced agricultural droids.
